Across several training sessions with different architectures, we show that, despite the complexity of the problem, the DBN can recover samples that remain critical even under input noise, with a gradual degradation of physical observables relative to the original sample.

</details>


### [147] [On the Impossibility of Unbiased and Length-Invariant Policy Optimization with Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23364)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准结果奖励+GRPO设置下，不存在一种基于长度的加权方案能同时实现梯度无偏性和长度不变性。GRPO近似满足长度不变性但梯度有偏，Dr. GRPO满足梯度无偏性但违背长度不变性。两者处于一个根本性且不可避免的权衡的两端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Dr. GRPO声称其优化器是"无偏的"这一不完全正确的论断，通过理论分析揭示GRPO与Dr. GRPO各自存在的问题，并刻画两者之间的根本权衡。

Method: 建立不可能性定理，证明在结果奖励+GRPO框架下，不存在能同时满足梯度无偏性（P1）和长度不变性（P2）的长度加权方案；通过参数化族f_α(L)=L^{α-1}刻画完整权衡谱，其中α=0对应GRPO，α=1对应Dr. GRPO。

Result: GRPO近似满足P2但违反P1；Dr. GRPO满足P1但违反P2；Dr. GRPO的长度偏置会导致更长轨迹以长度比比例的因子主导梯度更新。

Conclusion: 两种算法并非"做得对"的通用方案，而是处于一个根本且不可避免的权衡的两端，不存在完美的解决方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the dominant reinforcement learning algorithm for training reasoning capabilities in large language models, notably adopted by DeepSeek-R1. The recent improvement Dr. GRPO (COLM 2025) identifies the response-level length bias caused by per-trajectory length normalization in GRPO and proposes removing this normalization, claiming the resulting optimizer is "unbiased." We show that this claim is incomplete. Specifically, we establish an impossibility theorem: under the standard outcome reward + GRPO setting, no length-based weighting scheme can simultaneously achieve the following two properties. (P1) Gradient unbiasedness: the gradient estimator is an unbiased estimate of the true policy gradient. (P2) Length invariance: each trajectory's effective contribution to the gradient is independent of its token length. GRPO approximately satisfies P2 but violates P1; Dr. GRPO satisfies P1 but violates P2. We characterize the complete tradeoff spectrum via the parametric family f_alpha(L) = L^{alpha - 1}, where alpha = 0 recovers GRPO, alpha = 1 recovers Dr. GRPO, and provide quantitative analysis showing that Dr. GRPO's length bias can cause longer trajectories to dominate gradient updates by a factor proportional to the length ratio. Our results reveal that neither algorithm is universally "done right"; they occupy opposite ends of a fundamental and unavoidable tradeoff.

</details>


### [148] [Bitcoin Price Direction Prediction via Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Fusion of Social Sentiment and Technical Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23370)
*Muhammad Abdullah Haroon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Learning (RAML)框架，基于滚动24小时波动率动态检测市场状态（稳定/波动），通过可学习的sigmoid门控机制自适应融合比特币OHLCV价格特征与Reddit情感嵌入——在波动期信任情感、稳定期信任价格。在3,491个每小时观测数据上的实验表明，RAML在3小时和6小时预测窗口上分别达到0.5474和0.5513的macro-F1，消融实验证明自适应融合是必要的，静态拼接在6小时窗口上F1降至0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币价格预测在子日时间尺度上是计算金融领域的难题，传统方法将OHLCV技术特征与情感特征通过静态拼接融合，无论市场状态如何都使用相同的融合权重。这与行为金融学文献不一致——文献表明零售情感在波动期最具预测性，在平静期则充满噪声。因此需要一个能根据市场状态动态调整融合权重的框架。

Method: RAML框架：1) 滚动24小时波动率将观测值划分为稳定和波动两种二进制市场状态；2) 设计可学习的sigmoid门控，根据检测到的市场状态调整情感嵌入相对于价格嵌入的权重（波动期更信任情感，稳定期更信任价格）；3) 在3,491个每小时观测数据上，结合比特币OHLCV数据与Reddit /r/Bitcoin FinBERT情感，对比了四种模型（纯价格BiLSTM、纯情感分类器、静态拼接BiLSTM、RAML），分别在3小时和6小时预测窗口上评估。

Result: RAML在3小时预测窗口上macro-F1为0.5474，AUC为0.5084；在6小时窗口上macro-F1为0.5513。消融实验确认每个组件（情感分支、状态检测、自适应融合）都是必要的。用静态拼接替代自适应加权后，6小时窗口上召回率崩溃，F1降至0.14。

Conclusion: 状态条件自适应融合是多模态金融预测中必要设计原则，RAML框架通过动态检测市场状态并据此调整情感与价格特征的融合权重，显著优于静态拼接方法。

Abstract: Bitcoin price prediction on sub-daily timescales is a hard open problem in computational finance. Bitcoin exhibits fat-tailed returns, non-stationary dynamics, and a price discovery process influenced by social discourse on Reddit and Twitter. Conventional approaches fuse OHLCV technical features with sentiment via static concatenation, applying identical fusion weights regardless of market state. This is inconsistent with the behavioural finance literature, which shows that retail sentiment is most predictive during volatile periods and noisy during calm ones. This paper proposes Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Learning (RAML), which conditions fusion of sentiment and price features on a dynamically detected binary market regime. Rolling 24-hour volatility partitions observations into stable and volatile regimes; a learnable sigmoid gate adjusts the weight of the sentiment embedding relative to the price embedding, trusting sentiment more during volatility and price dynamics more during stable phases. The system is evaluated on 3,491 hourly observations (July 2024-September 2025), combining Bitcoin OHLCV data with Reddit /r/Bitcoin FinBERT sentiment. Four models are compared - price-only BiLSTM, sentiment-only classifier, static-concatenation BiLSTM, and RAML - across 3-hour and 6-hour horizons, with an ablation study isolating the sentiment branch, regime detection, and adaptive fusion. RAML achieves macro-F1 of 0.5474 (3h) and 0.5513 (6h), with the highest AUC at 3 hours (0.5084), indicating better calibration. Ablation confirms every component is necessary, and replacing adaptive weighting with concatenation causes recall collapse at 6 hours (F1: 0.14). These results establish regime-conditioned adaptive fusion as a necessary design principle for multi-modal financial forecasting.

</details>


### [149] [When Can Depth Replace Precision? A Resource Theory of Quantized Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23390)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定输入-输出映射下，低比特残差计算能否替代缺失的数值精度。通过将量化残差系统建模为从低比特运算库中选择字段的纯调度问题，并利用松弛控制刻画其无限深度极限。结果表明：深度替代精度依赖于声明的运算库、时间范围、执行语义和路由模型；纯调度以O(D⁻¹)或O(D⁻ᶿ+D⁻¹)的速率逼近松弛类；全状态写回引入Dρz惩罚，而增量误差反馈则通过有界进位项和公共格守恒律避免此问题；固定教师对偶证明该速率是紧的；深度L的一阶高精度比较器需要D=Θ(L)才能精度匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定输入-输出映射条件下，低比特残差计算何时能够替代缺失的数值精度，即深度与精度之间的权衡关系。

Method: 将量化残差系统建模为从低比特运算库中选择字段的纯调度问题，引入松弛控制理论分析无限深度极限，推导纯调度逼近松弛类的收敛速率，分析全状态写回与增量误差反馈两种执行语义的差异，并通过固定教师对偶证明速率紧性。

Result: 纯调度以O(D⁻¹)（有界变差时间依赖）或O(D⁻ᶿ+D⁻¹)（Hölder依赖）速率逼近松弛类；全状态写回引入Dρz惩罚，增量误差反馈只引入有界进位项并满足公共格守恒律；固定教师对偶显示深度L的一阶高精度比较器需要D=Θ(L)才能精度匹配。

Conclusion: 深度替代精度并非无条件成立，而是依赖于声明的运算库、时间范围、执行语义和路由模型；存在精确的结构下限，增加深度也无法消除；学习型码本增加元数据资源，状态依赖路由引入混合事件条件。

Abstract: When can additional low-bit residual computation replace missing numerical precision for a fixed input-output map? We model a quantized residual system over a fixed horizon as a pure schedule selecting fields from a declared low-bit operation library, and use relaxed controls to characterize its infinite-depth limit. The distance from the target to the closed relaxed reachable set is the exact structural floor: no increase in depth can remove it for that library. Pure schedules approach the relaxed class at rate $O(D^{-1})$ under bounded-variation time dependence and $O(D^{-\vartheta}+D^{-1})$ under Holder dependence of exponent $\vartheta$. Execution arithmetic can reverse this conclusion: full-state write-back introduces a $Dρ_z$ penalty and can freeze residual updates, whereas increment error feedback replaces this growth by a bounded carry term and obeys an exact common-lattice conservation law. A fixed-teacher converse makes this rate sharp: for coherent depth-$L$ first-order high-precision comparators, accuracy matching requires $D=Θ(L)$. Learned codebooks add a metadata resource, while state-dependent routing introduces hybrid event conditions. Verified primal and dual bounds yield feasible, impossible, or unresolved decisions before training. Companion software implements the workflow, and Lean 4 machine-checks the exact discrete core. Depth replaces precision only relative to a declared library, horizon, execution semantics, and routing model.

</details>


### [150] [Directional Influence Function: Estimating Training Data Influence in Constrained Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23388)
*Xin Wang,R. Tyrrell Rockafellar,Xuegang,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统影响函数在约束学习场景下失效的问题，提出方向性影响函数(DIF)，通过将约束学习的最优条件建模为变分不等式，有效估计训练样本对模型解的影响，在约束线性回归和公平性约束CNN上验证了其准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典影响函数(IF)在约束学习场景下不可靠，因为数据扰动会同时改变目标函数和可行域，导致影响估计违反可行性约束。

Method: 将约束学习的最优条件形式化为变分不等式(VI)，通过分析数据扰动如何影响该VI来构建方向性影响函数(DIF)。

Result: 在约束线性回归中DIF成功恢复留一法重训练结果，而IF和基于惩罚的IF存在显著偏差；在公平性约束CNN中，DIF能准确预测数据移除后的测试损失变化，与实际重训练高度一致。

Conclusion: DIF为约束学习场景下的数据归因提供了一种高效且可靠的工具。

Abstract: As constrained learning becomes increasingly common, models are trained under explicit feasibility requirements to enforce fairness, safety, robustness, regulariza- tion, and physics or logic constraints. Understanding how training samples in- fluence the model solution (e.g., learned parameters) is crucial for interpretability and robustness. The classical influence function (IF) estimates sample contribu- tions via local sensitivity analysis, measuring how the solution changes when a specific training sample is perturbed or removed. However, IF becomes unreli- able in constrained settings: data perturbations can reshape both the objective and the feasible region, leading to estimates that violate feasibility. In response, we propose the Directional Influence Function (DIF), a novel estimator that explicitly incorporates these constraints into influence estimation. DIF formulates the opti- mality conditions of constrained learning as a variational inequality (VI) and ana- lyzes how perturbing training data affects this VI. We validate DIF on constrained linear regression and demonstrate that it recovers leave-one-out retraining results, whereas IF and penalty-based IF exhibit significant bias. We further apply DIF to fairness-constrained CNNs, where DIF accurately predicts test loss changes under data removal and aligns closely with actual retraining. Our results establish DIF as an efficient and reliable tool for data attribution in constrained learning.

</details>


### [151] [A Multi-stage Constrained Optimization Framework for Data-driven Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23480)
*Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段约束优化框架(MCOF)，通过熵约束VAE、均匀变换和约束优先级过滤方法，解决了VAE约束优化中潜在空间采样、活动变量识别和约束强制三大难题，在合成问题和ZINC250k药物设计任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE在高维含噪数据约束优化中存在三个挑战：(i)潜在空间有效采样困难，(ii)难以识别影响目标和约束的活动决策变量，(iii)强制约束会破坏训练稳定性。

Method: 提出MCOF框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)熵约束VAE(EC-VAE)结合特征选择器，将目标与约束信息嵌入指定子集，在低维子空间优化；(2)均匀变换(UT)模块对每个维度进行概率积分变换，将不规则聚合后验转换为有界均匀分布；(3)约束优先级过滤方法(CPFM)通过交替违反缩减和目标缩减步骤，在无乘子估计下获得满足约束的解。

Result: 在合成问题上消融验证了各模块的有效性，并恢复了解析最优解；在ZINC250k药物设计任务上生成的分子满足约束条件且完全新颖。

Conclusion: MCOF有效解决了VAE约束优化的三大挑战，在低维子空间优化、消除后验坍塌和高斯混合偏差、无需乘子估计的约束处理等方面均有贡献。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) transform high-dimensional, often noisy data into a compact latent representation, making downstream optimization more tractable. Three challenges persist in VAE-based constrained optimization: (i) sampling effectively within the latent space, (ii) identifying the active decision variables that actually influence the objective and constraints, and (iii) enforcing constraints without destabilizing training. We propose a Multi-stage Constrained Optimization Framework (MCOF). First, an entropy-constrained VAE (EC-VAE) coupled with a feature selector embeds objective and constraint information into a designated subset of latent variables, so that optimization proceeds over a low-dimensional subspace while the remaining coordinates supply solution diversity. Second, a Uniform Transformation (UT) module applies a per-dimension probability integral transform, replacing the irregular aggregate posterior with a uniform distribution over a bounded box and mitigating posterior collapse and Gaussian mixture bias. Third, a constraint-priority filter method (CPFM) solves the resulting surrogate problem by alternating violation-reduction and objective-reduction steps under a filter acceptance test, returning solutions that are feasible for the learned surrogate to a specified tolerance without requiring multiplier estimation. Finally, unselected latent coordinates are resampled to generate diverse decodings of a single optimized solution. We validate MCOF on a synthetic problem, where we ablate each stage and recover the analytic optimum, and on a ZINC250k drug design task, where the generated molecules satisfy the imposed constraints and are entirely novel relative to the training set.

</details>


### [152] [Optimal Reward Shaping: Autonomous Car Parking Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23617)
*Emre Özkaya,Nicolas R. Gauger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数化奖励塑形框架，包含覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化和对齐回合终止机制，用于解决非完整约束下无模型强化学习的局部最小值问题，并通过基于代理的贝叶斯优化对环境奖励参数和算法超参数进行联合元优化，在自主平行泊车任务上显著优于未校准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整约束下无模型强化学习的奖励函数设计容易导致严重的局部最小值问题，如策略瘫痪或过度保守的避障行为，亟需有效的奖励塑形方法。

Method: 提出参数化奖励塑形框架，包含覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化和对齐回合终止机制；揭示环境奖励参数与算法超参数的深度相互依赖关系，采用基于代理的贝叶斯优化进行联合元优化。

Result: 经过联合优化的DQN智能体解决了特征性控制失效模式，在成功率和轨迹平滑度上均显著优于未校准基线。

Conclusion: 环境奖励参数和算法超参数存在深度协同依赖性，联合元优化是实现稳定收敛的关键，所提框架有效解决了非完整约束下的控制失效问题。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions for model-free reinforcement learning under non-holonomic constraints remains a persistent challenge, often resulting in severe local minima such as policy paralysis or over-conservative hazard avoidance. In this work, we present a parameterized reward shaping framework featuring coverage-gated alignment feedback, drive-direction switch regularization, and an aligned episode termination mechanism evaluated on an autonomous parallel parking task. Crucially, we show that environmental reward parameters and algorithmic hyperparameters are deeply co-dependent, requiring joint meta-optimization to achieve stable convergence. By employing surrogate-based Bayesian optimization, our co-optimized Deep Q-Network (DQN) agent resolves characteristic control failure modes, significantly outperforming uncalibrated baselines across both success rate and trajectory smoothness.

</details>


### [153] [A Statistical Difference between Single-Layer Learning and Hierarchical Learning in Wide Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23397)
*Sumio Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究三层宽神经网络，证明训练输入到隐藏层的权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差，且固定权重会导致参数空间出现奇点，而训练权重则不会，表明奇点在宽神经网络中仍发挥重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次神经网络在AI中广泛应用，但其数学性质尚未完全理解。无限宽极限下存在两种理论框架（固定核与优化核），本文旨在通过有限大宽度的三层网络研究这两种设置的区别，特别关注奇点对泛化性能的影响。

Method: 研究一个具有有限大隐藏单元数量的三层神经网络，比较训练输入到隐藏层权重与固定这些权重两种设置下的泛化误差和参数空间性质。

Result: 训练输入到隐藏层权重比固定它们获得更小的泛化误差；固定权重的设置中参数空间存在奇点，而训练权重的设置中则不存在。

Conclusion: 奇点在宽神经网络中仍然发挥重要作用，这对理解深度学习的理论性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: Hierarchical neural networks are widely used in artificial intelligence, yet their mathematical properties remain incompletely understood. In the infinite-width limit, two different theoretical frameworks have been proposed. One reduces deep learning to kernel regression with a fixed kernel by assuming that the parameters remain close to their initialization, whereas the other allows the parameters to move away from their initialization, requiring the kernel itself to be optimized.
  In this paper, we study a three-layer neural network with a finite but large number of hidden units. We show that training the input-to-hidden weights yields a smaller generalization error than keeping them fixed. Furthermore, the latter setting exhibits singularities in the parameter space, whereas the former does not. These findings indicate that singularities play an essential role even in wide neural networks.

</details>


### [154] [Scale Weight Decay and Train Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23777)
*Anuj Apte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缩放权重衰减（scaled weight decay）方法，通过将权重衰减系数与当前学习率相对于峰值学习率的比例（η/η_max）相乘，避免标准权重衰减导致的权重范数持续缩小问题，在保持稳定性优势的同时不改变渐近优化目标。在混合专家模型训练中，该方法在72M-930M参数规模下以约600 tokens/active parameter的训练量实现30%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解耦权重衰减会导致网络权重在训练过程中持续缩小，这改变了未正则化方法的渐近优化目标（引入额外渐近偏差）。受Robbins-Monro条件启发，希望设计一种权重衰减方法，既能保留权重衰减的稳定性优势，又不会改变渐近优化目标。

Method: 将权重衰减系数缩放为当前学习率与峰值学习率的比例（η/η_max），应用于随机梯度下降和谱优化器Muon。通过理论证明该方法保留了未正则化方法的渐近平稳性保证，并通过稳态分析解释为何标准权重衰减下权重范数持续缩小而缩放权重衰减下权重范数趋于稳定。

Result: 在72M-930M参数规模的混合专家模型训练中（~600 tokens/active parameter），Muon-SW比标准权重衰减的Muon达到相同验证损失快30%。

Conclusion: 缩放权重衰减是一种简单有效的改进方法，仅需几行代码实现，有望显著加速前沿模型的预训练。

Abstract: The discovery of scaling laws has motivated training neural networks on ever increasing quantities of data. This is typically done with a constant decoupled weight decay which causes the network weights to shrink steadily over the course of training. Taking inspiration from the Robbins--Monro conditions, we propose to scale weight decay by the fraction of the peak learning rate $η/η_{\max}$. We prove that this scaled weight decay preserves the asymptotic stationarity guarantees of the corresponding unregularized methods for both stochastic gradient descent and the non-Euclidean spectral optimizer Muon, thereby avoiding the additional asymptotic bias introduced by constant decoupled weight decay. This retains the stability benefits of weight decay without changing the asymptotic optimization target. Using a steady-state analysis, we explain why under standard weight decay the weight norm shrinks steadily as training proceeds, whereas under scaled weight decay it settles to a roughly constant value. When applied to the training of mixture-of-experts models, Muon with scaled weight decay (Muon-SW) consistently outpaces Muon with identical hyperparameters, reaching the same validation loss $\mathbf{30\%}$ faster at our largest scale across models from $72 - 930$ million parameters trained at $\sim 600$ tokens per active parameter. If this trend continues to hold, the method promises to substantially accelerate the pre-training of frontier models while requiring only a few lines of code to implement.

</details>


### [155] [Transfer Learning Architectures for Scalable Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23404)
*Jaewook Lee,Ethan Errington,Christian D. Lorenz,Miao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了11种迁移学习代理模型与4种高斯过程方法在多保真贝叶斯优化中的表现，发现迁移学习在分子和材料问题上显著优于GP，且贪婪利用迁移学习均值比不确定性驱动的探索更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程作为多保真贝叶斯优化的默认代理模型，存在两大局限：数据积累时扩展性差，且假设的平滑性在分子和材料搜索空间中常被违反。迁移学习虽已用于属性预测，但尚未被测试为闭环优化的引擎，因此需要系统评估其在该场景下的有效性。

Method: 在相同的选择规则、保真度预算和模型大小下，对11种迁移学习代理模型与4种GP方法进行基准测试，涵盖从合成函数到真实化学与材料问题的9个任务。

Result: GP在平滑、低维函数上表现最佳，但在分子和材料问题上表现最差；迁移学习代理模型使用更少的计算资源达成了显著更优的解。由于采集策略在所有代理模型上固定，这一优势归因于代理模型本身。不确定性驱动的探索并非稳定有益，校准度不能预测优化性能。

Conclusion: 贪婪地利用迁移学习均值是更稳健的默认策略，迁移学习是分子和材料多保真贝叶斯优化的首选代理模型。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MFBO) to balance cheap, approximate evaluations against scarce, expensive ones, with a predictive surrogate at its core. Gaussian processes (GPs) are the default choice, but they scale poorly as data accumulate and assume a smooth landscape that molecular and materials search spaces routinely violate. Transfer learning offers an alternative suited to this regime: it learns a representation from abundant cheap data and adapts it to sparse expensive data. Despite its use in property prediction, transfer learning has not been tested as the engine of a closed-loop optimization. Here we benchmark eleven transfer-learning surrogates against four GP methods under an identical selection rule, fidelity budget, and model size, across nine tasks spanning synthetic functions to real chemistry and materials problems. GPs win on smooth, low-dimensional functions but perform worst on molecular and materials problems, where transfer-learning surrogates reach substantially better solutions using far less computation. Because acquisition policy is held fixed across surrogates, this advantage is attributable to the surrogate itself. Uncertainty-driven exploration is not reliably beneficial, and calibration does not predict optimization performance, so greedy exploitation of the transfer-learned mean is the more robust default. Transfer learning is therefore the surrogate of choice for molecular and materials MFBO.

</details>


### [156] [Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG: A Comparative Study of Direct and ECG-Mediated Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23406)
*Bo Wu,Haoling Wang,Zhuodiao Kuang,Kateryna Shapovalenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模生理相关性分析发现PPG与动脉血压的耦合强度（|r|=0.247）显著强于ECG（r=0.018），直接PPG-to-BP预测方法在174万条数据上达到英国高血压学会A级精度（SBP MAE=4.82 mmHg，DBP MAE=4.31 mmHg），优于所有依赖ECG的间接方法，证明可穿戴设备可直接利用PPG信号实现准确连续的血压监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有研究认为ECG与血压生理关联更强、需通过ECG作为中间表征进行血压预测的假设，验证直接PPG-to-BP路径的可行性，以简化可穿戴血压监测系统的设计。

Method: 在MIMIC-III波形数据库上进行大规模生理相关性分析（PPG vs ABP、ECG vs ABP）；采用多种最先进深度学习模型，系统比较直接PPG-to-BP预测与ECG介导的间接预测管道；在3,127名患者的174万条数据段上评估。

Result: PPG与ABP的相关系数|r|=0.247（p<0.001），ECG与ABP的r=0.018（p=0.187）；直接PPG-to-BP方法达到BHS Grade A（SBP MAE=4.82 mmHg，DBP MAE=4.31 mmHg），ECG介导方法仅达Grade B。

Conclusion: 准确连续的血压监测可直接通过可穿戴PPG信号实现，无需依赖ECG，从而支持更简单、更高效的连接健康系统管道。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring is essential for connected health systems and wearable devices, enabling early detection, longitudinal tracking, and personalized management of cardiovascular disease. Many prior approaches attempt to estimate BP indirectly by reconstructing electrocardiography (ECG) from photoplethysmography (PPG), assuming ECG provides a stronger physiological link to BP. However, ECG sensing is less accessible in wearable settings and may introduce unnecessary complexity.
  In this work, we first perform a large-scale physiological correlation analysis on the MIMIC-III waveform database, revealing that PPG exhibits substantially stronger coupling with arterial blood pressure (ABP) ($|r|=0.247$, $p<0.001$) than ECG does ($r=0.018$, $p=0.187$), challenging the assumption that ECG provides a superior intermediate representation. Motivated by this insight, we conduct a systematic comparison between direct PPG-to-BP prediction and ECG-mediated pipelines using multiple state-of-the-art deep learning models.
  Across 1.74M segments from 3,127 patients, direct PPG-to-BP prediction achieves British Hypertension Society Grade A performance ($\mathrm{MAE}_{\mathrm{SBP}} = 4.82 mmHg$, $\mathrm{MAE}_{\mathrm{DBP}} = 4.31 mmHg$), outperforming all ECG-mediated approaches, which achieve only Grade B accuracy.
  Our findings suggest that accurate continuous BP monitoring can be achieved directly from wearable PPG signals, enabling simpler, more efficient pipelines for real-world connected health systems.

</details>


### [157] [Harmonized Interpretable ECG Waveform Features for Robust Cross-Dataset Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23412)
*Jie Lin,Weijie Sun,Sunil V. Kalmady,Anita Khalafbeigi,Abram Hindle,Padma Kaul,Russell Greiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于心电波形直接提取的标准化特征表示（FeatureDB），在两个大型队列、三个任务上验证了跨数据集泛化能力。XGBoost模型在统一特征空间上内部AUROC为0.79-0.82，跨数据集AUROC为0.74-0.78，保留了源域性能的90%以上，且与端到端ConvNeXt相比具有更强的跨数据集迁移稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图风险预测模型因协议、人群和测量差异难以跨医院迁移，缺乏标准化且可解释的跨数据集特征表示。

Method: 从原始心电波形计算统一的可解释特征表示（FeatureDB），包括形态学/心率变异性总结、自回归和小波时频描述符；使用XGBoost模型训练，并在MIMIC-IV和Alberta Cohort两个队列上进行内部和双向外部验证。

Result: 内部AUROC为0.79-0.82，跨数据集AUROC为0.74-0.78；外部迁移时AUROC保留源域内部性能90%以上；统一特征集内部AUROC与原始机器测量模型差距在10%以内；端到端ConvNeXt内部AUROC更高，但统一特征表示在跨数据集迁移稳定性上更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 一致的心电波形特征接口能够保持迁移性能、支持真实外部验证，为跨站点临床预测提供了透明且可替代的方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used for cardiovascular risk prediction, yet models often fail to transfer across hospitals because of protocol, population, and measurement differences. We benchmark cross-dataset generalization on three tasks - heart failure classification, 30-day all-cause mortality, and 30-day mortality among sinus-rhythm ECGs - using two large cohorts (MIMIC-IV and the Alberta Cohort). To reduce vendor-specific measurement mismatch, we build a harmonized, interpretable feature representation computed directly from raw waveforms: FeatureDB morphology/heart-rate-variability summaries plus compact time-frequency descriptors (autoregressive and wavelet features). We train XGBoost models on this unified feature space and evaluate with patient-disjoint internal and bidirectional external testing. We pre-specify two hypotheses: (H1) external AUROC retains at least 90% of source-site internal AUROC under transfer, and (H2) internal AUROC of the harmonized feature set stays within 10% of dataset-native machine-measurement models. Across tasks, internal AUROC is 0.79-0.82 and cross-dataset AUROC is 0.74-0.78, with larger and direction-dependent AUPRC shifts under transfer. As an exploratory benchmark, an end-to-end ConvNeXt model trained directly on raw ECG waveforms with age and sex achieves higher internal AUROC, while the harmonized representation remains competitive in relative cross-dataset transfer stability. These findings show that a consistent waveform-derived feature interface preserves performance, supports realistic external validation, and provides a transparent alternative for cross-site clinical prediction.

</details>


### [158] [Short-Term Pain for Long-Term Gain: Adaptive Experiment with Post-Commitment Reward Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23432)
*Puping Jiang,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究决策者在学习环境中的短期最优行动与长期收益之间的权衡，提出RAEC算法（用于离散选项）和ROSCOC算法（用于连续凸优化），在实验阶段预留部分轮次识别承诺阶段的最佳选项，同时最小化剩余轮次的短期遗憾，给出了严格的最优遗憾界，并通过实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者在学习环境中面临短期最优行动与长期收益之间的根本性权衡，即实验阶段的适应性测试可能与最终承诺阶段的收益不一致（存在承诺后奖励偏移），需要理解这一权衡背后的代价。

Method: 提出RAEC算法，预留实验阶段的一部分轮次来识别承诺后最佳选项，其余轮次用于最小化短期遗憾。在已知结构知识的情况下，识别排名变化的偏移分量比估计绝对幅度更重要。针对凹承诺奖励和投资组合选择，提出ROSCOC算法，将预留探索历史直接转化为承诺投资组合。

Result: 建立了RAEC在所有参数区间上的遗憾上界及匹配的极小化下界，给出了平衡短期表现与长期承诺代价的紧刻画。ROSCOC算法也实现了紧的遗憾界。数值实验验证了理论预测，且算法优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过预留部分实验轮次的方法可以有效平衡短期表现与长期承诺之间的权衡，且本文建立的遗憾界是最优的。

Abstract: Decision-makers in learning environments face a dilemma when their short-term optimal actions may not favor their long-term benefits the most. To understand the fundamental tradeoff behind the dilemma, we study adaptive experimentation with post-commitment reward shifts. During an experiment phase, the decision-maker may adaptively test multiple options; during a subsequent commitment phase, the decision-maker must commit to a single option, whose reward may differ from its pre-commitment reward. We propose the Reserved Arm Eliminations for Commitment (RAEC) algorithm, which reserves a predetermined portion of the experiment phase to identify the best post-shift option while using the remaining rounds to minimize short-run regret. We establish regret upper bounds for RAEC across all parameter regimes and matching minimax lower bounds, providing a tight characterization of the cost of balancing short-term performance and long-term commitment.
  We also study two extensions. With prior structural knowledge linking pre- and post-shift rewards, we show that correctly identifying the ranking-changing component of the shift is more important than estimating its absolute magnitude. For settings with concave commitment rewards and portfolio choice, we develop the Reserved Online Stochastic Convex Optimization for Commitment (ROSCOC) algorithm, which directly converts its reserved exploration history into a commitment portfolio and achieves tight regret bound. Finally, we also conduct numerical experiments which confirm that our proposed algorithms achieve the desired regret predicted by our theory, and also outperform other baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [159] [PerturbPFN: Probing the Limits of Synthetic Priors in Drug Perturbation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23447)
*Yuche Gao,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Siyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerturbPFN是一种基于Prior-Data Fitted Networks的摊销模型，通过层次化合成结构先验和结构因果模型解码器，从高维表达数据中推断潜在系统图、稀疏原子干预靶点及干预强度，实现未知靶点化学扰动预测，无需测试时梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细胞扰动预测面临三大挑战：未知靶点和机制、高维表达响应、小分子设计空间覆盖有限。需要一种能够在未见化学扰动下进行预测且具有可解释性的方法。

Method: 采用PFN风格的摊销模型，在层次化合成结构先验下训练。不直接回归高维表达，而是推断潜在系统图、稀疏原子干预靶点及干预强度，通过结构因果模型(SCM)解码器传播效应。模型完全在生物学启发的图与表达模拟器生成的先验预测合成片段上训练，实现结构化上下文学习。

Result: 在真实单细胞扰动数据和合成基准上评估，涵盖效应预测、靶点识别和调控结构发现。与专用基线相比，PerturbPFN提供了互补的权衡，实现了具有竞争力的扰动预测和低推理成本，同时提供靶点、强度和系统结构的可解释中间估计。

Conclusion: PerturbPFN在无需测试时梯度更新的情况下，通过结构化上下文学习实现了可解释的未知靶点扰动预测，为单细胞扰动响应预测提供了一种高效且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to unseen chemical perturbations is challenging due to unknown targets and mechanisms, high-dimensional expression responses, and limited experimental coverage of the large small-molecule design space. We propose PerturbPFN, a PFN-style amortized model for unknown-target perturbation prediction under a hierarchical synthetic structural prior. Instead of directly regressing high-dimensional expression responses, PerturbPFN infers a latent system graph, sparse atomic intervention targets, and intervention strengths, then propagates their effects through an SCM decoder. The model is trained entirely on prior-predictive synthetic episodes generated from biologically motivated graph and expression simulators, enabling structured in-context learning without test-time gradient updates. We evaluate PerturbPFN on both real single-cell perturbation data and synthetic benchmarks, covering effect prediction, target identification, and regulatory structure discovery. Our results show that PerturbPFN offers a complementary trade-off to specialized baselines, achieving competitive perturbation prediction with low inference cost while exposing interpretable intermediate estimates of targets, strengths, and system structure.

</details>


### [160] [Local Regularization Does Not Characterize Multiclass PAC Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23449)
*Eric Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数至多为2、可实现PAC样本复杂度为O(1/ε log(1/δ))的可数类，证明局部正则化（Local Regularization）不能刻画多类PAC可学习性，从而否定了Asilis等人提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究局部正则化原则是否能够刻画多类PAC可学习性这一开放问题，即验证Asilis等人的猜想是否成立。

Method: 构造反例：假设为完全图的边，实例为锦标赛（有向完全图）。在测试锦标赛中，分数固定边的排序，而训练样本独立地移除竞争者。利用循环三角形强制产生足够多的逆序，使得在任意大样本量下，存活的竞争者仍产生恒定的人口误差。

Result: 存在一个可数类，其Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维数≤2，可实现PAC样本复杂度为O(1/ε log(1/δ))，但没有任何局部正则化器能够学习该类。

Conclusion: 局部正则化不能刻画多类PAC可学习性，该原则不是多类PAC可学习性的充分条件。

Abstract: Local regularization assigns each hypothesis a test-point-dependent score and predicts with a minimum-score hypothesis consistent with the sample. Asilis et al. asked whether this principle characterizes multiclass PAC learnability. We give a negative answer. There is a countable class of Daniely--Shalev-Shwartz dimension at most two with realizable PAC sample complexity \[ O\!\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log\frac{1}δ\right), \] that no local regularizer learns. Hypotheses are edges of complete graphs and instances are tournaments. At a test tournament, the scores fix an edge ranking while the training sample independently removes competitors. Cyclic triangles force enough inversions that surviving competitors produce constant population error at arbitrarily large sample sizes.

</details>


### [161] [Generalization bounds and sample complexity for remaining useful life prediction from complete degradation trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23454)
*Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了剩余寿命预测中的样本复杂度理论框架，包含七个主要结论：推导了分布无关泛化界和极小化最优下界（收敛率Θ(p/n)）；量化了物理知识可将深度网络的数据需求降低两个数量级；揭示了车队变异性和右删失数据对预测精度的影响。在涡扇发动机、电池和轴承基准上验证了理论预测，为数据收集、模型选择和物理假设评估提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据驱动的剩余寿命预测方法需要完整的退化轨迹进行训练，但"运行至失效"数据稀缺且昂贵，而实践者缺乏关于需要多少失效样本才能达到特定模型和精度目标的原理性指导。

Method: 建立了包含七个主要结果的样本复杂度框架：推导分布无关泛化界和极小化最优下界；通过Bernstein型分析实现极小化最优的O(p/n)速率；给出指数、幂律和拉伸指数退化的闭式表达式。

Result: 分布无关泛化界显示均方误差的均匀偏差以O(B²√(p/n))速率下降，极小化下界证明Θ(p/n)速率不可改进；物理知识可将深度网络数据需求降低两个数量级；在涡扇发动机、电池和轴承基准上验证了理论预测，平均误差在2-3倍以内。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据收集规划、模型复杂度选择和物理模型假设评估提供了实用指南，揭示了错误物理模型可能适得其反的现象，以及车队变异性和右删失对预测精度的根本性影响。

Abstract: Data-driven remaining useful life (RUL) prediction requires complete degradation trajectories for training, yet such run-to-failure data are scarce and expensive. Practitioners currently lack principled guidance on how many failure examples suffice for a given model and accuracy target. This paper develops a sample complexity framework for RUL prediction comprising seven main results organised around three themes. First, we establish fundamental learning rates: a distribution-free generalization bound shows that the uniform deviation of the mean squared error decreases as $O(B^{2}\sqrt{p/n})$, where $p$ is the model complexity and $n$ the number of trajectories, and a minimax lower bound proves that the $Θ(p/n)$ rate is unimprovable.} \rev{Second, we quantify how domain knowledge accelerates learning: incorporating degradation physics reduces data requirements by up to two orders of magnitude for deep networks, a Bernstein-type analysis achieves the minimax-optimal $O(p/n)$ rate under high signal-to-noise conditions, and closed-form penalties reveal when an incorrectly assumed physics model hurts rather than helps. Third, we characterise the impact of data quality: fleet variability induces an irreducible bias$-$variance tradeoff, while right-censored observations suffer an efficiency loss that depends critically on the degradation class.} Closed-form expressions are provided for exponential, power-law, and stretched-exponential degradation. \rev{Cross-domain validation against published turbofan, battery, and bearing benchmarks confirms the theoretical predictions within a factor of 2$-$3 on average. The results yield practical guidelines for planning data collection, selecting model complexity, and evaluating physics model assumptions in prognostics applications.

</details>


### [162] [Extending Fourier Neural Operators for Modeling Parameterized and Coupled PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23466)
*Cheng Jing,Uvini Balasuriya Mudiyanselage,Abhishek Verma,Kallol Bera,Shahid Rauf,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化和耦合偏微分方程，在傅里叶神经算子（FNO）基础上提出两种改进：一是通过超网络调制将算子条件化于物理参数，二是系统探索耦合系统中算子组件的架构设计。在电容耦合等离子体方程和Gray-Scott系统上，该方法相比强基线降低55-72%的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化与耦合PDEs在科学工程建模中至关重要，但现有的神经算子方法在处理这两方面时仍存在局限性。

Method: 1) 超网络调制：通过最小化架构修改，将FNO条件化于物理参数。2) 系统架构探索：针对耦合系统，研究算子组件如何在保持共享结构与交叉变量交互之间取得平衡。

Result: 在一维电容耦合等离子体方程和Gray-Scott系统上，所提方法相比强基线降低了55-72%的误差。

Conclusion: 有原则的调制方法和系统的设计探索能够有效提升神经算子处理参数化与耦合PDEs的性能。

Abstract: Parameterized and coupled partial differential equations (PDEs) are central to modeling phenomena in science and engineering, yet neural operator methods that address both aspects remain limited. We extend Fourier neural operators (FNOs) with minimal architectural modifications along two directions. For parameterized dynamics, we propose a hypernetwork-based modulation that conditions the operator on physical parameters. For coupled systems, we conduct a systematic exploration of architectural choices, examining how operator components can be adapted to balance shared structure with cross-variable interactions while retaining the efficiency of standard FNOs. Evaluations on benchmark PDEs, including the one-dimensional capacitively coupled plasma equations and the Gray-Scott system, show that our methods achieve up to 55-72% lower errors than strong baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of principled modulation and systematic design exploration.

</details>


### [163] [Learning to Optimize: Joint Routing and Flow Allocation on Sparse Non-Euclidean Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23467)
*Haomiao Sun,Fang He,Congyuan Ji,Xindi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究稀疏非欧网络上的联合取送货问题，提出双通道图注意力（DCGA）端到端强化学习框架，通过分离网络可达性与需求服务逻辑、结合模拟器约束解码器构建有效路径。在LinerLib基准上实现秒级推理，在特定规模以上实例达到SOTA质量，且优势随问题规模扩大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏非欧网络上联合取送货问题中，循环路由、货物流量分配与跨周期服务的紧耦合导致离散-连续混合决策空间复杂、可行域高度受限，现有方法难以高效求解。

Method: 提出Double-Channel Graph Attention (DCGA) 端到端强化学习框架：1）将网络可达性与需求服务逻辑分离为两个独立图通道；2）使用耦合模拟器的约束感知解码器构建有效路径。

Result: 在LinerLib基准上实现秒级推理，在特定规模以上实例中达到SOTA解质量，且优势随问题规模增大而显著扩大。

Conclusion: 这种结构感知的学习方法为现实路由与流量优化问题提供了有效、低延迟的求解引擎。

Abstract: We study an integrated pickup-and-delivery problem on sparse, non-Euclidean networks that jointly optimizes cyclic routing, cargo flow allocation, and cross-cycle service. The tight coupling of these operational constraints creates a complex discrete-continuous decision space with highly restricted feasible regions. To overcome these computational challenges, we propose Double-Channel Graph Attention (DCGA), an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework. DCGA isolates network reachability and demand-service logic into separate graph channels and constructs valid routes using a simulator-coupled, constraint-informed decoder. Experiments on LinerLib benchmarks demonstrate that DCGA achieves seconds-level inference and delivers state-of-the-art solution quality on instances beyond a specific scale, with its advantage over existing baselines widening significantly as problem size increases. Supported by extensive stability and ablation analyses, our results demonstrate that this structure-aware learning approach provides an effective, low-latency engine for realistic routing-and-flow optimization.

</details>


### [164] [Sparse Gaussian-Mixture-Model Q-Functions via Hadamard Overparametrization for Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23474)
*Minh Vu,Konstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏高斯混合模型Q函数（S-GMM-QFs）的在线离策略策略迭代框架，通过Hadamard过参数化和黎曼优化，在显著减少参数量的同时匹配或超越深度强化学习方法，并实现了可解释的稀疏化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度RL方法参数效率低、可解释性差，且离策略学习中的分布不匹配问题缺乏有效的几何框架来处理。

Method: 提出S-GMM-QFs，通过Hadamard过参数化实现可解释稀疏化，利用平滑正则化促进黎曼优化，在乘积黎曼流形上进行在线梯度下降，结合经验回放处理离策略数据。

Result: 数值实验表明，S-GMM-QFs在显著减少参数量的情况下匹配或超越深度RL方法，每观测样本的改进速度更快，且在低参数 regime 下泛化能力优于稀疏化的深度RL方法。

Conclusion: 参数效率与几何可解释性的结合使S-GMM-QFs成为一种高效、可解释的RL替代方案，尤其适用于低参数场景。

Abstract: This paper develops an online, off-policy policy-iteration framework for reinforcement learning (RL), based on sparse Gaussian-mixture-model Q-functions (S-GMM-QFs). The framework reconciles streaming, non-stationary data with the Riemannian structure of the parameter space while handling distributional mismatch through experience replay. S-GMM-QFs are introduced via Hadamard overparametrization, enabling interpretable sparsification through smooth regularization that facilitates Riemannian-based optimization. Overparametrization allows the framework to adaptively identify meaningful components from a large initial pool, yielding sparse models where interpretability emerges naturally from geometry: each component's parameters (means and covariances) explicitly encode its geometric role in the ambient state-action space. These geometric roles are learned through online gradient descent on a smooth objective over a (Cartesian-product) Riemannian manifold. Numerical tests demonstrate that S-GMM-QFs match or exceed deep RL methods while using substantially fewer parameters and achieving faster improvement per observed transition. Notably, parameter efficiency and interpretability combine to maintain strong generalization in low-parameter regimes where sparsified deep RL approaches degrade.

</details>


### [165] [Charging Phase Health Indicators for Battery State-of-Health Estimation: A Systematic Comparison of CC, CV, and Combined Approaches under Cross-Battery Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23482)
*Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了恒流和恒压充电阶段的健康指标在电池健康状态估计中的有效性，采用留一电池法验证发现CC+CV组合指标效果最佳（R²=0.874），且传统5折交叉验证会高估实际精度达119%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已从CC和CV充电阶段提取了大量健康指标，但这些指标在跨电池交叉验证中的有效性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在NASA电池老化数据集上采用严格的留一电池法验证，系统比较了仅CC、仅CV以及组合指标集的表现，评估了四个CV指标和CC持续时间的单独及组合效果。

Result: CC+CV组合方法性能最佳（R²=0.874）；标准5折交叉验证与LOBO验证之间存在119%的性能差距，传统评价方法严重高估实际精度。

Conclusion: CC和CV阶段捕获互补的退化信息，组合使用效果更优；同时需警惕传统交叉验证的乐观偏差，并针对数据与计算约束提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Accurate State-of-Health estimation is essential for safe battery operation and cost-effective maintenance. Although numerous health indicators have been derived from constant-current (CC) and constant-voltage (CV) charging phases, their effectiveness under realistic cross-battery validation remains insufficiently studied. This work addresses this gap through a systematic comparison of CC-only, CV-only, and combined indicator sets using rigorous Leave-One-Battery-Out (LOBO) validation on the NASA battery aging dataset. Four CV-phase indicators and CC phase duration are evaluated individually and in combination. Results show that the combined CC+CV approach achieves the best performance (R2 = 0.874), confirming that CC and CV phases capture complementary degradation information. Moreover, a 119% performance gap is observed between standard 5-fold cross-validation and LOBO validation, indicating that conventional evaluation overestimates practical accuracy. Based on these findings, practical guidelines are provided for indicator selection under data and computational constraints.

</details>


### [166] [An adaptive multi-fuzzy logic model for diagnosing transformer faults using dynamic weight optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23486)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应多模糊逻辑（AMFL）模型，通过集成多种DGA方法、模糊逻辑和动态权重调整机制，显著提高了变压器故障诊断的准确性、一致性和可靠性，特别是在复杂故障场景下优于传统固定权重多模糊系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DGA解释方法（如杜瓦尔三角、IEC比率、罗杰比率、多南堡比率和关键气体法）在多种故障条件下存在不一致性和准确率差异大的问题，亟需一种更稳健、自适应的综合诊断方法。

Method: 提出AMFL模型，将多种DGA方法与模糊逻辑相结合，并引入动态权重调整机制：系统迭代评估每种方法的诊断性能，识别多种故障类型，根据预测准确率调整权重；通过基于反馈的优化在每个周期后重新校准权重，确保收敛到最优解；在MATLAB/Simulink中实现并验证。

Result: AMFL模型在复杂故障场景下显著提高了诊断准确率，对新数据集具有更强的适应性；对比分析表明，该方法在准确性、一致性和故障检测可靠性方面均优于传统的固定权重多模糊系统。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种鲁棒、灵活的变压器状态监测诊断工具，支持更准确的资产管理决策。

Abstract: Dissolved gas analysis (DGA) is crucial for diagnosing early power transformer failures. Traditional DGA interpretation methods like Duval Triangle, IEC ratio, Roger ratio, Doernenburg ratio and Key Gas are inconsistent and vary in accuracy, especially for multiple fault conditions. We propose an Adaptive Multi-Fuzzy Logic (AMFL) model integrating multiple DGA methods with fuzzy logic and a dynamic weight adjustment mechanism. Unlike existing approaches with fixed weights, this system iteratively evaluates each method's diagnostic performance, identifies multiple fault types, and adjusts weights based on fault prediction accuracy. A feedback-based optimization recalibrates weights after each cycle to ensure optimal solution convergence. The model, implemented in MATLAB/Simulink, is validated against DGA datasets with known error conditions. Results show the AMFL model significantly improves diagnostic accuracy, especially in complex error scenarios, and enhances adaptability to new datasets. Comparative analysis demonstrates the proposed method outperforms traditional fixed weight multi-fuzzy systems in accuracy, consistency, and reliability of error detection. This work provides a robust, flexible diagnostic tool for transformer condition monitoring and supports more accurate asset management decisions.

</details>


### [167] [Learning Sampling Parameters for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23488)
*Arisrei Lim,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeSAMP提出了一种通过强化学习训练大语言模型来动态生成文本到图像扩散模型的采样参数调度策略的方法，该方法能根据用户提示和去噪时间步自适应调整参数，在Flux.1和SD3.5上相比基线取得了最高68.12%的胜率（人类偏好）和73.37%的胜率（VLM评判）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本到图像扩散模型中的采样参数（如提示词、负面提示词、无分类器引导尺度、噪声调度等）通常人工选择一次后便固定不变，但不同提示词和生成阶段可能需要不同的参数值，现有方法缺乏对采样参数的动态自适应调整能力。

Method: 将采样参数选择问题建模为强化学习问题：使用大型语言模型（LLM）根据用户提示生成各采样参数随时间的调度策略，通过人类偏好模型和VLM作为评判器提供奖励信号来优化模型。

Result: 在Flux.1 [dev]和Stable Diffusion 3.5上，LeSAMP相比基线在人类偏好分数上达到68.12%的胜率，在VLM评判上达到73.37%的胜率；用户研究中达到59.46%的胜率。

Conclusion: 学习得到的采样参数策略为现有后训练方法提供了补充途径，能够有效提升扩散模型的输出质量。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models expose many inference-time sampling parameters, including prompts, negative prompts, classifier-free guidance scales, and noise schedules. These parameters are typically manually chosen once and then held fixed across prompts and denoising timesteps, even though different prompts and stages of generation can benefit from different parameter values. We introduce LeSAMP, a framework for learning prompt-conditioned, timestep-varying sampling parameters. We formulate parameter selection as a reinforcement learning problem: Given a user prompt, a large language model is trained to emit schedules for the chosen sampling parameters. We optimize our model using rewards from human preference models and VLM-as-a-judge. We evaluate our model on Flux.1 [dev] and Stable Diffusion 3.5, and find that compared to baselines, LeSAMP has a win rate of up to 68.12% using human preference scores and 73.37% using VLM-as-a-judge. These gains are validated in a user study where we achieve win rates of up to 59.46% over previous baselines. Our results suggest that learned sampling-parameter policies provide a complementary approach to existing post-training methods for improving diffusion model outputs.

</details>


### [168] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Discovering Periodic Orbits in the Gravitational Three-Body Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23501)
*Nikolaos Kollias,Nikolaos Matzakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，无需初始条件，仅基于稀疏含噪观测数据训练的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）即可恢复三体问题中的周期轨道，包括训练数据中不存在的轨道族。通过二阶ODE公式、固定频率傅里叶特征、百分位数自适应精化及可训练缩放参数，23-25%的运行收敛到新轨道族。统计检验表明，训练数据源显著影响恢复轨道族的分布（p<0.001），而初始化分布则无显著影响（p=0.620）。恢复的轨道经过验证可达高精度（如与LIA I.A.1类匹配至7位有效数字，轨道闭合误差δ_T<10^{-9}）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌动力系统中定位周期轨道通常需要足够接近目标轨道的初始猜测，以便数值延拓或梯度搜索收敛。本文旨在探索能否在不提供初始条件的情况下，利用PINNs从稀疏含噪观测数据中直接恢复周期轨道。

Method: 采用二阶ODE公式、固定频率傅里叶特征、百分位数基于自适应精化以及可训练缩放参数的方法。在100个种子的两组实验中，分别使用不同训练数据源和初始化分布进行对照，通过χ²检验分析轨道族恢复的分布差异。

Result: 23-25%的随机种子运行收敛到训练数据中不存在的轨道族。训练数据源的变化显著改变了恢复轨道族的分布（p<0.001，Cramér's V=0.339），而两种初始化分布间的切换无显著影响（p=0.620，V=0.094）。恢复的轨道经验证具有高精度：与Li-Liao I.A.1类匹配至7位有效数字，轨道闭合误差小于10^{-9}。

Conclusion: PINNs无需初始条件即可从稀疏含噪观测中恢复混沌系统的周期轨道，并能泛化至训练数据中未出现的轨道族。训练数据的选择是决定恢复轨道分布的关键因素，而权重初始化分布的影响可忽略。该方法虽在适定初值问题上慢于传统积分器，但提供了可验证的而非仅似合理的轨道恢复能力。

Abstract: Locating periodic solutions of chaotic dynamical systems normally requires an initial guess close enough to the target orbit for numerical continuation or gradient-based search to converge. We show that Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) trained on sparse, noisy observations \emph{without} initial conditions recover periodic orbits of the gravitational three-body problem, including orbit families absent from the training data. The method rests on a second-order ODE formulation, fixed-frequency Fourier features, percentile-based adaptive refinement, and a trainable scaling parameter, each validated on forward problems. Across two 100-seed ensembles, $23$--$25\%$ of runs converge to families not present in the training data. We then ask what determines which family emerges. Two $χ^2$ tests give a consistent answer: changing the training data source significantly shifts the distribution of recovered families ($p < 0.001$, Cramér's $V = 0.339$), whereas switching between the two initialization distributions tested does not ($p = 0.620$, $V = 0.094$). The random seed selects which family a given run recovers; the \emph{distribution} the weights are drawn from does not shift the aggregate frequencies, but the training data does. The evidence is empirical: we do not characterize the loss landscape analytically, and PINNs remain slower than conventional integrators on well-posed initial-value problems. What the experiments establish is that the recovered orbits are verifiable rather than merely plausible: the identified ones refine to genuine periodic solutions, a network trained on Lagrange data recovers the figure-eight choreography (Li--Liao class I.A.1, matched to seven significant digits in $T^*$), and one trained on figure-eight data recovers a Broucke--Hadjidemetriou--Hénon orbit closing to $δ_T < 10^{-9}$.

</details>


### [169] [Impute On-Demand: Adaptive Correlated Time Series Imputation for Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23503)
*Zhichen Lai,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang,Dong Gong,Lina Yao,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaCTSi是一种自适应时序插补方法，通过One-shot TCN、学习型时间-传感器索引表和稀疏空间注意力等机制，实现对不完整传感器子集的自适应插补，在五个基准数据集上相比最强基线平均降低MAE 33.1%，并支持资源自适应推理，可部署于MCU等设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTS插补方法准确率虽高，但缺乏对动态IoT环境的适应性：易受传感器故障影响、无法选择性插补不完整传感器、采用静态架构无法适应资源可用性变化。

Method: AdaCTSi结合One-shot时序卷积网络与学习型时间-传感器索引表，将复杂时空特征解耦为传感器级嵌入；采用稀疏空间注意力提取动态空间相关性；通过相关性加权传感器选择获取充分的空间上下文。

Result: 在12种基线方法、3种适应性场景、5个基准数据集上，AdaCTSi相比最强基线平均降低MAE 33.1%。单模型支持传感器子集和资源自适应推理，内存需求小，可部署于包括MCU在内的商品化计算设备。

Conclusion: AdaCTSi有效解决了IoT环境下CTS插补的适应性问题，在准确性和部署灵活性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) applications generate vast amounts of Correlated Time Series (CTS) data that often contain missing values and require imputation. Existing methods emphasize accuracy but often lack adaptability to changing IoT environments: they are vulnerable to sensor failures, cannot selectively impute only incomplete sensors, and use static architectures that do not adapt to resource availability. To address these limitations, we propose AdaCTSi, an adaptive CTS imputer for changing environments. AdaCTSi combines a One-shot Temporal Convolutional Network with a Learned Time-Sensor Index Table to extract and decouple complex spatio-temporal features into sensor-wise embeddings, enabling adaptation to varying sensor subsets. Sparse Spatial Attention efficiently extracts dynamic spatial correlations, while Correlation-Weighted Sensor Selection selects informative sensors to provide sufficient spatial context. Experiments with twelve baseline methods, three adaptability scenarios, and five benchmark datasets covering traffic, air quality, and trajectory data show that AdaCTSi reduces MAE by an average of 33.1% relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. A single trained model supports sensor-subset and resource-adaptive inference, and its modest memory footprint enables deployment on commodity computing devices, including MCUs.

</details>


### [170] [Topological Data Analysis and Graph-Theoretic Approaches for Tennis Match Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23509)
*Jake Schwaderer,Alexander Bastien,Omid Khormali,Alejandro Navarrete,Mia Pesavento,Angelika Elderbrook*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于拓扑数据分析(TDA)和图论的方法预测网球比赛结果，在2000-2025年ATP单打数据上测试。第一种方法使用下星滤波和持续同调提取拓扑特征，结合随机森林模型达到66.2%准确率(AUC=0.719)，其中排名特征贡献36.3%，中心性特征25.5%，TDA特征24.0%。当无排名信息时，纯拓扑模型仍保持63.56%准确率。第二种方法使用改进的Katz相似度指标结合时间边权重，达到62.48%准确率。这是下星滤波在网球预测中的首次应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑数据分析(TDA)和图论方法在网球比赛结果预测中的有效性，验证网络拓扑结构能否捕捉选手间的竞争关系并提升预测性能，特别是在缺乏传统排名特征时的预测能力。

Method: 方法一：构建选手竞争网络，应用下星滤波(lower-star filtration)提取持续同调特征，采用四种拓扑摘要方法(VAB, HNAV, HWNAV, OW-HNPV)结合Modified Band Depth分析，并通过ego图近似和三角形消除进行算法优化，最终使用随机森林模型融合拓扑、图论和排名特征。方法二：改进的Katz相似度指标，引入时间边权重计算选手相似度进行预测。

Result: 随机森林模型准确率66.2%(AUC=0.719)；特征重要性：排名36.3%，中心性25.5%，TDA特征24.0%；纯拓扑模型在无排名时准确率63.56%；Katz相似度方法准确率62.48%。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析能有效捕捉网球选手竞争网络的结构信息，为预测提供互补信号。即使在缺乏排名数据时，仅凭网络拓扑特征仍能实现高于随机水平的预测。下星滤波在体育预测任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We present two approaches for predicting tennis match outcomes using topological data analysis and graph theory on ATP singles matches from 2000-2025. The first method applies lower-star filtration to player competitive networks, extracting topological features through persistent homology using four summary methods (VAB, HNAV, HWNAV, OW-HNPV) combined with Modified Band Depth analysis. Algorithmic optimizations including ego graph approximations and triangle elimination enable analysis of about 66k matches. Our Random Forest model achieves 66.2% accuracy (AUC = 0.719) using topological, graph-theoretic, and ranking features. Feature importance analysis reveals that rankings contribute 36.3%, centralities 25.5%, and TDA features 24.0%, with topological features providing complementary signal. When rankings are unavailable, the topology-only model maintains 63.56% accuracy, demonstrating that network-derived features alone capture meaningful competitive structure. The second method uses a modified Katz similarity index with temporal edge weighting, achieving 62.48% accuracy on held-out test data. This work represents the first application of lower-star filtration to tennis prediction, provides systematic comparison of four topological summary methods in sports analytics, and demonstrates that TDA can achieve above-chance prediction using network topology alone while providing additional value when combined with traditional features.

</details>


### [171] [Chamaileon: Cross-Context Binder Design with Contextualized Modeling and Mixed Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23518)
*Hengyuan Cao,Shizhuo Cheng,Mingxuan Liu,Weicheng Huang,Yunhong Lu,Chenxi Cai,Yan Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chamaileon框架，通过跨上下文结合景观建模统一了多目标和多状态蛋白质结合设计，采用上下文内复合物协同设计（I3CD）训练范式和混合路径采样（MoPS）推理策略，在CROSS基准上展示了生成适应多样化构象景观和多目标需求序列的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结合设计方法局限于单目标、单状态假设，无法建模多目标或多状态相互作用，难以满足面向高级功能的蛋白质设计需求。

Method: 提出Chamaileon框架，将问题形式化为跨上下文结合景观建模；提出In-Context Complex Co-Design (I3CD)训练范式用于上下文感知的序列-结构协同建模；推理时采用Mixture-of-Paths Sampling (MoPS)策略，在跨上下文中优化单一序列同时缓解高质量多构象配对数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在新构建的CROSS基准上，Chamaileon有效生成了适应多样化构象景观和多目标需求的序列。

Conclusion: Chamaileon统一了多目标和多状态结合设计，为功能导向的蛋白质设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative models has unlocked new potentials in protein binder design, a pivotal task in structural biology, by facilitating end-to-end generation via joint sequence-structure modeling or hallucination. However, existing approaches are predominantly implemented under a single-target, single-state assumption, limiting their ability to model multi-target or multi-state interactions required for advanced function-oriented protein design. Here, we introduce Chamaileon, which unifies multi-target and multi-state binder design by formulating the problem as cross-context binding landscape modeling. The framework is underpinned by a training paradigm termed In-Context Complex Co-Design (I3CD) for context-aware sequence-structure co-modeling. During inference, we employ Mixture-of-Paths Sampling (MoPS), a scalable strategy that optimizes a single sequence across contexts while alleviating the scarcity of high-quality multi-conformational paired data. Extensive evaluation on our newly constructed benchmark, CROSS, demonstrates that Chamaileon effectively generates sequences adaptable to diverse conformational landscapes and multi-target requirements. The code is available on https://github.com/caohengyuan/Chamaileon.

</details>


### [172] [Neonatal Hypoxic-ischaemic Encephalopathy Classification from the EEG and HRV Signals Using a Conformer based Masked Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23554)
*Shuwen Yu,William P Marnane,Geraldine B. Boylan,Gordon Lightbody*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAEConformer框架，将Conformer架构与掩码自编码器(MAE)范式结合，用于从无标注的脑电图(EEG)和心率变异性(HRV)信号中学习大规模表示。通过集成卷积操作与Transformer自注意力机制，有效捕获生理时间序列中的局部时间模式和长程上下文依赖。引入多分辨率短时傅里叶变换(MR-STFT)损失增强重建保真度。在6030小时EEG和4868小时HRV无标注数据上预训练后，在下游任务中表现优异：EEG缺氧缺血性脑病(HIE)分类AUC达97.19%(二分类)和96.56%(四分类)，HRV-HIE分类AUC达82.42%，均超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EEG和HRV的HIE严重程度分类方法通常依赖大规模标注数据，而医学数据标注成本高昂。因此需要一种能够从大量无标注生理信号中学习通用、可迁移表示的自监督学习方法，以提升下游任务的性能和数据效率。

Method: 提出MAEConformer框架，核心包括：(1) 采用Conformer架构融合卷积模块和Transformer自注意力，同时捕获局部时间模式和长程依赖；(2) 采用掩码自编码器(MAE)范式进行自监督预训练，随机掩码部分输入序列并重建；(3) 引入多分辨率短时傅里叶变换(MR-STFT)损失作为辅助重建目标，联合学习多尺度时频特征；(4) 分别对EEG(6030h)和HRV(4868h)无标注数据进行模态特定预训练，再迁移到下游HIE分类任务。

Result: 在EEG-HIE分类任务上，MAE-EEG模型在二分类任务中达到97.19% AUC，四分类任务中达到96.56% AUC，均超越多种有监督和自监督基线方法。在HRV-HIE分类任务上，MAE-HRV达到82.42% AUC，同样优于自监督Transformer和全监督卷积基线。实验表明学习到的表示具有良好的迁移性和数据效率。

Conclusion: MAEConformer框架能够有效从无标注的EEG和HRV生理信号中学习鲁棒且可迁移的表示，在HIE严重程度分类任务中显著超越现有方法，验证了将Conformer与MAE自监督范式结合在多模态生理信号学习中的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the MAEConformer, a novel self-supervised learning framework that combines the Conformer architecture with the Masked Autoencoder (MAE) paradigm for large-scale representation learning from unlabelled electroencephalography (EEG) and heart rate variability (HRV) signals. By integrating convolutional operations with Transformer-based self-attention, MAEConformer effectively captures both local temporal patterns and long-range contextual dependencies in physiological time series. To enhance reconstruction fidelity and representation quality, a multi-resolution short-time Fourier transform (MR-STFT) loss is incorporated alongside the reconstruction objective, enabling the model to jointly learn temporal and spectral characteristics across multiple scales. Modality-specific EEG and HRV MAEConformer models were pretrained on 6,030h and 4,868h of unlabelled recordings, respectively, and subsequently transferred to expert-annotated downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned representations provide strong transferability and data efficiency. In EEG-based hypoxic ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) severity classification, the pretrained MAE-EEG model achieved test AUCs of 97.19% and 96.56% for binary and four-class classification tasks, respectively, outperforming a range of state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines. On the HRV-based HIE severity classification task, MAE-HRV achieved a test AUC of 82.42%, surpassing both self-supervised Transformer-based and supervised convolutional baselines. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of MAEConformer for learning robust and transferable representations across multiple physiological modalities.

</details>


### [173] [Random Forest-Based Prediction of Bone Volume Fraction and Fracture Position from S-Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23563)
*Jianhe Li,Jinsui Meng,Yida Zhao,Zihe Wang,Liaoran Sun,Tao Shan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型，用于预测骨体积分数(BVF)和骨折位置。设计并制作了九天线微波扫描系统采集数据，开发了仿骨体模并通过实验验证了方法的有效性。合成和实验结果均证明了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在骨体积分数和骨折位置的准确预测方面存在不足，需要一种非侵入性、有效的预测方法。

Method: 构建基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型；设计九天线微波扫描系统；开发仿骨体模进行实验验证。

Result: 合成和实验结果表明该方法能有效预测骨体积分数和骨折位置。

Conclusion: 基于多通道S参数的随机森林模型是一种有效的骨体积分数和骨折位置预测方法，具有潜在的临床应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method for predicting bone volume fraction (BVF) and fracture position by constructing a random forest model based on multichannel S-parameters. A nine-antenna microwave scanning system is designed and fabricated to acquire the multichannel S-parameter data. Bone-mimicking phantoms are developed, and corresponding experiments are conducted to validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Both synthetic and experimental results demonstrate the validity of the method.

</details>


### [174] [MS-GPT: Rethinking MS/MS De Novo Structure Elucidation as Spectrum-Induced Posterior Querying of a Molecule-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23607)
*Xin Zhao,Yumin Liu,Zhuo Li,Weichu Zheng,Feng Zhu,Xiaokang Yang,Yaohui Jin,Yanyan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-GPT通过将指纹介导的从头结构解析转化为条件分子语言模型的光谱诱导后验查询，引入主动位点密度校准和LoRA适配器，在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym上取得了新的最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从头解析方法存在训练-推理不匹配：解码器在训练时使用从分子计算得到的oracle指纹，但在推理时接收到的是噪声光谱诱导的、通常被阈值化压缩的指纹后验。这导致模型无法有效处理指纹不确定性。

Method: MS-GPT将条件分子语言模型与指纹和分子式结合；通过主动位点密度校准将光谱诱导后验转化为靠近oracle指纹流形的一组指纹查询；对采样候选进行池化和基于生成频率共识的排序；使用轻量级LoRA适配器缓解域特定的后验偏差，同时保留预训练的分子先验。

Result: 在NPLIB1上Top-1/Top-10精确匹配准确率达29.8%/41.1%，在MassSpecGym上达23.9%/28.7%，均创下新最先进水平。候选池扩展表明高效自回归分子生成能以少量额外推理成本持续提升召回率。

Conclusion: MS-GPT通过将光谱诱导后验转化为一组指纹查询而非单一阈值化指纹，有效解决了训练-推理不匹配问题，证明了指纹不确定性建模对从头结构解析的重要性。

Abstract: Molecular structure elucidation from tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) is a central inverse problem in analytical chemistry. Most existing approaches to MS/MS identification remain tied to reference libraries or predefined candidate sets, whereas de novo methods aim to generate structures directly from spectra. A common de novo route predicts a molecular fingerprint from the spectrum and then decodes structures from it, enabling decoder pretraining on large molecule-only corpora. However, this paradigm creates a training-inference mismatch: the decoder is trained on oracle fingerprints computed from molecules, but at inference it is queried with a noisy spectrum-induced fingerprint posterior that is typically collapsed to a single thresholded fingerprint. We introduce MS-GPT, which recasts fingerprint-mediated de novo elucidation as spectrum-induced posterior querying of a conditional molecule-language model. MS-GPT conditions a molecule-language model on fingerprints and formulas, then converts the spectrum-induced posterior into a band of fingerprint queries near the oracle-fingerprint manifold through active-bit density calibration. Candidates sampled across this band are pooled and ranked by generation-frequency consensus. A lightweight LoRA adapter further mitigates domain-specific posterior bias while preserving the pretrained molecular prior. On NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym, MS-GPT sets a new state of the art, reaching Top-1/Top-10 exact-match accuracy of 29.8\%/41.1\% and 23.9\%/28.7\%, respectively. Candidate-pool scaling shows that efficient autoregressive molecular generation continues to improve recall with a little additional inference cost. The source code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/VIKI623/MS-GPT.

</details>


### [175] [Restoration Flow Matching-Based Channel Refinement and Equalization Correction for MIMO Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23615)
*Wenkai Liu,Nan Ma,Jianqiao Chen,Xiaodong Xu,Meixia Tao,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MIMO语义通信中非理想CSI和均衡失配导致语义重建质量下降的问题，本文提出统一恢复流匹配（RFM）框架，通过信道RFM模块精化信道估计、语义RFM模块校正后均衡潜空间的残差失真，并采用双锚点扰动训练策略提升鲁棒性，在更少采样步数下显著改善了信道估计与语义重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO语义通信中，非理想的信道状态信息（CSI）和均衡失配会严重劣化语义重建质量，现有方法对此问题缺乏有效的统一解决方案。

Method: 提出统一恢复流匹配（RFM）框架，包含两个核心模块：信道RFM（CRFM）用于精化粗信道估计；语义RFM（SRFM）用于校正后均衡潜空间的残差失真。将信道估计和均衡两个级联逆问题统一为条件恢复任务，通过学习条件速度场引导扰动分布向目标分布演化。提出双锚点扰动训练策略，联合学习近流形精化和大误差校正，推理时通过几步确定性ODE求解器实现。

Result: 在MIMO信道和视觉语义传输任务上的大量实验表明，所提方案在信道估计和语义重建质量的关键指标上均有显著提升，且相比基于扩散的生成基线方法所需采样步数更少。

Conclusion: 统一RFM框架能有效解决MIMO语义通信中CSI不完美和均衡失配带来的性能退化问题，在提升重建质量的同时降低了推理开销，为鲁棒语义通信提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: In multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) semantic communication, imperfect channel state information (CSI) and equalization mismatch can seriously degrade semantic reconstruction quality. To address this issue, we propose a unified restoration flow matching (RFM)-based framework for channel refinement and equalization correction. Specifically, the channel RFM (CRFM) module is developed to refine the coarse channel, thereby improving channel estimation accuracy. Based on the refined channel, the developed semantic RFM (SRFM) module is employed to correct the residual distortions in the post-equalization latent space. The key idea is to formulate the two cascaded inverse problems of channel estimation and equalization as the unified conditional restoration task, in which the learned conditional velocity field guides the perturbed distribution towards the target distribution. To enhance the robustness of these two modules under various distortion conditions, we develop a dual-anchor perturbation training strategy that jointly learns near-manifold refinement and large-error correction, and implement inference through a few-step deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE) solver. Extensive experiments on MIMO channels and visual semantic transmission tasks demonstrate that the proposed scheme improves key metrics for channel estimation and semantic reconstruction quality. Moreover, compared with representative diffusion-based generative baselines, the proposed method requires fewer sampling steps.

</details>


### [176] [Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA):TailoredAttentionMattersfor Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23634)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分伊辛注意力（VIA），通过在softmax归一化中引入相互作用的伊辛模型，利用变分平均场推断学习可成对耦合的注意力模式，将注意力从孤立项的排序重新定义为交互实体的集体状态。在逆合成反应中心预测任务上，VIA显著优于标准softmax注意力，证明了针对科学问题定制化注意力机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制研究在工业长上下文需求驱动下集中于稀疏性和效率，但softmax的独立性假设持续存在。对于不受长token约束的科学任务，更丰富的结构化耦合往往是必要的，因此需要定制化的注意力机制。

Method: 提出Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA)，在softmax归一化基础上增强伊辛模型交互；通过变分平均场推断学习可学习的成对耦合，使注意力从孤立项的排序变为交互实体的集体状态。

Result: 在逆合成反应中心预测任务上，VIA在各种模型变体和机制分析中一致且显著优于标准softmax注意力。

Conclusion: 对于科学问题，最优解并非通用效率，而是与内在领域结构对齐的定制化注意力。本文为该范式提供了理论扎实且实验验证的实例化。

Abstract: Attention enables context modeling via query-key scoring with softmax normalization. Driven by industrial long-context demands, mainstream research has converged toward sparsity and efficiency--yet softmax's independence assumption persists. For scientific tasks unburdened by long-token constraints, however, richer structured coupling may often be essential, making tailored attention both viable and more appropriate. To this end, we propose Variational-Ising-Attention (VIA), which augments softmax normalization with an interacting Ising model; attention patterns emerge from learnable pairwise couplings via variational mean-field inference, redefining attention from a ranking over isolated items to a collective state over interacting entities. We instantiate VIA on retrosynthesis reaction center prediction, a task inherently governed by cooperative bond-breaking constraints. Comprehensive experiments across model variants, coupled with mechanistic analyses, demonstrate that VIA consistently and substantially outperforms standard softmax attention. More broadly, our findings suggest that for scientific problems, the optimal solution is not general-purpose efficiency, but appropriately tailored attention aligned with intrinsic domain structure. This work provides a theoretically grounded and empirically validated instantiation of this paradigm.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C-VCE 提出了一种将分类器直接嵌入扩散模型概念瓶颈层的方法，用于生成视觉反事实解释，无需依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器，在保持图像真实性的同时实现语义级编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，这些分类器必须在噪声图像上可靠工作，导致系统脆弱且难以部署。作者希望构建一个将分类器直接集成在生成模型内部的反事实解释框架，从而避免对外部鲁棒分类器的依赖。

Method: 1) 在扩散模型中引入概念瓶颈层（concept bottleneck layer），将分类器内置于生成模型中；2) 用户可在采样过程中切换语义概念，模型仅调整相关区域，保留其余部分并尊重特征相关性；3) 加入概率正则化项以平衡"改变预测"与"保持与原图接近"；4) 使用基于梯度的掩码将修改限制在最相关的区域。

Result: 在 CelebA 等基准上，C-VCE 达到或提升了翻转率（flip rates），同时生成的对抗样本在视觉上更接近输入图像，畸变更少，优于依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器的基线方法。

Conclusion: C-VCE 为视觉系统提供了一种实用的"假设分析"工具，用户无需信任额外的噪声鲁棒分类器即可获得具体的反事实图像。更广泛地，暴露并控制内部概念层是让强大生成模型更易理解和更安全使用的有前景方向。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [178] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SeT-Diff，首个基于扩散模型的计算节点遥测基础模型，通过语义条件生成实现零样本泛化，在真实超算数据集上重建MAE仅0.0470，支持数据插补、预测和虚拟感知等多种任务，可作为HPC系统的数据驱动数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC遥测机器学习方法依赖静态固定传感器变量子集，当目标任务变化或传感器指标变更时模型失效，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力，难以构建准确且灵活的数字孪生。

Method: 基于扩散模型的生成式架构，将生成过程条件化于每个传感器的语义描述上，使系统动力学与数据集结构解耦，实现跨任务、跨传感器的零样本泛化。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上，重建任务MAE为0.0470；展现零样本置乱稳定性，传感器打乱后精度几乎无下降；单一预训练模型在数据插补、预测和虚拟感知任务中均有效，热推断MAE达0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff是首个面向计算节点遥测的基础模型，通过语义条件扩散范式实现灵活的数字孪生，有效解决了传统方法因任务或传感器变化而失效的根本问题。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [179] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFoldAgent，一种用于5残基四面体晶格折叠的闭环多智能体框架。该框架通过设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项、VQE量子-经典流水线优化哈密顿量、反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代优化惩罚系数。在QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，在未见序列上结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，证明迭代智能体控制可系统改善量子环境下的蛋白质折叠优化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于晶格的混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测工作流中，哈密顿量惩罚权重通常手动固定且仅评估极短的片段，缺乏系统化的惩罚系数优化方法，导致优化效果受限。

Method: 提出QFoldAgent闭环多智能体框架，包含：(1) 设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项；(2) 基于VQE的量子-经典流水线在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量；(3) 反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代优化惩罚系数。仅在评估阶段使用RMSD等真实指标。

Result: 在QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，最难目标改进最大。在未见序列上结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%初始无效案例被恢复，最强控制器在87%序列上改善第三轮能量，同时维持96% Ramachandran偏好几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制可系统改善5残基量子设置下的优化行为并减少失败案例，为混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测中的惩罚系数自动化优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [180] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在基准测试中常取得高准确率，但面对语义等价的改写时，其答案表现出显著不稳定性：单次提示的正确性无法反映可靠性，实例级别的答案翻转率可达23%以上。研究表明模型实际拥有潜在知识但检索不一致，可通过自改写策略部分恢复并提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注单次提示下的准确率，忽略了模型在不同但等价的表述下是否能够稳定输出正确回答，因此需要系统研究答案对措辞的依赖程度。

Method: 在四个基准和13个模型上，构建语义等价的改写版本，比较模型在原始问题与改写问题上的答案一致性，并设计自改写策略来恢复潜在知识。

Result: 整体准确率变化不大，但实例级别答案翻转率超过23%；许多问题在不同改写下交替正确与错误回答；模型对至少一个改写版本能给出正确答案。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标掩盖了实质性的不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才能更清晰反映LLM的可靠性，简单的自改写策略可部分缓解该问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [181] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLens诊断智能体，一个五阶段流水线，基于小规模医学推理模型（7B参数）和RAG，在诊断推理中通过流程约束而非模型规模实现高性能。在915例DiagnosisArena基准上达到60.14%的top-1准确率，比同一模型无智能体流程时提升36个百分点，成本仅$0.0072/例，优于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿LLM虽然是强大的通用模型，但在医疗诊断这一多阶段推理任务中，单次提示往往产生脆弱的诊断推理结果，缺乏结构化、可审计的推理过程。小模型在标准医学基准上表现良好（88.2%），但在不确定性下的诊断推理中表现不佳（23.99%），说明知识回忆不足以支撑可靠诊断。

Method: 提出DeepLens诊断智能体，一个五阶段流水线：1）结构化临床事实提取（结构化的临床提取）；2）基于RAG的受控知识检索（受规训的检索）；3）约束候选诊断生成（受约束的候选生成）；4）显式证据三角验证（显式证据三角验证）；5）可审计最终决策。核心模型为JSL Medical Small 7B v2医学推理模型。

Result: 在DiagnosisArena（915例）上达到60.14% top-1准确率，优于所有中小规模模型。同一模型无智能体流程仅为23.99%，提升36个百分点。每例成本$0.0072（24K tokens），延迟24秒。比Claude Sonnet 4.5便宜35-45%，且准确率高出9.70pp；比Gemini 3.1 Pro高出9.17pp。流程约束可纠正前沿模型错误，效果超越参数量或API成本优势。

Conclusion: DeepLens验证了在医疗诊断中，流程设计（约束与结构化）比模型规模更为关键。该流水线不仅提升准确率，还产生可检查的中间产物，支持错误定位、可追溯性、可重复性和可审计性，适用于高风险医疗场景。流程约束可以补偿甚至超越模型参数量和成本优势。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [182] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA是一种内存驱动的推理时自适应框架，通过在受限存储下存储监督经验为紧凑的修正方向原型并附加语义锚点，在推理时利用语义和不确定性线索检索，通过门控临时隐藏状态适配实现非破坏性知识复用，无需更新骨干网络、扩展提示或测试时反向传播，有效缓解了小语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在资源受限部署中需要持续学习以适应现实需求，但直接更新有限参数空间会导致灾难性遗忘；现有为大语言模型设计的基于内存的方法依赖充足的存储和强大的上下文推理能力，小语言模型不具备这些条件。

Method: MIITA将监督经验存储为紧凑的修正方向原型并附加语义锚点，推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索，通过门控临时隐藏状态适配应用检索到的方向，无需更新骨干网络、扩展提示或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种有监督持续学习设置下的广泛实验表明，MIITA在固定内存预算下持续提升了最终性能并有效缓解了遗忘。

Conclusion: MIITA通过内存驱动的推理时自适应机制，在存储受限条件下有效实现了小语言模型的持续学习，其设计有局部理论分析支撑，连接了一阶损失降低、不确定性引导检索和方向覆盖等原理。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues.
