### [255] [Risk Governance for Generative AI Mental Health Support: A Multi-Turn Safety Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22692)
*Anabela C. Areias,Catarina Botelho,António Farinhas,Areti Vassilopoulos,Dora Janela,Xin Tong,Nuno M. Guerreiro,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的安全治理架构，结合上下文风险检测、推理验证和协议引导响应生成，用于多轮心理健康对话。在GPT-5-chat和Qwen3.5-27B上测试，风险检测特异性0.85、灵敏度0.92，临床首选升级响应提升25.6-59.2个百分点，同时保持对话融洽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在情感支持场景中被广泛使用，但缺乏安全管理心理健康风险的机制，现有安全方法主要检测风险却很少能在对话风险演变过程中规范模型的响应方式。

Method: 提出模型无关的安全治理架构，包含三个组件：上下文风险检测、基于推理的验证和协议引导的响应生成。使用基于真实心理健康叙事的合成对话进行评测，测试了GPT-5-chat和Qwen3.5-27B两个模型。

Result: 风险检测特异性0.85（95%CI: 0.78-0.91），灵敏度0.92（95%CI: 0.88-0.95）；临床首选升级响应提升25.6-59.2个百分点；性能在不同对话长度下保持稳定，且在闭源和开源模型上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于临床导向的安全治理可以超越风险检测，改善LLM管理心理健康风险的能力，为模型安全部署提供可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for emotional support despite lacking mechanisms to safely govern evolving mental health risk. Existing safety approaches primarily detect risk but rarely shape how models respond as conversational risk unfolds. We developed a model-agnostic safety governance architecture that combines contextual risk detection, reasoning-based verification, and protocol-guided response generation for multi-turn mental health interactions. Synthetic conversations grounded in real-world mental health narratives were used to evaluate the architecture's performance, tested with GPT-5-chat and Qwen3.5-27B, achieving high risk detection performance (specificity: 0.85 (95\%CI: 0.78;0.91), sensitivity: 0.92 (95\%CI: 0.88;0.95)) and increasing clinician-preferred escalation responses by 25.6--59.2pp while preserving rapport and connection. Performance remained stable across conversation length and generalized across both proprietary and open-source models. These findings demonstrate that clinically-grounded safety governance can extend beyond risk detection to improve how LLMs manage evolving mental health risk, providing a scalable framework for safer deployment across models.

</details>


### [256] [Bayesian Repetition Penalty: A Principled Adjacent-Conditional Framework for Reversing Attention Collapse in Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22694)
*Wenjie Fan,Bin Ma,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相邻条件概率的自归一化惩罚比R，通过冻结偏置修正机制解决自回归语言模型中的注意力崩溃问题（即重复token循环），无需修改训练流程即可从崩溃吸引子中恢复模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码启发式方法无法从根本上解决自回归语言模型中反复出现的注意力崩溃（重复token循环）问题，需要一种能够根治此病理现象的框架性方案。

Method: 通过比较token的观测频率与其语料先验，构造相邻条件概率的自归一化惩罚比R；该比值以闭式logit偏移量形式实现，零近似误差；通过指数移动平均将修正量累加到冻结输出层偏置中，与损失梯度隔离。

Result: 在1.5B参数模型上，该机制能将已陷入崩溃吸引子的模型恢复，2-gram重复率从0.073降至接近0，且生成质量得以保持。

Conclusion: 冻结偏置修正机制是一种无需侵入性修改训练流程即可部署的修复手段，能从根源上解决自回归语言模型的注意力崩溃问题。

Abstract: Attention collapse in autoregressive language models -- manifested as repetitive token loops where the model becomes trapped in self-reinforcing attractors -- is a persistent pathology that existing decoding-time heuristics fail to address at its root cause. We present a principled framework that penalises or compensates anomalous confidence arising from collapsed generation patterns, by comparing a token's observed frequency against its corpus prior through an adjacent-conditional probability construction. The resulting self-normalising penalty ratio $R=f(m,n,p)/f(np,n,p)$ requires no ad hoc standardisation and admits a closed-form logit offset with zero approximation error. The correction is isolated from the loss gradient and accumulated into a frozen output-layer bias via exponential moving average, enabling deployment as a repair mechanism for models that have already collapsed without requiring intrusive modifications to standard training pipelines. Experimental validation on a 1.5B-parameter model demonstrates that the frozen-bias mechanism can rescue a model already trapped in a collapsed attractor, reducing 2-gram repetition from 0.073 to near 0 while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [257] [PANOPTICON: A PII-Based Assemblage of Naturalistic Output Tokens for Investigating Privacy Leakage Within LLM Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22695)
*Ryan Thornton,Mir Mehedi Ahsan Pritom,Maanak Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PANOPTICON流程和数据集，利用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型生成了包含67,718条含PII的提示词数据集，用于研究LLM隐私风险，特别是Prompt反演攻击，成为首个针对私有语料库PIA研究的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在完成任务时常需插入个人身份信息(PII)，引发隐私担忧。然而伦理限制导致缺乏公开、真实的PII数据集，难以量化隐私风险。

Method: 使用Meta的Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型，基于9,674个公开合成用户资料生成67,718条包含PII的提示词；通过词汇多样性和S-BERT多样性评估数据集的真实性。

Result: 生成了包含67,718条提示词的数据集，源自9,674个合成用户资料，并通过词汇多样性和S-BERT多样性指标验证了数据集的真实感。

Conclusion: PANOPTICON是首个用于研究Prompt反演攻击的基准数据集，为未来LLM隐私研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are capable of generalizing human language for the completion of never-before-seen tasks, leading to widespread deployment. While this automation provides clear utility, completing these tasks often requires the insertion of Personally Identifiable Information (PII), strings of information that uniquely identify some individual, raising privacy concerns. However, ethics has prevented the curation of a public, authentic dataset of PII. Without an appropriate dataset, it is difficult to quantify privacy risks. Thus, we introduce the PANOPTICON pipeline and dataset. The dataset, generated by Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct model, contains 67, 718 prompts, intended for the models context window, containing PII spans derived from 9,674 publicly available synthetic user profiles. We measure lexical diversity and S-BERT diversity of the created dataset to evaluate realism. Finally, we present a case study showcasing the utility of PANOPTICON data for understanding Prompt Inversion Attacks (PIAs). PANOPTICON thus emerges as the first benchmark dataset for studying PIAs over private corpora, providing a foundation for future LLM privacy research.

</details>


### [258] [Test-Time Coverage: Test-Conditioned Data Curation for Deployment-Aware Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22697)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Shizhe Diao,Jialiang Wang,Jingde Chen,Thomas Breuel,Pavlo Molchanov,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TTCov，一种测试时覆盖度数据筛选方法，在训练前利用测试侧信息（而非推理时更新权重）来筛选更匹配部署分布的训练数据。通过构建任务Atlas（原子命题集合）和知识Atlas（K-Atlas，带频率的部署分布表征），TTCov选择预算内训练集使其AP分布逼近部署目标。在自动驾驶任务上，TTCov比基线方法有更好的部署相关覆盖度、K-Atlas匹配度和端到端驾驶性能，并支持城市间扩展适应新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的数据筛选方法多基于训练侧标准评分，而非直接优化与部署测试分布的匹配度，导致训练数据与真实部署场景存在偏差。

Method: TTCov将部署条件化的数据筛选分解为覆盖度（coverage）和分布（distribution）两个维度。覆盖度方面，构建任务Atlas——一个基于LLM的原子命题集合，从开放任务知识种子出发，用未标注部署样本中提取的未匹配AP进行扩展。分布方面，用匹配的部署AP及其频率实例化知识Atlas（K-Atlas），将部署分布作为筛选目标。最后选择预算内的训练集，使其部署AP分布逼近该目标。

Result: 在自动驾驶任务上，TTCov相比基线数据筛选方法，在部署相关覆盖度、K-Atlas匹配度以及下游端到端驾驶性能方面均表现更优，并且通过城市间扩展实现了对新领域的无缝适应。

Conclusion: TTCov通过在训练前利用测试侧信息进行数据级筛选，有效弥合了训练数据与部署分布之间的差距，且不增加推理开销，为数据筛选提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Deployed AI systems are often trained from broad candidate data pools, necessitating data curation towards the deployment test distribution. However, standard data curation methods score training-side criteria rather than directly optimizing deployment match. We introduce TTCov (Test-Time Coverage), a data-level test-conditioned curation method that uses test-side information before training instead of updating model weights at inference. TTCov decomposes deployment-conditioned curation into coverage and distribution. To represent coverage, it builds a task Atlas, a collection of LLM-based atomic propositions (APs) describing deployment-relevant concepts, seeded from open task knowledge and expanded with unmatched APs extracted from unlabeled deployment samples. To represent distribution, it instantiates the matched deployment APs with their frequencies, yielding a Knowledge Atlas (K-Atlas) that operationalizes the deployment distribution as a curation target. TTCov then selects a budgeted training set whose deployment APs distribution approximates this target. We apply TTCov towards autonomous driving (AD), keeping adaptation off the inference path while selecting data with greater deployment-relevant coverage, closer K-Atlas matching, and stronger downstream end-to-end driving performance than data-curation baselines, including seamless adaptability to novel domains via city-to-city expansion.

</details>


### [259] [Similarity All The Way Up: Multilingual Generalization in LLMs Relies on Language-Level Similarity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22699)
*Supantho Rakshit,Adele Goldberg,Henry Conklin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLMs）的多语言泛化能力，发现其潜在表征能够恢复印欧语系的层次结构，并且表征中语言相似性结构的反映程度与XNLI基准上的跨语言推理性能呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在多语言泛化方面表现不佳，尤其对非英语及训练数据中稀有的语言；研究者希望理解为何有些模型泛化能力更强，并从认知科学中寻找理论依据。

Method: 基于认知科学中"相似性驱动的泛化"理论，分析LLMs潜在表征是否捕捉了语言间的层次相似性结构；将模型表征空间中的语言距离与印欧语系树状结构进行对比，并通过XNLI基准评估泛化性能。

Result: LLMs的潜在表征在很大程度上恢复了印欧语系的层次结构，同一子族语言在表征空间中紧密聚类；模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与XNLI上的多语言推理性能呈正相关。

Conclusion: 扩展了经典相似性驱动泛化理论至大规模模型规模，表明能够将相似语言表征得相似的模型，在跨语言迁移中泛化能力更强。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) grow more capable across diverse tasks, their (in)ability to generalize remains difficult to quantify and poorly understood beyond limited domains. In particular, LLMs are known to struggle generalizing multilingually, to languages outside of English, and that are poorly attested in their training data. To understand why this may be, and what enables some models to perform better than others, we turn to a long history of work across the cognitive sciences, arguing that successful generalization derives from appropriate representations in similarity space. We look at how well LLMs' representations capture the hierarchical similarity structure between distinct languages. Strikingly, we show LLMs' latent representations largely recover the hierarchical structure of the Indo-European language family tree -- grouping languages that are members of the same subfamily closely together in representation space. Furthermore, we show that the degree to which models reflect the similarity structure of languages correlates with their performance on XNLI, a multilingual natural language inference benchmark. This extends classic work on similarity-driven generalization at scale, showing how models that represent similar languages similarly generalize better from one language to another.

</details>


### [260] [RoleMix: Unifying Sequential and Non-Sequential Features via Semantic Tokenization for Post-Click Conversion Rate Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22700)
*Wenan Wang,Qin Zhao,Zhixiang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoleMix提出了一种统一交互架构，通过共享的角色保留令牌接口融合稀疏多域特征与长行为序列，在KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge上以83.648%在线AUC超越工业基线1.953%，证明了在令牌接口层面保留字段语义的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐中的点击后转化率(PCVR)预测面临稀疏无序的多域特征与长行为历史之间的结构性不匹配问题，现有模型通常通过分离通路处理这些信号并进行晚期融合，削弱了语义角色并限制了跨信号的精细化交互。

Method: RoleMix通过统一的角色保留令牌接口表示序列与非序列证据：将非序列字段转换为保留用户、物品、成对、密集、上下文和交叉特征角色的显式语义令牌；通过两阶段分层窗口注意力将长行为历史压缩为物品感知和上下文感知的序列查询令牌；全局令牌、语义令牌和序列查询令牌通过堆叠的UniMixing-Lite模块联合精炼进行PCVR预测。

Result: 在KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge大规模数据集上，RoleMix实现了83.648%的在线AUC，比官方工业基线高出1.953%。消融实验表明，语义令牌化产生了最大的独立增益。

Conclusion: 大规模PCVR建模的关键原则：在令牌接口层面保留字段语义与扩展交互骨干网络同等重要。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is central to industrial recommendation, but remains challenged by the structural mismatch between sparse, unordered multi-field features and long, domain-specific behavior histories. Existing models often process these signals through separate pathways and fuse them late, weakening semantic roles and limiting cross-signal refinement. We propose RoleMix, a unified interaction architecture that represents sequential and non-sequential evidence through a shared, role-preserving token interface. Non-sequential fields are converted into explicit semantic tokens that preserve user, item, pairwise, dense, contextual, and cross-feature roles, while long behavior domains are compressed into item- and context-aware sequence-query tokens through two-stage hierarchical window attention. The resulting global, semantic, and sequence-query tokens are jointly refined by stacked UniMixing-Lite blocks for PCVR prediction. On the large-scale KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, RoleMix achieves 83.648% online AUC, outperforming the official industrial baseline by 1.953%. Ablation studies show that semantic tokenization yields the largest isolated gain, highlighting a key principle for large-scale PCVR modeling: preserving field semantics at the token-interface level is as important as scaling the interaction backbone.

</details>


### [261] [MPR-CiteG: Enhancing RAG with Multi-Portfolio Retrieval and Citation-Grounded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22706)
*Hyewon Lee,Minkyung Song,Junghyun Oh,Seunghoon Han,Sungsu Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MPR-CiteG框架，通过多组合检索器（MPR）和引文生成模块（CiteG）解决生成式AI中的检索低效和缺乏来源验证问题，在ScienceON AI Challenge中取得第二名，有效提升了LLM的可靠性和事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI存在两个核心问题：检索效率低下以及生成输出缺乏可验证的来源引用，导致模型容易出现幻觉现象，亟需一种能够同时解决检索和归因可信度的系统。

Method: 提出双组件系统MPR-CiteG：1）多组合检索器（MPR）高效检索多样且相关的信息；2）引文生成模块（CiteG）确保每个生成输出与来源事实一致并明确标注引用。

Result: 在ScienceON AI Challenge数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了方法的有效性和可靠性，最终获得赛事第二名。

Conclusion: MPR-CiteG是构建更可信、更准确的LLM的重要一步，能使模型不仅生成信息，还能将响应基于可靠证据，有效缓解幻觉问题。

Abstract: This paper presents the MPR-CiteG framework, which achieved second place in the ScienceON AI Challenge by addressing two fundamental challenges in generative AI: inefficient retrieval and the absence of source verification. We propose a dual-component system, termed MPR-CiteG, in which the Multi-Portfolio Retriever (MPR) efficiently retrieves diverse and relevant information, while the Citation-Grounded Generation (CiteG) module ensures that every generated output remains factually consistent and explicitly attributed to its source. MPR-CiteG represents a significant step toward building more trustworthy and accurate LLMs that are not only capable of generating information but also of grounding their responses in reliable evidence, thereby mitigating common issues like model hallucination. Extensive experiments on the challenge dataset validate the effectiveness and reliability of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/2noweyh/MPR-citeG.

</details>


### [262] [SEGRA: Structured Experience-Guided Graph Reasoning Agent for Gremlin Based Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22713)
*Saiyue Lyu,Mariam Dundua,Vishaal Kapoor,Sarthak Ahuja,Neda Kordjazi,Evren Yortucboylu,Harsh Amin,Rebecca Steinert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SEGRA是一个面向企业IT支持场景的文本转Gremlin问答智能体，通过意图路由、模式引导的查询生成、多轮分解、执行感知验证和课程引导的技能库，在基准测试中相比纯思维链提示提升了7.0倍的评分均值，同时技能库减少了20%的LLM调用和18%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业IT支持知识图谱查询需要掌握Gremlin语言及其图模式、遍历语义、边方向性等专业知识，非专家用户难以直接使用，亟需一种能够将自然语言自动转化为Gremlin查询的方法。

Method: SEGRA包含五个关键组件：意图路由、模式与分类体系引导的查询生成、多轮分解、执行感知验证、以及基于课程引导启动的技能库（复用已验证的查询模式）。

Result: 在企业IT支持基准测试上，SEGRA的评分均值是纯思维链提示的7.0倍；带技能库的SEGRA相比不带技能库的版本减少了20%的LLM调用和18%的成本，同时保持回答质量。

Conclusion: 基于模式引导的智能体设计以及可复用的执行经验能够同时提升企业图问答的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Enterprise IT support knowledge graphs capture rich relationships among cases, users, devices, symptoms, taxonomic categories, root causes, and historical resolutions. Yet querying them in Gremlin requires knowledge of graph schemas, traversal semantics, edge directionality, and property-graph-specific constraints, making them difficult for non-expert operators to use. We introduce SEGRA, an experience-guided agent for enterprise text-to-Gremlin question answering. SEGRA integrates intent routing, schema- and taxonomy-grounded query generation, multi-shot decomposition, execution-aware verification, and a curriculum-bootstrapped skill library that reuses verified query patterns. On an enterprise IT support benchmark, SEGRA achieves a $7.0\times$ higher mean judge score than backbone-only chain-of-thought prompting. Its skill library further reduces LLM calls by $20\%$ and dollar cost by $18\%$ relative to SEGRA without skills, while preserving answer quality. These results show that schema-grounded agent design and reusable execution experience improve both accuracy and efficiency for enterprise graph QA.

</details>


### [263] [Spatial Reasoning in LLM Game Agents: Impact of Causal Context and Multi-Step Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22732)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Ronja Fuchs,Robin Schmöcker,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的游戏代理在复杂任务中的空间推理局限性，通过因果提示增强和多步规划方法提升胜率。使用Qwen3模型系列在自定义GVGAI基准上实验，发现大模型启用思考模式能更准确定位，因果提示可提升胜率，多步规划在推理深度和执行速度间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based游戏代理在复杂任务中表现不佳，本文旨在探究这些失败是否与有限的空间推理能力相关，并评估因果提示增强和多步规划是否能提升胜率同时控制响应延迟。

Method: 使用开源Qwen3模型系列，在不同模型规模、推理模式和规划视野下进行实验。设计了包含三个自定义游戏和五个难度级别的GVGAI基准测试，采用定位实验（测试坐标感知能力）和游戏成功率两种评估范式。

Result: 大模型启用思考模式后位置识别更准确，但小模型在坐标匹配上表现有限。胜率随游戏关卡和布局复杂度增加而下降。因果上下文提示能提升代理成功率（尤其对大模型）。启用思考模式和更长规划视野显著提升性能，多步规划进一步降低了平均每步响应时间。

Conclusion: 因果提示增强和多步规划是提升LLM游戏代理空间推理和任务完成能力的有效手段，能在推理深度和执行速度之间实现实用平衡。

Abstract: LLM-based game agents often perform poorly on more complex tasks. This work examines whether these failures are linked to limited spatial reasoning and evaluates whether causal prompt augmentation and multi-step planning can improve win-rates while managing response latency. Using the open-source Qwen3 model family, we conduct experiments across varying model scales, reasoning modes, and planning horizons. We further introduce a focused GVGAI benchmark consisting of three custom games with five difficulty levels to isolate spatial navigation. The evaluation follows two paradigms: an initial ``positioning experiment'' to test an agent's ability to find its exact coordinates, and a study of game-play success. Our results show that while larger models with an enabled thinking mode identify their positions more accurately, overall performance in coordinate matching remains limited for smaller models. Win rates decrease as game levels and layout complexity increase, validating the benchmark's difficulty scaling. Integrating causal context into the prompts tends to improve the agents' success rates, particularly for bigger models. While enabling thinking mode and longer planning horizons significantly improve performance, multi-step planning further reduces mean per-step response times, offering a practical trade-off between reasoning depth and execution speed.

</details>


### [264] [Commitment To Cooperation With Self-Negotiated Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22750)
*Tim Wyse,Kaitlin Bustos,Yulia Volkova,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于合约的多智能体协作机制，通过让LLM智能体在空间-时间博弈中自主协商合约，实现可信承诺并改善合作效果。实验表明，自协商合约能比常规交易更有效地提升合作收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在多智能体环境中面临合作困境：合作成本即时发生而收益延迟兑现，导致背叛激励。受人类社会法律契约制度启发，研究如何通过合约实现可信承诺以促进AI智能体间的合作。

Method: 在名为\CT的空间-时间博弈中，使用基于LLM的智能体进行合约协商实验。合约表示形式涵盖从编译为代码的形式合约到需要重新解释的自然合约。使用不同规模和提供商的多种LLM骨干网络评估智能体性能。

Result: 自协商合约能够显著提升合作收益，效果优于常规交易机制。不同LLM骨干网络和合约表示形式下均观察到这一趋势。

Conclusion: 合约机制是促进AI多智能体系统合作的有效手段，通过提供可观察的协议表示和执行条款实现可信承诺，为解决智能体合作困境提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As AI agents operate with increasing autonomy in a multi-agent world, they will need to learn to cooperate with other agents and with humans to generate mutual benefits. However, cooperation is a challenge because the costs of cooperation are often incurred early on, but the benefits are only realized later, creating an incentive to defect. How can AI agents cooperate with commitment? Here, we draw on inspiration from legal institutions and contracting that human societies have used to solve principal-agent problems of this kind. Contracts provide observable representations of agreements that enable credible commitments through the enforcement of terms. We study the role of contract-based cooperation using LLM-based agents in \CT, a spatial-temporal game that combines bargaining with navigation towards a goal. We study a suite of contract representations that range from formal contracts that compile to code to natural contracts that require reinterpretation. We evaluate agents with a range of LLM backbones using different sizes and providers. We find that self-negotiated contracts can improve cooperative outcomes beyond what is possible with regular trading.

</details>


### [265] [Disentangling Multi-View Scanning in Mamba for Network Traffic Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22829)
*Xinglin Lian,Chengtai Cao,Ting Zhong,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DisenMamba提出了一种解耦多视图Mamba框架，通过两阶段"解耦-融合"过程，在融合前显式分离视图不变和视图特定信息，解决了多视图Mamba扫描中冗余累积导致的表示同质化问题，提升了网络流量异常检测的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mamba多视图扫描机制存在结构缺陷：不同扫描分支捕获了大量视图不变信息，在多视图融合时被重复放大，而视图特定信息被稀释甚至抑制，导致表示同质化和多视图退化，影响NTAD的检测精度。

Method: 提出DisenMamba框架，将多视图扫描重构为两阶段"解耦-融合"过程：先显式分离视图不变和视图特定成分，再进行融合，从而避免不变信息累积并保留互补的多视图线索。

Result: 大量实验证明了DisenMamba的有效性，建立了新的解耦多视图Mamba范式。

Conclusion: DisenMamba通过解耦多视图表示有效解决了冗余累积问题，为网络流量异常检测提供了更具判别力的表示，代码已开源。

Abstract: Network Traffic Anomaly Detection (NTAD) is a critical task in cybersecurity, yet timely and accurate anomaly detection remains challenging. Mamba has emerged as a particularly promising backbone for NTAD due to its linear-time complexity for long-sequence modeling. It further incorporates a dedicated multi-view scanning mechanism to enhance detection precision through complementary contextual cues. However, we identify a previously overlooked structural deficiency in multi-view Mamba scanning for NTAD: redundancy accumulation. Specifically, distinct scanning branches capture substantial view-invariant information, which is repeatedly amplified during multi-view fusion; conversely, view-specific information is diluted or even suppressed, leading to representation homogenization and multi-view degradation. To address this problem, we propose DisenMamba, a novel disentangled multi-view Mamba framework. DisenMamba reformulates multi-view scanning as a two-stage disentangle-then-fuse process that explicitly separates view-invariant and view-specific components prior to fusion. This design prevents the invariant information accumulation while preserving complementary multi-view cues, yielding more discriminative representations for subtle traffic anomalies. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DisenMamba, establishing a new disentangled multi-view Mamba paradigm. Code is available at https://github.com/ikun0124/DisenMamba.

</details>


### [266] [Coordinated Networking for On-Device Agent-Augmented Real-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22854)
*Goodsol Lee,Juheon Yi,Jinglu Wang,Haowen Xu,Saewoong Bahk,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HFS框架，通过应用层统一调度器联合控制视频流和智能体上下文流的发送速率，在设备端智能体增强的实时通信中实现了高视频质量和低智能体响应延迟，相比基线方法视频质量提升1.5倍，智能体响应时间降低31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云端智能体存在隐私风险和服务器成本不可扩展的问题，而设备端智能体增强的实时通信虽然可以解决这些问题，但面临人类视频流和智能体上下文流之间的并发流量竞争，亟需一种能够同时保证高视频质量和低智能体延迟的方案。

Method: 提出HFS框架，采用应用引导的多流传输方法，通过统一的应用层编排器（orchestrator）根据异构的应用需求联合控制视频流和智能体上下文流的发送速率。

Result: 基于WebRTC和llama.cpp的原型实验表明，HFS相比基线方法视频质量提升1.5倍，智能体响应时间降低31%。

Conclusion: HFS框架有效解决了设备端智能体增强RTC场景中多流并发竞争的问题，为隐私保护且可扩展的实时协同智能体应用提供了可行的网络传输方案。

Abstract: AI agents are enabling a new paradigm of agent-augmented real-time communication (RTC), where humans focus on high-level collaboration, while agents autonomously retrieve, analyze, and generate information in real time to support their interactions. These apps enable new experiences across various domains: for example, when corporate employees co-author a legal document, their agents can discuss and draft on their behalf, sparing them the burden of manually reviewing each other's work. As existing cloud-based agents suffer from privacy risks and unscalable server costs, on-device agent-augmented RTC offers a promising alternative. However, this on-device paradigm introduces a new networking challenge: contention between concurrent traffic flows generated by humans (for live video streaming) and agents (for sending context files for analysis). We design HFS, a framework to ensure both high live video quality and low agent response latency in agent-augmented RTC apps. We achieve the goal through an app-guided multi-flow transport approach, where a unified app-layer orchestrator jointly controls the sending rates of live video and agent context flows based on their heterogeneous app requirements. Our prototype built atop WebRTC and llama.cpp demonstrates that HAFS outperforms baselines, achieving 1.5x higher video quality while reducing agent response time by 31%.

</details>


### [267] [What Can Be Enforced? A Theory of Certified Runtime Safety for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22868)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了运行时护栏在不可逆工具调用前的三个核心问题：策略状态表示性（确定性门控的强制能力与可判定性）、基于固定外生法则的法官统计校准（Neyman-Pearson最优边界与共形校准的有限样本保证），以及门控改变未来提议后闭环动态的识别难题。实验通过静态诊断、控制模型枚举、表示重写和闭环重跑来区分这些维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时护栏的保证取决于策略状态的可表示性、法官的观测能力以及干预是否改变未来行为，现有工作缺乏对这三方面分离的严格刻画。

Method: 采用三部分分离分析：1) 确定性门控的强制能力与寄存器模型的关系，以及可判定性复杂度（两个递减计数器下不可判定，可分离单调片段在PSPACE中）；2) 固定外生法则下Neyman-Pearson给出误拦截/漏放精确边界，共形校准提供有限样本边际保证；3) 闭环场景下静态分数和未门控轨迹无法识别闭环前沿，需指定有限控制模型求解占据程序。

Result: 确定性门控恰好强制寄存器模型识别的非空安全策略的好前缀；策略非平凡性在双递减计数器下不可判定，但可分离单调片段在PSPACE中。Neyman-Pearson给出精确的误拦截/漏放前沿，共形校准提供有限样本边际证书。闭环场景下静态分数和未门控轨迹无法识别闭环前沿，需要指定有限控制模型。有界表示攻击引入了鲁棒性裕度，良性校准本身无法迁移。

Conclusion: 运行时护栏的保证依赖于策略表示、法官观测和闭环动态三者的分离分析；简单的校准方法在存在表示攻击或闭环动态时无法直接迁移，需要更系统的建模和验证。

Abstract: Runtime guardrails act before irreversible tool calls, but their guarantees depend on what policy state is representable, what a judge observes, and whether intervention changes future behavior. We separate three questions. First, relative to fixed oracle predicates, a deterministic gate enforces exactly the nonempty safety policies whose good prefixes its register model recognizes; policy nontriviality is undecidable with two decrementable counters but in PSPACE for a separable monotone fragment. Second, under a fixed exogenous law, Neyman-Pearson gives the exact false-block/miss frontier and conformal calibration gives a finite-sample marginal certificate, possibly via block-all. Third, once blocking changes future proposals, static scores and ungated trajectories need not identify the closed-loop frontier; a specified finite controlled model instead yields an occupancy program. Bounded representation attacks add a robustness margin, so benign calibration alone does not transfer. Experiments target these distinctions through static diagnostics, controlled-model enumeration, representation rewrites, and paired closed-loop reruns.

</details>


### [268] [Physical AI Governance: From Theory to Practice Across Life Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22877)
*Wang Yang,Shaobo Wang,Hongxuan Liu,Xiaoran Cai,Yunyu He,Jingzong Zhou,Mengzhong Ma,Yi Yu,Rohit Sharma,Jingjing Fu,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统调研了Physical AI的治理问题，从科学和操作两个维度出发，提出了一套统一的治理框架，并基于五阶段生命周期（研究、设计、数据、模型开发、部署）展示了如何在每个阶段落实治理实践，旨在构建安全、可信且符合社会价值的Physical AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI（物理人工智能）正在从传统的屏幕交互AI扩展到能够感知、交互并作用于物理世界的具身系统，其面临实时安全约束、动态环境交互及与人类共存等新挑战，而现有的AI治理框架未能明确覆盖这些治理问题。

Method: 本文采用综合性调研方法，首先梳理并整合现有治理原则，构建统一治理框架；在此基础上提出一个包含研究、设计、数据、模型开发、部署五个阶段的Physical AI生命周期，并针对每个阶段展示具体的治理操作化实践。

Result: 形成了一套针对Physical AI的统一治理框架，以及覆盖全生命周期的可操作治理实施方案，将治理原则与工程流程相连接。

Conclusion: 通过将治理原则与工程工作流对接，本文为研究人员、开发者和政策制定者提供了结构化参考，助力构建安全、可信且与社会价值对齐的Physical AI系统。

Abstract: With the emergence of Physical AI, artificial intelligence is extending beyond screen-based applications to embodied systems that perceive, interact with, and act in the physical world. Unlike traditional AI, Physical AI operates under real-time safety constraints, continuously interacts with dynamic environments, and coexists with humans, introducing governance challenges that existing AI governance frameworks do not explicitly address. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of Physical AI governance from both scientific and operational perspectives. We synthesize existing governance principles and organize them into a unified governance framework tailored to physical AI systems. Building on this foundation, we propose a five-stage Physical AI lifecycle comprising research, design, data, model development, and deployment, and demonstrate how governance can be operationalized across each stage through concrete implementation practices. By connecting governance principles with engineering workflows, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers, developers, and policymakers to build Physical AI systems that are safe, trustworthy, and aligned with societal values.

</details>


### [269] [How Well Can AI Generate Backlogs from App Mockups?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22902)
*Andrea Lezcano Airaldi,Lourdes Romera,Walid Maalej*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉应用原型的多模态方法，用于辅助生成冲刺待办事项列表。研究评估了三种提示策略（零样本基线、组合思维链、角色驱动提示）在GPT-4o上的表现，涉及7个应用开发项目。结果表明组合思维链策略在史诗和用户故事上F1达52-66%，添加架构上下文可提升后端任务精度最高35%。开发者访谈显示26%的误报仍有价值，为此提出"修正召回率"新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建冲刺待办事项列表需要大量人工投入，史诗、用户故事和任务等条目容易遗漏或规格不一致。现有方法缺乏对早期项目阶段中可视化原型的有效利用，亟需自动化辅助手段。

Method: 提出多模态方法，利用视觉应用原型生成待办事项列表。评估GPT-4o上的三种提示策略：零样本基线、组合思维链（CCoT）视觉语言推理、角色驱动提示。在两国7个应用开发项目上实验，并结合开发者访谈进行定性评估。

Result: 基线提示偏重召回率而非精确率；CCoT更均衡，史诗和用户故事平均F1达52-66%；任务生成更具挑战性。添加架构上下文后精确率提升最稳定，后端任务精确率提升达35%。开发者访谈发现26%的假阳性仍被认为有用。

Conclusion: 混合提示结合架构上下文可辅助从早期原型生成待办事项列表，但效果因条目类型而异，仍需开发者监督。提出"修正召回率"新指标以结合开发者评估，反映待办事项创建的创造性和开放性。

Abstract: Creating sprint backlogs requires considerable effort, as items such as epics, user stories, and tasks can be missed or inconsistently specified. We propose a multimodal approach to support backlog generation from visual app mockups, an artifact available at early project stages. We evaluate three prompting strategies on GPT-4o: a zero-shot baseline, Compositional Chain-of-Thought (CCoT) for vision-language reasoning, and a persona-driven prompt. We study seven app development projects across two countries and interview developers about the results. Overall, we observed that the baseline prompt favours recall over precision, whereas CCoT is more balanced, achieving average F1 scores of 52-66% for epics and user stories. Tasks were more challenging to generate accurately. Precision gains were most consistent when adding architectural context, particularly for backend tasks (precision gains up to 35%). Interviews with developers revealed that up to 26% of false positives were still considered useful, reflecting the creative and open-ended nature of backlog creation. To capture this, we propose a new measure called Revised Recall, which complements ground-truth evaluation with developer assessments. Our findings suggest that hybrid prompting with architectural context can assist backlog generation from early mockups, though results vary by item type and developer oversight remains necessary.

</details>


### [270] [Agent Team Work Zone: An Automated, Persistent Workspace for Long-Lived Coding Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22917)
*Shouren Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATWZ 是一个基于文件系统的操作层，构建在 Claude Code 的原生 Agent Teams 之上，通过将代理及其工作状态持久化为文件来解决 LLM 编码代理在长期工作流中面临的四大问题：不可恢复的代理团队、压缩导致工作细节丢失、代理"技术债务"累积以及繁重的提示编写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Claude Code 等 LLM 编码代理在长期工作流中存在四个关键缺陷：(1) 代理团队的工作状态在进程终止后不可恢复；(2) 对话压缩导致工作细节模糊；(3) 代理的决策和操作逐渐被锁死在压缩后的旧聊天中，形成"技术债务"；(4) 用户需要反复编写冗长提示来分配任务，效率低下。

Method: ATWZ 将每个代理视为"人类员工"，将其重要工作状态持久化存储在专用目录（称为"工作站"）的文件中，同时配合使用和维护这些文件的技能（skills）、钩子（hooks）和脚本（scripts）。代理团队可以定期备份工作状态，进程结束后可通过单条命令恢复团队。

Result: ATWZ 实现了代理团队工作状态的定期备份与恢复，使代理的知识在压缩后仍可恢复；进程结束后团队可一键恢复；大幅缓解了代理"技术债务"；同时代理"员工"之间可以直接传递文档，显著减少了编写提示的工作量。

Conclusion: ATWZ 通过文件系统级的持久化设计，系统性地解决了 LLM 编码代理在长期、多代理协作场景下的状态丢失、细节退化、债务累积和交互成本高等核心问题，提升了代理工作流的可持续性和可维护性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have significantly improved coding and programming workflows. Claude Code, in particular, is one of the most powerful LLM coding agents and is capable of conducting complex coding tasks. However, several drawbacks can undermine long-term agentic workflows. (1) Irrecoverable agent teams: The Agent Teams feature is powerful, but the working state accumulated by each teammate is lost and cannot be resumed once the process stops, for example, when a terminal is closed. (2) Compaction erodes working detail: Compaction condenses the conversation into a summary, causing an agent's working details to become vague. (3) Agentic "technical debt": Over time, a user's decisions and the agents' operations become trapped in compacted old chats, making the project increasingly difficult to maintain and review. (4) Heavy prompt writing: Assigning or handing off tasks requires users to repeatedly write long prompts to achieve the expected agentic performance. We propose ATWZ (Agent Team Work Zone), a filesystem-based operations layer built around Claude Code's native Agent Teams that addresses these problems. Its central design principle is to treat each agent and teammate as a human employee and preserve their important working state in files stored in a dedicated directory called a "workstation," together with the skills, hooks, and scripts that use and maintain these files. With ATWZ, an agent team can periodically back up its working state, allowing an agent's knowledge to be recovered after compaction. After a process ends, the team can be restored with a single command. These features also substantially mitigate the agentic "technical debt" described above. Moreover, within ATWZ, agent "employees" can send documents to one another, greatly reducing the effort required to write prompts.

</details>


### [271] [SAGE: Safety-First Defense-in-Depth Guardrails for Verified Lifecycle Control of High-Impact Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22926)
*Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE安全优先架构，将灾难性误用视为全生命周期控制问题，并通过形式化验证和实验表明该架构能有效约束有害合规风险，同时提供了可扩展的测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高影响力生成式AI存在灾难性误用风险，当前仅靠提示过滤不足以应对，需要从全生命周期角度进行安全控制。

Method: 提出SAGE安全架构，包含签名发布清单、多样化检测器、鲁棒风险包络、最小风险默认、输出检查、三值监控、审计链、隔离与回滚机制。通过PRISM抽象进行形式化验证，并采用冻结的供应商对称研究设计进行实验评估。

Result: 840次调用获得794个目标响应和46个提供者错误，449次成功判定覆盖375个响应。有害合规估计值较低，变异主要来自良性效用和安全重定向。多重性校正后，Claude/Gemini与GPT-5/5.5的部分对比得到支持。

Conclusion: SAGE架构将安全置于首位，形式化结果建立了安全优先级、保守检测器边界、单调发布门控、防篡改记录和授权隔离。观察到的有害合规范围是保守的协议绑定视图，并非操作辅助的上界。

Abstract: High-impact generative AI makes catastrophic misuse a lifecycle-control problem, not merely a prompt-filtering problem. SAGE is a safety-first, authorization-separated architecture in which credible catastrophic-enablement risk constrains admissibility before utility, latency, or commercial objectives are considered. It combines signed release manifests, diverse detectors, robust risk envelopes, least-risk defaults, output checking, three-valued monitoring, protected audit chains, containment, and rollback. Formal results establish safety priority, conservative detector bounds, monotone release gating, tamper-evident records, and an authorization cut; two PRISM abstractions verify authorization separation and lifecycle invariants under explicit assumptions.
  A frozen, vendor-symmetric study sent 84 cases to each of four GPT, four Claude, and two Gemini snapshots: 840 calls yielded 794 target responses, 46 provider errors, and 449 successful judgments covering 375 responses. Eight snapshots had complete judged domain coverage. Harmful-compliance estimates were low; variation arose mainly from benign utility and safe redirection. Seven multiplicity-adjusted contrasts involving Claude, Gemini, or GPT-5 snapshots and the GPT-5 mini and GPT-5 nano snapshots were supported, while no tested contrast between the Claude or Gemini snapshots and GPT-5 or GPT-5.5 survived correction.
  The observed harmful-compliance range is a conservative, protocol-bound view from one generation per prompt with no tools, retrieval, history, or human adjudication; it is not an upper bound on operational assistance. A preregistered extension specifies how to test a wider best-worst gap using a locked split, repeated sampling, multi-turn and sandboxed-tool conditions, and domain-expert scoring.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [Optimal Investment with Switching Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22536)
*Yu-Jui Huang,Liviu Ignat,Traian A. Pirvu,Reihaneh Vafadar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究重大生活事件（如失业、疾病等）如何通过改变投资者的风险厌恶程度来影响其最优投资策略。通过求解含事件前后风险偏好变化的全非线性HJB方程，获得了最优投资策略的显式形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究表明，重大生活事件会显著且持久地提高个体的风险厌恶水平，但这一行为特征对最优投资决策的影响在理论上尚未被充分建模。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在有限时间水平下，假设重大生活事件独立于金融市场发生，投资者预见其风险厌恶程度可能在事件后永久升高。建立含事件前后风险偏好的HJB方程，发现Fenchel-Legendre变换无法线性化该方程，产生了一个含全非线性项的抛物型方程。通过不动点、紧性和验证论证的组合，证明了该方程存在正凸经典解。最优策略由事件前策略（通过HJB方程解表达）和事件后策略（传统Merton比例）拼接而成。

Result: 证明了全非线性抛物方程存在具有适当增长性的正凸经典解；该解的凸共轭满足HJB方程并与事件前最优值函数一致；得到了拼接形式的最优交易策略。

Conclusion: 本文为事件触发的风险偏好变化下的最优投资问题提供了严格的理论框架和求解方法，拓展了传统Merton模型对实际投资者行为的解释能力。

Abstract: Major life events can significantly increase individuals' risk aversion over a sustained period of time, as empirical studies reveal. How such an event-triggered shift of risk preferences impacts optimal investment is the focus of this paper. On a finite time horizon where a major life event may occur independently of the financial market, an investor aims to maximize her expected utility from terminal wealth while foreseeing a potential change in her risk aversion. We find that the associated Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation involves the post-event optimal value function (under elevated but fixed risk aversion after the event's occurrence), and the Fenchel--Legendre transform fails to linearize this HJB equation: it yields a parabolic equation with a fully nonlinear term, induced precisely by the post-event optimal value function. Through a combination of fixed-point, compactness, and verification arguments, we establish the existence of a positive convex classical solution with suitable growth to the fully-nonlinear parabolic equation. The convex conjugate of this solution is shown to satisfy the HJB equation and coincides with the pre-event optimal value function. The optimal trading strategy is obtained by concatenating the optimal pre-event and post-event strategies -- the former is expressed in terms of the solution to the HJB equation and the latter is traditional Merton's ratio.

</details>


### [273] [A uniqueness result for finite-state mean field games with non-separable Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22537)
*Alekos Cecchin,Luca Di Persio,Nicola Fraccarolo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限状态空间上连续时间平均场博弈，其中转移率依赖于种群分布，导致非可分离哈密顿量。在经典Lasry-Lions单调性论证无法直接应用的情况下，通过结合成本强单调性假设和动力学中交互项的定量界，建立了任意时间跨度上的新唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lasry-Lions单调性论证无法直接适用于转移率依赖于种群分布（即非可分离哈密顿量）的平均场博弈问题，需要建立新的唯一性理论。

Method: 结合成本的强单调性假设与动力学中交互项的定量界，在有限状态空间连续时间平均场博弈框架下进行理论分析。

Result: 在任意时间跨度上建立了新的唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 通过强单调性和定量界条件，克服了非可分离哈密顿量带来的挑战，扩展了平均场博弈唯一性理论的应用范围。

Abstract: We study a class of continuous-time mean field games on a finite state space with transition rates depending on the population distribution, leading to a non-separable Hamiltonian. In this setting, classical Lasry--Lions monotonicity arguments do not apply directly. We establish a new uniqueness result on arbitrary time horizons under a combination of strong monotonicity assumptions on the costs and quantitative bounds on the interaction term in the dynamics.

</details>


### [274] [Low-Rank KKT Updates and a Parallel Flipping Mechanism for Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22538)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于最小Frobenius范数更新的二次插值模型，利用KKT矩阵的固定内积结构，推导出$\mathcal{O}(n^2)$的秩-2逆更新公式，避免了每次迭代的$\mathcal{O}(m^3)$重构。将该方法集成到并行置信域算法中，在530个基准问题上相比标准求解器降低了模型维护开销并提高了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的导数自由优化依赖二次插值，但保持这些模型通常需要$\mathcal{O}(m^3)$的线性系统求解，计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的更新方法。

Method: 发现了最小Frobenius范数更新模型对应的KKT矩阵具有固定内积结构；单点替换和提出的坐标轴翻转操作均对该矩阵产生精确的秩-2扰动；据此推导了KKT逆的$\mathcal{O}(n^2)$更新公式，避免了每次迭代的昂贵重构；将更新集成到并行置信域算法中，工作节点独立翻转插值轴、刷新局部模型并同步最优配置。

Result: 在530个基准问题上的测试表明，该方法降低了模型维护开销，在紧函数评估预算下相比标准求解器获得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 利用KKT矩阵的固定内积结构和秩-2扰动特性，可以显著降低基于模型的导数自由优化中模型维护的计算复杂度，提升算法在有限资源下的性能。

Abstract: Model-based derivative-free optimization relies on quadratic interpolation, but maintaining these models typically requires $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ linear system solves. We show that for the least Frobenius norm updating model, the associated KKT matrix possesses a fixed inner-product structure. Both single-point replacements and a proposed coordinate-axis flipping operation induce exact Rank-2 perturbations to this matrix. Using this structure, we derive an $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ update formula for the KKT inverse, eliminating costly refactorizations at each iteration. We integrate the update into a parallel trust-region algorithm where workers independently flip interpolation axes, refresh local models, and synchronize the best configuration. Tests on 530 benchmark problems show the method reduces model-maintenance overhead and achieves higher success rates under tight function-evaluation budgets compared to standard solvers.

</details>


### [275] [Comparing Optimization Models for Radiotherapy Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22539)
*C. C. Rambaldi Migliore,D. Stanicel,N. Musliu,G. Iacca,M. Roveri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对放射治疗调度问题（RTSP），提出了两种新型贪心启发式算法（RTSP First Fit和RTSP Best Fit），并将其作为模拟退火（SA）的构造启发式方法。与整数线性规划精确求解器相比，新方法在保持接近最优解质量的同时，大幅降低了运行时间和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于整数线性规划的每日批量调度方法虽有效，但计算资源（时间与内存）需求巨大，限制了实际应用。本文旨在解决这一计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 开发了两种贪心启发式算法（RTSP First Fit和RTSP Best Fit），并将它们作为模拟退火（SA）的初始解构造方法。在公开数据集上，与两种商用ILP精确求解器进行对比评估。

Result: 新启发式算法在六项调度目标（等待时间、偏好满足、加速器分配变更等）及四种权重配置下，解质量接近精确方法，同时显著降低运行时间和内存使用；结合SA后，解质量进一步提升，同时保持低计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 贪心启发式结合模拟退火是RTSP问题的高效求解方案，能够在可接受解质量下实现计算资源的极大节约，适用于临床大规模调度场景。

Abstract: The Radiotherapy Scheduling Problem (RTSP) involves determining an optimal schedule for patients undergoing radiation treatments, a task that has a massive impact on clinical outcomes given the central role of radiotherapy in cancer care. The daily batch approach--which consists of scheduling all the newly arrived patients together at the end of each day--modelled with Integer Linear Programming, is currently one of the most effective methods for the RTSP. However, this kind of formulation requires substantial computational resources in terms of time and memory. Here, we address these limitations by developing two novel greedy heuristics (named RTSP First Fit and RTSP Best Fit) and use them as constructive heuristics for a Simulated Annealing (SA) approach to optimize the scheduling. The proposed methods--the heuristics alone and their combination with SA--are evaluated on a publicly available dataset against an integer linear program formulation solved with two different state-of-the-art exact solvers. Evaluation metrics include six scheduling objectives capturing patient waiting times, preference satisfaction, and changes in linear accelerator assignment (aggregated in four different weight configurations), solving time, and memory consumption. The results show that the novel heuristics achieve solutions close to those of exact methods, while dramatically reducing runtime and memory usage; furthermore, when combined with SA, they further improve the solution quality while maintaining low runtime and memory usage.

</details>


### [276] [Multi-Path Routing in Decentralized Exchange Networks: Convex Allocation and an Improving-Path Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22540)
*Ilia Zhavoronkov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向去中心化交易所网络的图论与凸优化多路径路由框架，包含候选路径生成和流量分配两层实现，并在以太坊主网上进行了实证评估。核心贡献是改进路径验证证书机制，通过KKT乘子判断是否存在遗漏的有利路径。实验表明，与四个生产级DEX聚合器相比，中位价格偏差低于5个基点，前三名报价排名超过57%的周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化交易所网络（如Uniswap等AMM池构成的多跳路径）中，多路径路由问题尚未被系统地以图论和凸优化框架解决。现有DEX聚合器缺乏对路径完整性和最优性的理论保证，需要在计算效率与最优性之间取得平衡。

Method: 1) 将市场建模为有向代币多重图，弧线携带AMM交换函数。2) 路由分解为两层：(a) 候选路径生成层：采用gas感知的边缘k-最短路径枚举，边缘得分嵌入预期执行成本，路径构建中跟踪显式池-简单约束；(b) 流量分配层：在单纯形上求解凹最大化问题，施加每池价格影响上限。3) 核心贡献：改进路径验证证书——在k=20条候选路径上求解分配后，利用KKT乘子作为阈值，通过单次最短路径查询判断是否存在任何被忽略的池-简单路径能改进当前解。4) 链上执行通过智能合约级别的滑点容忍度保护。

Result: 在以太坊主网上，针对WETH-USDT报价对、6种交易规模的重复观测：中位价格偏差低于5个基点；前三名报价排名超过57%的周期。改进路径证书在大多数周期中确认了最优性充分性。

Conclusion: 证明将图论、凸优化和实际部署约束（gas成本、滑点）相结合能够构建理论上有保证、实际效果优于现有生产系统的DEX多路径路由引擎。改进路径验证框架为多路径路由最优性提供了可计算的理论保障。

Abstract: We present a graph-theoretic and convex optimization framework for multi-path routing in decentralized exchange networks, together with its implementation and empirical evaluation on Ethereum mainnet. The framework models the market as a directed token multigraph whose arcs carry AMM exchange functions. Routing is decomposed into two implemented layers: candidate path generation via gas-aware marginal k-shortest-path enumeration, where edge scores embed expected execution cost directly into graph traversal with an explicit pool-simple constraint tracked during path construction, and continuous flow allocation over the selected candidates solved as a concave maximization over a simplex with a per-pool price-impact cap. Under standard concavity and monotonicity assumptions, the KKT conditions imply marginal-output equalization across active paths. The central technical contribution is an improving-path certificate: after solving the allocation on k=20 candidate paths, the KKT multiplier is used as a threshold to determine via a single shortest-path query whether any omitted pool-simple path could improve the current solution; in our implementation the certificate confirms sufficiency in the majority of epochs. Execution is protected by an on-chain slippage tolerance enforced at the smart-contract level. We evaluate the implemented engine against four production DEX aggregators on repeated WETH-USDT quote observations across six trade sizes on Ethereum mainnet: median shortfall is below 5 bps across all sizes and top-3 quote rank exceeds 57% of epochs.

</details>


### [277] [SDE Guided Monte Carlo Reinforcement Learning: A Stochastic Maximum Principle Approach for Robust Decision Making in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22541)
*Juncai Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出SDE-MC-AC框架，将随机最大原理（SMP）的最优性条件作为启发式指导，映射到基于势能的奖励塑形、软max温度（扩散系数）、以及绝对值时序差分误差（值函数梯度模长），并推导出自适应温度调度策略。在固定迷宫测试床的三种噪声环境下，五个可证伪假设的实验验证了倒U型最优随机性曲线、自适应调度的鲁棒性、势能场引导的加速收敛、跨噪声泛化以及各组件不可或缺性，展示了“宏观确定、微观随机”的导航模式，建立了随机最优控制与鲁棒强化学习之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机最大原理（SMP）的定性最优性条件能否作为设计鲁棒表格强化学习算法的启发式指导原则，从而在严格随机最优控制与实用鲁棒强化学习之间建立理论对接。

Method: 提出SDE-MC-AC框架：将SMP的势场梯度映射为基于势能的奖励塑形，扩散系数映射为softmax温度参数，值函数梯度模长映射为回合平均绝对时序差分误差。推导出SMP启发的自适应温度调度策略，根据局部值不确定性随机调控探索程度，结合势能塑形奖励、熵正则化和自适应温度控制，在回合更新中集成蒙特卡洛Actor-Critic算法。

Result: 在固定迷宫测试床的三种噪声（感知噪声、动态目标噪声、动作噪声）下验证了五个可证伪假设：(i) 倒U型最优随机性曲线；(ii) 自适应温度调度在非平稳噪声下的鲁棒失败预防；(iii) 势能场引导加速收敛；(iv) 跨噪声泛化能力；(v) 系统性消融实验证实各组件不可或缺。轨迹可视化呈现“宏观确定、微观随机”的导航模式。

Conclusion: 即使将SMP启发式地转置到离散MDP中也能获得显著的实证收益，为随机最优控制与鲁棒强化学习之间沟通桥梁的建立提供了有力证据，表明SMP条件可作为设计鲁棒RL算法的有效启发式工具。

Abstract: This work investigates whether the qualitative optimality conditions of the SMP can serve as guiding heuristics for designing robust tabular RL algorithms. We propose the \textbf{SDE-MC-AC} framework, which establishes a set of SMP-inspired correspondences: the potential field gradient is mapped to potential-based reward shaping, the diffusion coefficient to a softmax temperature, and the value function gradient magnitude to the episode-averaged absolute temporal-difference (TD) error. Crucially, we derive an SMP-motivated adaptive temperature schedule that scales exploration stochastically in response to local value uncertainty. The resulting Monte Carlo Actor-Critic agent integrates potential-shaped rewards, entropy regularization, and adaptive temperature control within episodic updates. We conduct a set of five principled experiments in a minimal, fixed maze testbed under three canonical noise regimes (perceptual, dynamic goal, action) to test five falsifiable hypotheses derived from the SMP. Our results provide evidence for (i) an inverted-U optimal stochasticity curve, (ii) the robust failure prevention of the adaptive schedule under non-stationary noise, (iii) convergence acceleration by potential field guidance, (iv) cross-noise generalization, and (v) the indispensability of the combined SDE components through systematic ablation. Trajectory visualizations reveal a characteristic ``macroscopically deterministic, microscopically stochastic'' navigation pattern consistent with the SDE model. The study demonstrates that even a heuristic transposition of the SMP into a discrete MDP can yield significant empirical gains, thereby opening a bridge between rigorous stochastic optimal control and practical robust reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [278] [A Hybrid Matheuristic Framework for the Chinese Postman Problem with Load-Dependent Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22542)
*Thieu Khang Nguyen,Thu Huong Dang,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合优化框架，结合元启发式搜索与数学规划（特别是简化MILP模型和蚁群优化算法），有效解决了现实中物流运输中因负载影响成本的中国邮差问题，在基准测试中取得了优于现有方法的求解质量和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际物流和交通系统中，旅行成本依赖于车辆负载和能耗，而传统中国邮差问题（CPP）未考虑这一因素。为更真实地建模现实场景，需要解决带负载依赖成本的CPP变体（CPP-LC）。

Method: 提出混合优化框架，将局部搜索过程与简化的混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型相结合，在探索和强化之间取得平衡；同时开发了蚁群优化（ACO）算法以增强大规模实例上的可扩展性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提框架在求解质量上持续优于现有方法，同时保持了具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 混合优化策略对于复杂的负载依赖路由问题在实践应用中非常有效，且实现已开源。

Abstract: The Chinese Postman Problem with load-dependent costs (CPP-LC) arises in real-world logistics and transportation systems where travel costs depend on vehicle load and energy consumption. In this work, we propose a hybrid optimization framework that integrates metaheuristic search with mathematical programming to efficiently solve CPP-LC. The proposed method combines local search procedures with reduced mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) models to balance exploration and intensification. In addition, we develop an Ant Colony Optimization (ACO) algorithm to enhance scalability on large instances. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves high-quality solutions and outperforms existing approaches in solution quality, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of hybrid optimization strategies for complex, load-dependent routing problems in practical applications. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/HySonLab/MatCPP

</details>


### [279] [Route Based Map Matching via a Structured Codebook and Token Sequence Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22543)
*Takara Sakai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路线编码本的高效地图匹配方法，利用命名道路和交叉口的符号结构，通过网格量化器将GPS探针转换为令牌序列，并通过DAFSA×Levenshtein自动机进行模糊匹配，在都市高速路网上实现低计算成本的精确路线恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于路线的地图匹配方法未能充分利用命名道路和交叉口这一符号结构，导致计算效率低下。本研究旨在利用层次化路线名称表示和近似字符串匹配技术，降低地图匹配的计算开销。

Method: 1) 将候选路线表示为线路和交叉口名称的序列，构建路线码本；2) 利用网格量化器将GPS探针坐标转换为令牌序列；3) 采用DAFSA×Levenshtein自动机对探针令牌序列与码本成员进行模糊匹配解码，显著降低每次查询的解码成本。

Result: 在东京首都高速道路网的变形副本上评估，该方法在中等GPS噪声下能精确恢复路线，在高噪声下仍能识别道路和交叉口序列。灵敏度分析给出了网格量程的有效范围。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有极高的计算效率，解码成本相比暴力扫描降低数个数量级。真实探针评估、信道模型校准及与HMM的对比将在后续版本中完成。

Abstract: This study proposes an efficient and computationally light route based map matching method for GPS track data on urban expressway networks. The key idea is to exploit a symbolic structure of named lines and named junctions that link level map matching leaves unused. We represent each candidate route as a sequence of line and junction names, take the set of such sequences as a route codebook, and formulate map matching as scored alignment of a probe trajectory against members of the codebook. Probes become token sequences via a mesh quantizer, a precomputed grid mapping each coordinate to a line or junction token, and the decoder returns a member of the codebook by construction. The codebook is indexed by a DAFSA $\times$ Levenshtein automaton, a fuzzy lookup technique from approximate string matching and speech recognition; the per query decoding cost is orders of magnitude lower than a brute force scan. We evaluate the method on a deformed replica of the Tokyo Metropolitan Expressway topology. The method recovers the exact route at moderate GPS noise and continues to identify the line and junction sequence under heavy noise; a sensitivity analysis maps the mesh resolution operating range. Real probe evaluation, channel model calibration, and a head to head HMM comparison are left to a forthcoming version.

</details>


### [280] [Learning to Optimize at Scale: A Benders Decomposition-TransfORmers Framework for Stochastic Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22550)
*Seung Jin Choi,Kimiya Jozani,Josh Cooper,Esra Buyuktahtakin Toy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习增强的Benders分解框架，用于求解大规模两阶段随机混合整数规划，通过预训练Transformer模型加速场景子问题求解，成功将可解实例规模扩展至T=270。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Benders分解在处理大规模两阶段随机混合整数规划时收敛缓慢，尤其是对两阶段随机能力受限批量问题（TSSCLSP），现有方法难以扩展到大规模实例。

Method: 采用预训练的Transformer模型快速生成场景子问题的高质量近似解，并用这些预测生成强最优性割和可行性割来指导Benders主问题；同时设计了可扩展的生成机制，使固定训练时长的模型能求解任意长度的实例。

Result: 在测试集上成功求解了T=270的实例（此前难以处理），且生成的子问题解零不可行性。

Conclusion: Transformer可以作为强大的替代求解器嵌入经典分解算法中，显著扩展可求解问题的规模。

Abstract: We propose a learning-augmented Benders decomposition framework to solve large-scale two-stage stochastic mixed-integer programs. We focus on the two-stage stochastic capacitated lot-sizing problem (TSSCLSP) under demand uncertainty. Our method accelerates the convergence of the decomposition by using a pre-trained TransfORmer model to rapidly generate high-quality approximate solutions for the scenario subproblems. This hybrid strategy uses the TransfORmer predictions to generate strong optimality and feasibility cuts, effectively guiding the Benders master problem. Our framework includes a novel expandable generation mechanism, allowing a model trained on a fixed horizon to solve instances of arbitrary length. For the test set considered, our method solves instances up to T = 270, a scale previously intractable for this approach, while maintaining zero infeasibility in the generated subproblem solutions. This demonstrates the potential of TransfORmers as powerful surrogate solvers embedded within classical decomposition algorithms.

</details>


### [281] [Mirror Descent Methods for Quasar Convex Optimization Problems With Non-Smooth Inequality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22551)
*Mohammad Alkousa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑拟凸目标函数在非光滑凸泛函约束下的约束优化问题，提出了两组算法（标准版和改进版），通过在生产性/非生产性迭代点间切换来运作，并建立了确定性和随机设置下的镜像下降型算法及其收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非光滑拟凸目标函数在非光滑凸泛函不等式约束下的约束优化问题，这类问题在机器学习、信号处理等领域有广泛应用，但现有方法缺乏系统的收敛性分析。

Method: 提出两组算法（每组包含标准版和改进版），通过在生产性（productive）和非生产性（non-productive）两种迭代点类型之间切换来更新；分别针对确定性和随机环境设计了镜像下降型（mirror descent-type）算法。

Result: 建立了所提出算法在确定性和随机两种设置下的收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两组算法能有效处理非光滑拟凸目标函数在非光滑凸约束下的优化问题，在理论和实践上具有价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider constraint optimization problems subject to non-smooth convex functional (inequality-type) constraints, wherein the objective function is non-smooth and quasar convex. We propose and analyze two groups of algorithms, each consisting of a standard version and a modified variant, that operate by switching between two types of iteration points: productive and non-productive. Within each group, we develop distinct mirror descent-type algorithms for both deterministic and stochastic settings, and we establish their convergence rates.

</details>


### [282] [Threshold-indexability of restless bandits with real interval state spaces: a performance-metric verification framework and long-run average analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22557)
*José Niño-Mora*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为具有实值状态空间的二动作项目提出了一种基于性能度量的框架，用于证明阈值可索引性并计算Whittle指数。该框架将折扣部分守恒律（PCL）方法推广到不依赖特定准则的场景，通过边际生产率（MP）指数的单调性和连续性等价于阈值可索引性，并应用于网络爬虫、噪声信道传输和卡尔曼滤波bandits问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whittle指数策略在不稳定多臂老虎机问题中广泛应用，但应用该策略需要证明可索引性和计算指数，这是两个分析上具有挑战性的任务。现有方法通常需要先证明阈值最优性再证明最优阈值随资源价格的单调性，过程繁琐且依赖于特定准则。

Method: 本文提出一个性能度量框架，将折扣部分守恒律（PCL）方法推广到准则无关（criterion-agnostic）的场景，直接利用阈值策略的奖励和资源度量工作。主要定理是一个验证与刻画结果：在边际资源正性和边际分部积分恒等式下，阈值可索引性等价于边际生产率（MP）指数的单调性和连续性，此时MP指数等于Whittle指数。框架通过消失折扣转移法处理长期平均准则，并通过连续延拓或消失折扣极限处理异常状态。

Result: 应用于网络爬虫和噪声信道传输问题，恢复了已知的长期平均Whittle指数。对于标量卡尔曼滤波bandits，证明了正则部分平均成本结果，并将剩余可索引性问题简化为显式的异常状态度量极限猜想。

Conclusion: 本文提出的性能度量框架为二元动作、实值状态空间的Whittle指数计算提供了一种统一且更直接的方法，避免了传统方法中先证明阈值最优性的繁琐步骤，并通过多个应用验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Restless multiarmed bandits are Markov decision process models for allocating a scarce resource among projects whose states evolve under active or passive actions. Whittle's index policy is widely used for such problems, but its application to a given model requires both a proof of indexability and a means of computing the index, two analytically challenging tasks. This paper develops a performance-metric framework for proving threshold-indexability and computing Whittle indices for binary-action projects with real interval state spaces. The framework extends discounted partial conservation law \textup{(PCL)} methods to a criterion-agnostic setting and works directly with reward and resource metrics of threshold policies, rather than first proving threshold optimality and then monotonicity of optimal thresholds in the resource price. The main theorem is a verification and characterization result: under marginal-resource positivity and a marginal integration-by-parts identity, threshold-indexability is equivalent to monotonicity and continuity of the marginal productivity (MP) index, which then equals the Whittle index. The framework is specialized to the discrete-time long-run average criterion by a vanishing-discount transfer of discounted threshold metrics and includes exceptional states where the MP marginal-resource denominator vanishes, handled by continuous extension or vanishing-discount limits. Applications to web crawling and noisy-channel transmission recover known long-run average Whittle indices. For scalar Kalman-filter bandits, it proves a regular-part average-cost result and reduces the remaining indexability question to explicit exceptional-state metric-limit conjectures.

</details>


### [283] [Major-Minor LQ Mean Field Games with Erroneous Initial Information: Distributed Error Estimation and Strategy Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22558)
*Yuxin Jin,Haotian Wang,Wang Yao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在受限观测结构下，具有错误初始信息的主从线性二次型均值场博弈（MMLQMFGs）。每个次要智能体仅观测自身状态和主要智能体状态，主要智能体观测自身状态和部分次要智能体状态，双方均无法直接观测均值场状态。研究表明初始信息误差通过博弈动态线性传播，导致主要状态、实际均值场及智能体内部更新的均值场状态产生显式偏差。基于此结构，将分布式误差识别建模为从离散时间局部观测中估计未知初始误差的参数估计问题，并构建了最大似然估计器。随后提出一种基于估计的中间时刻策略修正方法，通过重构当前均值场并切换至相应控制律。数值结果验证了所提方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际大规模多智能体系统中，初始信息往往存在误差且观测结构受限（智能体无法直接获取全局均值场信息），现有均值场博弈理论大多假设完美信息或完全观测，缺乏对这类现实约束下误差传播与补偿机制的研究。

Method: 1) 建立初始信息误差在博弈动态中的线性传播模型；2) 将误差识别转化为参数估计问题，基于离散时间局部观测构建最大似然估计器；3) 提出中间时刻的策略修正方法：先利用估计误差重构当前均值场，再切换至对应控制律。

Result: 初始信息误差可线性传播并产生显式偏差；最大似然估计器可有效估计未知初始误差；在对称设定下，主要智能体的估计精度仅取决于观测到的次要智能体数量，而非其身份；数值结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为受限观测结构下具有初始信息误差的均值场博弈提供了系统的误差分析与补偿框架，理论推导了误差传播的显式形式，并给出了可操作的分布式估计与策略修正方法，对大规模多智能体系统的鲁棒控制具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: This paper studies major-minor linear-quadratic mean field games (MMLQMFGs) with erroneous initial information under a constrained observation structure. Each minor agent observes only its own state and the major agent's state, while the major agent observes its own state and the states of a subset of minor agents; neither side observes the mean field state directly. We show that the initial-information errors propagate linearly through the game dynamics and lead to explicit deviations in the major state, the actual mean field, and the agents' internally updated mean field states. Based on this structure, we formulate distributed error identification as a parameter-estimation problem from discrete-time local observations and construct maximum-likelihood estimators for unknown initial errors. We then propose an estimate-based strategy modification at an intermediate time by reconstructing the current mean field from the estimated errors and switching to the corresponding control law. We also characterize the resulting estimation errors and show that, in the present symmetric setting, the major agent's estimation precision depends on the number of observed minor agents but not on their identities. Numerical results illustrate the proposed method.

</details>


### [284] [Efficient multidisciplinary design via Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22560)
*Nathalie Bartoli,Thierry Lefebvre,Rémi Lafage,Paul Saves,Youssef Diouane,Joseph Morlier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: SEGOMOE是一个基于贝叶斯优化的工具，结合自适应高斯过程模型和专家模型，用于高效处理航空领域复杂、高计算成本的多学科优化问题，支持混合变量、多保真度数据及单/多目标优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空领域复杂系统的优化涉及高计算成本的仿真、混合设计变量（连续、离散、分类、层次化）以及非线性约束，现有方法难以同时高效处理这些问题，需要一种更鲁棒且通用的优化工具。

Method: 采用贝叶斯优化框架，结合自适应高斯过程模型处理混合变量，通过组合专家模型（SEGOMOE）处理目标和约束的非线性，基于开源代理建模工具箱（SMT）实现，支持多保真度数据和单/多目标优化，并能处理隐式约束和高维分解。

Result: 通过基准测试和真实航空应用验证，SEGOMOE表现出鲁棒性和多功能性，能有效解决多学科挑战。

Conclusion: SEGOMOE是一种适用于航空领域复杂高计算成本系统优化的鲁棒且通用的贝叶斯优化工具。

Abstract: This study introduces SEGOMOE, a Bayesian optimization tool for optimizing complex, computationally expensive systems, especially in aeronautics. It efficiently handles mixed design variables (continuous, discrete, categorical, hierarchical) using adaptive Gaussian process models. SEGOMOE combines expert models to address nonlinearities in objectives and constraints, leveraging the open-source Surrogate Modeling Toolbox (SMT). The tool supports multi-fidelity data and solves both single- and multi-objective problems, including hidden constraints and high-dimensional decomposition. Validated through benchmarks and real-world aeronautical applications, SEGOMOE proves to be robust and versatile for tackling multidisciplinary challenges.

</details>


### [285] [A Formal Kinetic Theory for Zeroth-Order Newton Dynamics:Stein-Corrected Hessian Estimation and Curvature--Variance Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22567)
*Shihao Ji,Mingyu Li,Zihui Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了零阶牛顿型方法的动力学框架，揭示了无梯度条件下Hessian估计的有偏性及Gaussian-Stein修正的必要性，分析了逆Hessian线性化下的双重噪声通道，并通过小质量动理学升维建立了有限步牛顿更新与欠阻尼相空间模型的联系，数值实验验证了估计量恒等式、方差律、维度标度及优化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度和Hessian不可用时，零阶牛顿型方法与一阶无梯度方法行为差异显著，但缺乏统一的动力学分析框架来理解其噪声结构、偏差来源及曲率-方差权衡。

Method: 1) 对随机方向Hessian估计量进行偏差分析，引入Gaussian-Stein修正以消除二次型上的偏差；2) 通过逆Hessian线性化识别梯度噪声（被逆Hessian预条件）和Hessian噪声（通过逆Hessian夹层传播）两个通道；3) 采用小质量动理学升维将有限步牛顿更新映射到欠阻尼相空间模型，并在过阻尼空间极限下导出Lyapunov界。

Result: 推导出Hessian噪声通道承载二阶差分因子μ_H^{-4}的方差律，建立了步长、批量大小、平滑半径与正则化之间的曲率-方差权衡关系，数值实验验证了估计量恒等式、梯度/Hessian方差律、维度标度、逆扰动精度及优化行为。

Conclusion: 零阶牛顿型方法存在独特的偏差与噪声结构，Gaussian-Stein修正是无偏Hessian估计的关键，小质量动理学框架为理解其优化动力学和超参数权衡提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Zeroth-order Newton-type methods are useful when gradients and Hessians are unavailable, but they behave quite differently from first-order gradient-free methods. We develop a kinetic framework for algorithms that estimate both gradient and Hessian from black-box function values. The naive random-direction Hessian estimator turns out to be biased even on quadratics; a Gaussian--Stein correction is needed to estimate the Hessian of the Gaussian-smoothed objective. Linearizing the inverse Hessian exposes two noise channels: gradient noise preconditioned by the inverse Hessian, and Hessian noise transmitted through an inverse-Hessian sandwich. Under a noisy oracle the second channel carries the second-difference factor $μ_H^{-4}$. A small-mass kinetic lift links the finite-step Newton update to an underdamped phase-space model; the overdamped spatial limit yields a Lyapunov bound that exposes the curvature--variance trade-off between step size, batch sizes, smoothing radii, and regularization. Numerical experiments confirm estimator identities, the gradient and Hessian variance laws, dimension scaling, inverse-perturbation accuracy, and optimization behavior under query-budget and regularization ablations.

</details>


### [286] [Rational Minimax Approximations for Matrix-Valued Functions: Existence, Optimality and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22576)
*Lei-Hong Zhang,Chenkun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续复矩阵值函数在Frobenius范数下的有理极小极大逼近问题，其中所有逼近项共享公共分母。证明了点集上矩阵值逼近子的存在性、建立了局部与全局最优性的Kolmogorov对偶准则和Ruttan型充分条件，并揭示了与m-d-Lawson数值方法之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的标量有理逼近推广到矩阵值情形，以解决系统建模、微波设计和非线性特征值问题等应用中的矩阵逼近需求。

Method: 1) 在稠密点集上证明矩阵值有理逼近子的存在性（推广Walsh定理）；2) 导出矩阵值Kolmogorov对偶准则和Ruttan型全局最优性充分条件；3) 对解析函数建立连续域与边界离散点逼近之间的联系（极大模原理）；4) 将最优性条件与m-d-Lawson迭代方法的收敛方程建立等价关系。

Result: 建立了矩阵值有理极小极大逼近的完整理论框架：包含存在性定理、局部/全局最优性的对偶刻画、连续问题与离散问题最优性之间的桥梁，以及强对偶性与Ruttan条件的等价关系。

Conclusion: 为矩阵值有理极小极大逼近的认证和计算提供了理论基础，特别是Ruttan条件可作为从边界或离散节点获得的逼近子解决原连续问题最优性的认证证书。

Abstract: In this paper, we study rational minimax approximation for continuous complex matrix-valued functions in the Frobenius norm, where all approximant entries share a common denominator. This generalizes classical scalar rational approximation, with applications in system modeling, microwave design, and nonlinear eigenvalue problems. We first prove the existence of such matrix-valued approximants on point sets dense in themselves, extending Walsh's foundational scalar result. Next, we establish characterizations of the local and global minimax approximants by deriving primal/dual matrix-valued Kolmogorov criteria and a Ruttan-type sufficient condition for global optimality. For analytic functions on a continuum, we link continuum minimax approximation to approximation on its boundary, and finite boundary samples via the maximum norm principle. We show that Ruttan's sufficient optimality condition provides a certificate under which a minimax approximant obtained from the boundary or from a discrete set of boundary nodes also solves the original continuum problem. Finally, for discrete approximation, we connect these conditions to a dual problem and the related dual-based numerical method m-d-Lawson: when the original minimax problem admits a solution, strong duality is equivalent to Ruttan's sufficient optimality condition, and, the optimality equations underlying the m-d-Lawson iteration coincide with Kolmogorov's dual criteria. These results provide a theoretical basis for certifying and computing matrix-valued rational minimax approximants.

</details>


### [287] [A unified approach to non-unique fixed points via modified C-class Ciric-type contractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22579)
*Olaoluwa Omidire,Memudu Olatinwo,Kamiludeen Tijani,Babatunde Ishola*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入改进的C类函数，在完备度量空间中为Ciric型映射建立了新的非唯一不动点定理，推广了经典压缩条件，并在轨道连续性和轨道完备性下证明了Picard迭代的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不动点定理通常要求严格压缩性并保证唯一性，这限制了在固有非唯一不动点映射中的应用。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 构造了一个包含三变量改进C类函数和变距函数的广义压缩不等式；在轨道连续性和轨道完备性条件下，不依赖全局Lipschitz条件或严格压缩性，证明Picard迭代收敛到不动点。

Result: 建立了新的非唯一不动点存在性定理；提供了唯一性的简单判据；设计的例子展示了框架的尖锐性和一般性，严格推广了经典Ciric、Chatterjea压缩及混合(Φ,ψ)-压缩。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性算子理论、迭代方法以及优化和变分问题中的应用提供了统一且稳健的理论基础。

Abstract: Fixed point theory is central to nonlinear analysis, yet classical results often assume strict contractivity and guarantee uniqueness, limiting applicability to mappings with inherently non-unique fixed points. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a modified C-class function and establishing new non-unique fixed point theorems for Ciric-type mappings in complete metric spaces. We construct a generalized contractive inequality using a three-variable modifed C-class function combined with an altering distance function. Under orbital continuity and orbital completeness, we prove that Picard iteration converges to a fixed point without requiring global Lipschitz conditions or strict contractivity. Carefully designed examples illustrate the sharpness and generality of the framework, strictly extending classical Ciric and Chatterjea contractions as well as hybrid $(Φ,ψ)$-contractions. Our main contributions are: (i) a unified framework encompassing classical and generalized C-class contractions, (ii) existence results for non-unique fixed points, (iii) a simple criterion for uniqueness when desired, and (iv) practical validation through nontrivial examples. These results provide a robust foundation for further research in nonlinear operator theory, iterative methods, and applications in optimization and variational problems.

</details>


### [288] [Optimality of a Threshold Policy for a Queueing System with One Fast Server and Two Identical Slow Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22580)
*Weina Wang,Taha Ameen,Yudong Chen,Yige Hong,Josh Nichols,Matthew Zurek*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有三个服务器（一个快速服务器和两个相同慢速服务器）的排队系统最优控制问题，证明阈值策略在该三服务器系统中是最优的。核心技术思路由GPT-5.5 Pro生成，作者验证了证明并用Lean 4验证了三个关键引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lin和Kumar（1984）提出了两服务器（一快一慢）的排队系统最优控制问题，并证明了阈值策略的最优性。但将该结果推广到两个以上服务器一直被视为开放问题。本文旨在解决第一个非平凡情况——三服务器系统。

Method: 论文证明了三服务器（一个快速服务器和两个相同慢速服务器）系统中阈值策略的最优性。核心技术思路由GPT-5.5 Pro生成，作者验证了证明并重写了论文以确保严谨性和清晰度，同时在Lean 4中验证了三个关键引理。

Result: 成功证明了三服务器排队系统中最优控制策略属于阈值策略，解决了此前被视为开放问题的推广难题。

Conclusion: 本文解决了Lin-Kumar问题的第一个非平凡推广情况，表明阈值策略在更一般的多服务器排队系统中仍可能保持最优性。同时展示了AI辅助数学证明的可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimal control problem of a queueing system with three servers: one fast server and two identical slow servers. The two-server version of this problem, with one fast server and one slow server, was introduced by Lin and Kumar (1984), and the optimal policy has been shown to be of threshold type. However, generalizing this result beyond the two-server setting has been considered an open problem. In this paper, we resolve the first nontrivial case by proving that a threshold policy is optimal for the three-server system considered.
  The core technical ideas in this paper are generated by GPT-5.5 Pro. We have included a short report describing the authors' interactions with GPT-5.5 Pro. The authors verified the proofs and rewrote the paper for better rigor, clarity, and exposition. In addition, three key lemmas have also been verified in Lean 4.

</details>


### [289] [Randomized Constructive Heuristics for the VRPTW: A Focus on Regret-k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22581)
*Florian Rascoussier,Romain Billot,Lina Fahed,Christine Solnon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种经典构造式启发式算法（最近邻、最佳插入、Regret-k）在随机化多起点框架下的性能，提出了一种新颖的Regret-k随机化方法，通过分离"选择哪个客户"和"插入位置"的决策，并基于后悔值进行概率选择，在保持启发式前瞻性的同时引入多样性。实验在Solomon和Gehring & Homberger基准（100-1000客户）上遵循DIMACS 2021约定进行，分析了解决方案质量与计算成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造式启发式算法在有时间窗车辆路径问题（VRPTW）中广泛应用，但经典算法多在确定性地使用，其在随机化多起点框架下的潜力尚未被充分探索。特别是Regret-k的随机化方法此前未被研究过。

Method: 将三种经典启发式（最近邻、最佳插入、Regret-k）置于随机化多起点框架中；提出Regret-k的新型随机化方法，分离"选择哪个客户"（who）和"插入位置"（where）的决策，基于后悔值进行概率选择；进一步研究结合局部搜索和蚁群优化的影响；按照DIMACS 2021约定（整数截断欧氏距离）在标准基准集上实验。

Result: 实验展示了所提随机化方法在解决方案质量与计算成本之间的权衡，为MAMUT项目中混合方法（ACO、ILS、分支定价）解决时间依赖VRPTW提供了初步基础。

Conclusion: 提出的Regret-k随机化方法在多起点框架下有效，是迈向面向时间依赖VRPTW的混合方法的第一步；所有关键组件已用现代C++实现并通过pybind11绑定到Python。

Abstract: The Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (VRPTW) requires a fleet of capacitated vehicles to serve customers within strict time windows while minimizing total travel time. Constructive heuristics are fundamental for generating solutions and commonly serve as starting points for metaheuristics such as Iterated Local Search (ILS), Genetic Algorithms, and Ant Colony Optimization (ACO). This work studies three classical constructive heuristics - Nearest Neighbor, Best Insertion, and Regret-k - within a randomized, multi-start framework, a setting that remains largely under-explored despite their well-established deterministic use. We address how to effectively randomize these heuristics and, in particular, propose a novel randomization of Regret-k by separating the ''who'' (which customer to insert next) and ''where'' (insertion position) decisions, applying a probabilistic selection proportional to the regret value to preserve the heuristic's foresight while injecting the diversity needed for a multi-start approach. We further study the impact of combining these greedy randomized constructions with Local Search and ACO. Experiments follow the DIMACS 2021 conventions (integer, truncated Euclidean distances) on the Solomon and Gehring \& Homberger benchmarks (100--1000 customers), comparing against the Best-Known Solutions and the state-of-the-art Hybrid Genetic Search solver, with all critical components implemented in modern C++ and bound to Python via pybind11. To the best of our knowledge, the randomization of Regret-k has never been examined; our analysis highlights the trade-off between solution quality and computational cost, providing a preliminary step toward hybrid methods (ACO, ILS, Branch-and-Price) for the Time-Dependent VRPTW within the MAMUT project.

</details>


### [290] [Branch \&amp; Price \&amp; Cut for the Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (TDVRPTW)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22582)
*Florian Rascoussier,Romain Billot,Lina Fahed,Christine Solnon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文旨在通过将单车辆变体（TDTSPTW）的最新进展（如A*的精确任意时间扩展和动态规划引导的大邻域搜索）迁移到多车辆设置，并集成数据挖掘与机器学习来引导列生成，从而推进带时间窗的时变车辆路径问题（TDVRPTW）的精确可解释求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变旅行时间在现实城市交通中普遍存在，但TDVRPTW的精确求解方法尚不成熟，现有方法仅依赖于Dabia等人（2013）的Branch & Price算法，需要进一步提升其性能和可解释性以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 重新实现并改进Branch & Price算法，具体包括：(1) 将单车辆TDTSPTW的精确任意时间A*扩展和动态规划引导的大邻域搜索迁移到多车辆场景；(2) 集成数据挖掘与机器学习，利用历史路径知识指导列生成过程。

Result: 预期构建一个高性能、可解释的求解器，能够在保证解质量的同时满足真实城市交通场景需求。

Conclusion: 通过跨变体的技术迁移和数据驱动方法融合，有望推进TDVRPTW精确求解的前沿水平，实现accuracy与interpretability的统一。

Abstract: In urban contexts, travel times vary strongly with the time of day and traffic conditions. The Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (TDVRPTW) extends the classical VRPTW by making travel times depend on departure time, with the objective of minimizing the total travel time of at most $k$ vehicle routes serving customers within strict time windows. Exact resolution of this realistic yet understudied problem currently relies on the Branch \& Price approach of Dabia et al. (2013), in which a Column Generation scheme decomposes the problem into a set-partitioning master problem and a resource-constrained shortest-path pricing problem solved by dynamic programming. This work, conducted within the MAMUT project (Machine Learning and Matheuristics for Urban Transport), aims to advance the state of the art for the exact and explainable resolution of the TDVRPTW. We propose to reimplement and revisit the pioneering Branch \& Price algorithm by transferring recent advances made on the single-vehicle variant (TDTSPTW) to the multi-vehicle setting, notably an exact anytime extension of A* and a Large Neighborhood Search guided by dynamic programming for the pricing sub-problem. We further explore the integration of data mining and machine learning to guide column generation by exploiting knowledge from previously computed routes. The goal is a high-performance and interpretable solver that meets the requirements of real-world urban transport while preserving guarantees on solution quality.

</details>


### [291] [Diamond transports in quadratic-form and distorted optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22589)
*Ruodu Wang,Zhenyuan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了钻石型传输（diamond transport）在二次型最优传输（QOT）和扭曲最优传输（DOT）框架下的优化性质。主要结果表明，在对称一维边际分布下，钻石传输是一大类QOT问题的优化器，并在矩形成本下是唯一最小化器；在DOT框架下，钻石型传输是自然成本类的最小化器，且钻石传输在特定例子中唯一。此外，还揭示了DOT与QOT的交集对应于二次型扭曲函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输（OT）目标函数是耦合的仿射函数，因此由菱形copula支撑上的均匀律生成的钻石传输不能成为经典设置中的唯一最小化器。为了在更广泛的非经典设置（如非凸的QOT和DOT）中研究钻石型传输的优化性质，本文探索了这些框架下钻石传输成为最优解的条件。

Method: 本文在QOT框架下分析对称一维边际分布在依赖坐标内距离的成本函数下的优化问题，研究正定性和凸性条件下的乘积成本以及混合矩形成本；在DOT框架下研究自然成本类中钻石型传输的最小化性质，并分析DOT与QOT的交集。

Result: 在QOT框架下，钻石传输是对称一维边际分布下一大类成本函数的优化器，对于矩形成本（除边界情况外）是唯一最小化器。在DOT框架下，钻石型传输是自然成本类的最小化器，钻石传输在特定例子中是唯一最小化器。DOT与QOT的交集恰好对应于二次型扭曲函数。

Conclusion: 钻石传输在非经典OT设置（QOT和DOT）中具有重要的优化性质，能够在多种成本函数下成为最优甚至唯一最优传输方案，拓展了最优传输理论的应用范围。

Abstract: The diamond transport is generated by the uniform law on a diamond-shaped copula support. Since a classical optimal transport (OT) objective is affine in the coupling, this transport cannot be the unique minimizer in the classical setting. We study a broader family of transports, called diamond-type transports, in non-classical settings such as quadratic-form optimal transport (QOT) and distorted optimal transport (DOT), which are generally nonconvex. Our main results are within the QOT framework: for symmetric one-dimensional marginals, the diamond transport is an optimizer for a large class of QOT problems whose costs depend on within-coordinate distances. Examples include product costs under positive-definiteness and convexity conditions and, in particular, mixed rectangular costs. For rectangular costs, we show that the diamond transport is the unique minimizer except for boundary cases. In the DOT framework, diamond-type transports are minimizers for a natural class of cost, and the diamond transport is the unique minimizer in specialized examples. We also identify the intersection between DOT and QOT, which corresponds precisely to quadratic distortion functions.

</details>


### [292] [DRP-FLR: Data-Driven Assessment of Demand Response Potential for Flexible Load Regulation in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22590)
*Yunhao Yao,Siyu Jing,Yang Yang,Qiang Xu,Changqi Weng,Xiang-Yang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出DRP-FLR框架，通过嵌入外部知识进行短期负荷预测、构建实体特定负荷模式画像、以及MILP优化求解，实现了灵活负荷调节，在区域电网和校园微电网实验中调节偏差降低36.63%-91.87%，参与者收益平均提升44.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI负载和可再生能源的快速增长加剧了电力系统供需失衡，传统负荷调节难以满足系统级平衡需求，现有DR方法要么只优化电费或用户舒适度而对系统平衡贡献有限，要么忽略异构能源实体的多样性和动态消费模式导致过度或不足调节。

Method: 1) 通过将外部知识（实体信息、预测时间）嵌入历史负荷表示来实现短期负荷预测；2) 对历史负荷曲线聚类构建实体特异性负荷模式画像，通过匹配预测负荷与模式画像估计DR潜力；3) 将灵活负荷调节建模为混合整数优化问题，使用MILP求解器联合优化DR利用率、参与者经济效益和可再生能源消纳，同时强制执行供需平衡和经济可行性约束。

Result: 在区域电网和校园微电网实验中，DRP-FLR将调节偏差降低36.63%-91.87%，参与者收益平均提升44.66%。

Conclusion: DRP-FLR通过融合外部知识、聚类画像和MILP优化，有效提升了需求响应调节的准确性和参与者经济效益，实现了系统级供需平衡。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads and renewable energy resources exacerbates supply-demand imbalance in power systems, making traditional load regulation designed for efficient allocation inadequate and motivating demand response (DR) mechanisms to enable load controllability in smart grids. However, existing DR-oriented approaches either focus on optimizing electricity cost or occupant comfort with limited benefit to system-level balance. Others overlook the diverse and dynamic consumption patterns of heterogeneous energy entities, leading to significant over- or under-regulation. Therefore, we propose DRP-FLR. First, DRP-FLR achieves accurate short-term load forecasting by embedding exogenous knowledge (e.g., entity information, prediction time) into historical load representations. Next, it constructs entity-specific load-pattern profiles by clustering historical load curves, and estimates DR potential by matching forecasted loads with pattern profiles. Finally, DRP-FLR formulates flexible load regulation as a mixed-integer optimization problem and solves it with an MILP solver to jointly optimize DR utilization, participant economic benefit, and renewable accommodation, while enforcing supply-demand balance and economic feasibility.
