Experiments on a regional grid and a campus microgrid show that DRP-FLR reduces regulation deviation by 36.63%-91.87% and improves participant benefit by 44.66% on average.

</details>


### [293] [d'Alembert's Functional Equation and a Globally Convex Free-Action Principle on Positive Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22594)
*Sebastian Pardo-Guerra,Jonathan Washburn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明d'Alembert泛函方程在正路径上诱导的动能作用是强凸的，其代价函数为cosh形式。该凸性导出三条推论：单边弦条件刻画全局最小性、均匀对数速度路径是唯一固定端点极小元、作用量差满足精确Bregman/Pythagorean等式。文章严格界定了该纯数学定理的适用范围，并条件性地将其与牛顿力学和快度力学建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究d'Alembert泛函方程在正路径上诱导的动能作用，并证明其强凸性，以揭示该作用量的几何与变分性质。

Method: 从d'Alembert泛函方程出发，在正实数路径上推导出cosh代价函数；通过对数坐标变换将问题转化为log-space中的强凸性分析；利用Bregman散度、Friedrichs-Poincaré不等式等工具建立凸性推论。

Result: 1) 单边弦条件可刻画全局最小性；2) 均匀对数速度路径是唯一固定端点全局极小元；3) 作用量差满足精确的Bregman/Pythagorean恒等式，并由定量Friedrichs-Poincaré界加强。该cosh作用在牛顿小步极限下恢复经典结果，且快度剖面为Kkin_m(φ)=m(γ_L-1)，但其对偶Hamiltonian并非狭义相对论自由粒子Hamiltonian。

Conclusion: cosh动能作用是强凸的，全局最小性是自由扇区现象；一旦加入非仿射严格凸势函数，联合凸性丢失，回归经典稳态作用量图景。该定理与物理力学的桥梁是有条件的，需额外结构支撑。

Abstract: We study the kinetic action that d'Alembert's functional equation induces on positive paths in $\Rplus$, and prove it strongly convex. Calibrated d'Alembert forces the cosh cost $\Jcost(x)=\tfrac12(x+x^{-1})-1$, i.e.\ $\Jlog(ξ)=\coshξ-1$ in the log coordinate $ξ=\log x$. Evaluating this log-cost at the log-\emph{velocity} $\dotξ$ rather than the log-position -- a single postulate (Postulate~\ref{post:step}) -- yields $\actionA[γ]=\int_a^b(\cosh\dotξ-1)\,dt$, strongly convex under geometric (log-space) interpolation. This convexity has three consequences, none requiring an Euler--Lagrange equation, a Fréchet derivative, or a second variation. First, a one-sided chord condition characterizes global minimality. Second, the unique fixed-endpoint minimizer is the uniform-log-velocity path. Third, the action gap obeys an exact Bregman / Pythagorean identity $\actionA[γ]-\actionA[γ_*]=\int D_\Kkin(\dotξ\,\|\,\dotξ_*)\,dt$, sharpened by a quantitative Friedrichs--Poincaré bound on $\log(γ/γ_*)$. It has a dually-flat / Hessian-manifold reading in the additive coordinate $ξ$. \\ This theorem is purely mathematical, and we delimit it. The bridge to Newtonian and rapidity mechanics is \emph{conditional}, requiring structure beyond Postulate~\ref{post:step}: a kinematic embedding, a mass coupling, a time calibration, and a Hamiltonian-primary Legendre structure. Granted these, the cosh action recovers the Newtonian small-step limit and the rapidity profile $\Kkin_m(φ)=m(γ_L-1)$; yet the cosh-dual Hamiltonian is \emph{not} the special-relativistic free-particle Hamiltonian (Proposition~\ref{prop:not-SR}), the agreement being one of profile, not an identity of Hamiltonians. Global minimality is a free-sector phenomenon: once a non-affine strictly convex potential is added, joint convexity is lost and the classical stationary-action picture returns.

</details>


### [294] [The Combined Van-and-Mopeds Routing Problem with Time Windows: Formulation, Variants, and Solution Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22601)
*Pedro Lameiras,Alexandre P. Francisco,Adriano Serrano,Cátia Vaz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了组合式货车与踏板车带时间窗路径问题（CVMRPTW），这是一种面向欧洲密集城市最后一英里包裹递送的新型异构车队路径规划变体。CVMRPTW以一辆货车作为移动基站，按需部署踏板车前往货车无法或低效到达的区域。主要目标是最小化所用踏板车数量，同时满足所有时间窗约束。作者提出了包含22个结构化约束族的混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，并拓展了三种运营变体。开发了词典序两阶段优化框架和三种图稀疏化预处理技术，以及基于聚类的组合路径启发式算法（CBCR）。在里斯本和斯图加特的真实数据实验表明，MILP可在一小时内求解最多48个客户的实例，CBCR启发式在有利条件下可将可解规模扩展至80个客户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲密集城市中，传统货车在狭窄街道、限行区域或低效路段进行最后一英里包裹递送时面临诸多限制，而踏板车等微型交通方式更具灵活性。现有异构车队路径规划研究较少考虑货车作为移动基站动态部署踏板车的协同模式，因此需要一种新的路径优化模型来平衡运营效率与成本。

Method: 1) 构建包含22个结构化约束族的MILP模型，涵盖车辆同步、容量追踪和时间窗合规；2) 提出三种运营变体（标准模型、主动等待货车变体、公共仓库变体）；3) 设计词典序两阶段优化框架，在保持主目标最优的前提下优化次目标；4) 开发三种图稀疏化预处理技术，减少变量和约束多达25%；5) 提出CBCR启发式算法，包含聚类阶段、簇级精确求解和路径重构阶段。

Result: 基于里斯本和斯图加特OpenStreetMap真实数据的实验表明：MILP求解器能够在一小时内求解最多48个客户的实例；CBCR启发式在有利条件下可将可解规模扩展至80个客户。

Conclusion: 该研究为城市最后一英里递送提供了新的建模框架和求解方法，验证了货车-踏板车协同模式的可行性，所提出的预处理技术和启发式算法有效提升了问题规模的可解性。

Abstract: We introduce and study the Combined Van-and-Mopeds Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVMRPTW), a novel variant of the heterogeneous fleet vehicle routing problem motivated by last-mile parcel delivery in dense European cities. In the CVMRPTW, a single van serves as a mobile base from which on-demand mopeds are deployed to reach customers located in areas inaccessible or inefficient for vans. The primary objective is to minimise the number of mopeds used while guaranteeing that all delivery time windows are met. We propose a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation that captures vehicle synchronisation, capacity tracking, and time-window compliance through 22 structured constraint families, and extend it to three operationally motivated variants: the standard model, the active-waiting-van variant, and the common-depot variant. A lexicographic two-stage optimisation framework allows secondary objectives-route duration, combined travel time, and combined distance-to be optimised without sacrificing the primary objective. Three graph-sparsification preprocessing techniques reduce the number of variables and constraints by up to 25%. For instances beyond the practical limits of exact solvers, we develop the Cluster-Based Combined Routing (CBCR) heuristic, which decomposes the problem into a clustering phase, a cluster-level exact solve, and a route-reconstruction phase. Computational experiments on real-world instances derived from OpenStreetMap data for Lisbon and Stuttgart show that the MILP solves instances with up to 48 customers to feasibility within one hour, and that the CBCR heuristic extends tractability to instances with up to 80 customers under favourable conditions.

</details>


### [295] [Transform Method for Stochastic Processing and Matching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22618)
*Sushil Mahavir Varma,Prakirt Jhunjhunwala,Daniela Hurtado-Lange,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了变换方法（Transform Method）——一种用于随机处理与匹配网络（SPNs/SMNs）稳态分析的统一框架。该方法通过利用指数检验函数的零漂移特性直接处理极限前系统，推导出队列长度分布变换的显式泛函方程，从而提供非渐近的性能保证，弥合了理论渐近分析与实际系统行为之间的差距。自2020年提出以来，该方法已扩展至客户放弃、状态相关到达、马尔可夫调制到达、大规模系统及多维网络等复杂场景，并在匹配市场和网络化服务系统等领域展示了实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代服务系统（如云数据中心、网约车平台）在巨大规模下运行时，拥塞是关键挑战。传统基于过程级收敛（流体极限和扩散极限）的分析方法技术密度高，且对有限规模实际系统的指导有限。因此，需要一种统一、易处理且能提供非渐近性能保证的稳态分析框架。

Method: 变换方法直接处理极限前（pre-limit）系统，通过利用指数检验函数的零漂移特性，为队列长度分布的变换（如矩母函数）推导出显式的泛函方程，这些方程作为全局平衡方程的代理，从而绕过传统过程级收敛的技术障碍。

Result: 该方法提供了尖锐的非渐近性能保证，产生可直接关联系统参数的可解释界限。自2020年提出以来，已成功扩展至客户放弃、状态相关到达、马尔可夫调制到达、大规模系统以及具有多个瓶颈的多维网络等现实复杂场景。

Conclusion: 变换方法为随机处理与匹配网络的稳态分析提供了一种统一且易处理的框架，作为纯仿真方法或纯渐近方法的强大分析替代方案，适用于系统设计与控制。其在实际系统中的可解释性和可操作性使其具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Modern service systems -- ranging from cloud data centers to ride-hailing platforms -- operate at massive scales where congestion is a critical challenge. Utilizing an operations research approach, these systems are analyzed by modeling them as complex stochastic processes, which are typically understood through process-level convergence to fluid and diffusion limits. However, these methods often prove technically dense and provide limited guidance for finite, practical system scales. The transform method, presented in this tutorial, was recently developed as a unified and tractable framework for the steady-state analysis of Stochastic Processing and Matching Networks (SPNs/SMNs). The transform method overcomes the technical hurdles of process-level convergence by working directly with the pre-limit system. By exploiting the zero-drift property of exponential test functions, the method derives explicit functional equations (acting as a proxy for global balance equations) for the transforms (such as moment-generating functions) of queue-length distributions. This approach provides sharp, non-asymptotic performance guarantees, bridging the gap between theoretical asymptotics and real-world system behavior.
  Since its introduction in 2020 for load-balancing in data center networks, the transform method has been extended to handle realistic complexities, including customer abandonment, state-dependent arrivals, Markov-modulated arrivals, large-system scale, and multi-dimensional networks with multiple bottlenecks. We survey these theoretical advances and demonstrate their practical relevance across diverse domains, such as matching markets and networked service systems. The transform method provides interpretable bounds tied directly to system parameters, offering a powerful analytical alternative to simulation-heavy or purely asymptotic approaches for system design and control.

</details>


### [296] [A Resolution of the SS--RS--GD Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22620)
*Binghui Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了Yun, Sra和Jadbabaie (COLT 2021)提出的关于单次洗牌SGD、随机重排SGD和梯度下降算子范数排序的SS-RS-GD不等式猜想。结果表明SS-RS不等式不成立（存在反例），而RS-GD不等式在特定条件下成立。证明过程借助了GPT-5.5 Pro的辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Yun, Sra和Jadbabaie在COLT 2021上提出的公开问题：对于良态对称矩阵，单次洗牌SGD、随机重排SGD和梯度下降的期望迭代算子范数是否存在单调递增的排序关系。

Method: 通过构造反例证明SS-RS不等式不成立（n=3, K=2, d=4，条件数任意接近1的PSD矩阵）；通过理论分析证明RS-GD不等式成立（在特定条件(1-1/(4n²+1))I ≤ A_i ≤ I下）。证明过程借助GPT-5.5 Pro扩展提示完成。

Result: SS-RS不等式不成立：存在条件数任意接近1的PSD矩阵使得||W_ss|| > ||W_rs||。RS-GD不等式成立：对所有满足条件的对称矩阵A_i，有||W_rs|| ≤ ||W_gd||。

Conclusion: 部分解决了公开猜想，否定了SS-RS部分，确认了RS-GD部分，并展示了AI辅助数学证明的潜力。

Abstract: Yun, Sra, and Jadbabaie (COLT 2021, open question) conjectured the SS--RS--GD inequalities: for well-conditioned symmetric matrices $A_1,\dots,A_n$, the operators $W_{ss}$, $W_{rs}$, and $W_{gd}$ that encode the expected iterate of single-shuffle SGD, random-reshuffle SGD, and gradient descent on a quadratic finite sum should satisfy \[
  \|W_{ss}\|\le \| W_{rs}\|\le \|W_{gd}\|. \] The conjecture is resolved,
  $\bullet$ SS-RS inequality fails. Already for $n=3$, $K=2$, and $d=4$, we exhibit explicit PSD matrices whose condition number is arbitrarily close to $1$, yet $\|W_{ss}\|>\|W_{rs}\|$.
  $\bullet$ RS-GD inequality holds. For every symmetric $A_i$ with $\bigl(1-\frac1{4n^2+1}\bigr)I\preceq A_i\preceq I$, one has $\|W_{rs}\|\le\|W_{gd}\|$.
  The proof was found via GPT-5.5 Pro extended prompted by the author.

</details>


### [297] [Resilience Enhancement of Electricity-Computing Interdependency System against Contingency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22623)
*Minqiao Zheng,Zejun Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对电力和计算能力在AI时代高度相互依赖但被低估的问题，提出了电力-计算相互依赖系统（ECIS），将数据中心建模为将电能转化为算力的设施，并提出了考虑电力约束、算力供给和基础设施相互依赖的恢复优化策略，案例表明该策略可动态调整数据中心电力配额，在电力短缺时提升系统韧性超过20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力和计算能力之间的相互依赖关系在正常和恢复运行中被严重低估，需要量化这种依赖并制定相应的恢复策略。

Method: 开发了电力-计算相互依赖系统（ECIS），将数据中心建模为电能到算力的转换设施，提出考虑电力约束、算力供给和基础设施相互依赖的恢复优化策略。

Result: 所提策略可根据系统状态动态调整数据中心电力配额，在电力短缺时系统韧性提升超过20%。

Conclusion: 通过动态调整数据中心电力配额来改善系统韧性的方法有效，可显著提升应对电力短缺的能力。

Abstract: Electrical power and computing power are highly interdependent especially due to the AI revolution. This dependency is largely underestimated during normal and recovery operation. To address this issue, an Electricity-Computing Interdependency System (ECIS) is developed in this paper, which first quantifies the interdependencies between ECIS and other critical infrastructures. The data center (DC) is modeled as a facility that transforms electrical power into computing power, and a recovery optimization strategy for ECIS is then proposed considering electrical power constraints, computing power supply, and infrastructure interdependencies. The case study demonstrates that the proposed strategy dynamically adjusts the DC electrical power quota according to system states, improving system resilience by more than 20% during power shortage.

</details>


### [298] [Spectral Analysis of Heavy-Ball Q-value Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22627)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从切换线性系统(SLS)和联合谱半径(JSR)角度分析了动量Q值迭代(heavy-ball QVI)的收敛性与加速条件，并给出了其优于标准QVI的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管值迭代的加速研究已很广泛，但针对控制任务中heavy-ball QVI的加速研究较少，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将heavy-ball QVI转化为精确的切换线性系统(SLS)，利用联合谱半径(JSR)理论分析其收敛性，并在JSR框架下研究其比标准QVI更快的条件。

Result: 成功将heavy-ball QVI建模为SLS，并基于JSR给出了收敛性分析与加速条件的理论刻画。

Conclusion: 基于JSR的理论框架可以有效解释heavy-ball QVI的收敛行为，并明确其加速条件。

Abstract: We study the convergence and acceleration of Q-value iteration (QVI) with momentum, or heavy-ball QVI. Although acceleration of value iteration has been studied extensively, there has been less work on the acceleration of heavy-ball QVI for control tasks. We analyze heavy-ball QVI from the viewpoint of switched linear system (SLS) theory and the joint spectral radius (JSR). First, we convert heavy-ball QVI into an exact SLS and interpret its convergence through the JSR. We also study, in JSR-based terms, conditions under which heavy-ball QVI can be faster than standard QVI.

</details>


### [299] [Network analysis reveals phase transitions in agro-food nitrogen systems with contrasting feeding capacity and land requirement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22628)
*Adrien Fauste-Gay,Jeremie Unterberger,Olivier Vidal,Lauriane Mouysset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于网络的建模框架，用于表征农业-食品氮系统的替代稳态构型，并识别这些构型之间的相变。该模型将生产、分配、回收和损失表示为质量守恒流，通过粗粒化程序识别主导回收循环。以法国参考数据为参数，通过案例研究识别出不同的稳态构型及其相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化模型或情景方法仅返回单一可行构型，对系统性能提供估计，但关于可行氮循环构型的信息有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，开发能够表征多种可行氮系统构型并识别构型间相变的方法。

Method: 构建连续时间区室网络模型，将生产、分配、回收和损失表示为质量守恒流。在稳态条件下分析系统，采用粗粒化程序识别主导回收循环并推导聚合指标描述系统构型。模型基于法国参考数据参数化，并应用于施肥、畜牧和饮食模式的案例研究。

Result: 识别出氮网络的多个不同的稳态构型，由主导回收结构中的相变分隔。法国参考系统中，农田基础喂养能力约为每公顷耕地8.7人，严格饮食自主需要约1970万公顷农业用地。减少合成施肥而不改变饮食或氮源会导致更高的土地需求或更大的外部投入依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种方法，用于映射可行的氮系统构型，并在不同参数和约束下分析农业-食品氮网络中的相变。

Abstract: Agro-food transitions are commonly assessed using optimization models or scenario approaches that return a single feasible configuration. These approaches provide estimates of system performance but limited information on feasible nitrogen cycling configurations. In this study, we develop a network-based modelling framework to characterize alternative steady-state configurations of agro-food nitrogen systems and identify phase transitions between these configurations.
  We formulate a continuous-time compartmental network model in which production, allocation, recycling, and losses are represented as mass-conserving flows. The system is analyzed under steady-state conditions. A coarse-graining procedure identifies dominant recycling cycles and derives aggregate indicators describing system configuration. The model is parameterized using French reference data and applied to case studies covering fertilization, livestock, and dietary patterns.
  The analysis identifies distinct steady-state configurations of the nitrogen network, separated by phase transitions in dominant recycling structures. These transitions correspond to changes in system performance indicators. For the French reference system, cropland-based feeding capacity is about 8.7 people per hectare of cropland (depending on constraints), while strict dietary autonomy requires about 19.7 million hectares of agricultural land. Reducing synthetic fertilization without changes in diet or nitrogen sourcing leads to higher land requirements or greater dependence on external inputs.
  The framework provides a method for mapping feasible nitrogen system configurations and analyzing phase transitions in agro-food nitrogen networks under alternative parameters and constraints.

</details>


### [300] [Modelling the Correlation Structure of Uncertain Input Parameters for Energy System Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22630)
*Klara Maggauer,Yannick Werner,Stefan Strömer,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于copula的统计依赖性建模工作流，用于表征能源系统优化模型中输入参数间的线性和单调相关性，并以奥地利2019-2025年太阳能发电、日前电价和电力需求数据验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机能源系统优化模型中输入参数间的统计依赖性常被忽略，这可能导致结果产生实质性偏差，需要开发系统性框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出基于copula的工作流，用于识别、表征和建模输入参数间的线性和单调相关结构，采用奥地利实际数据（太阳能发电、日前电价、电力需求）进行验证。

Result: 验证了变量间存在显著的线性和单调依赖性，且copula方法能够有效捕获这些依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作流是可扩展的基础框架，未来可向时变依赖和高维依赖建模方向拓展，以构建完整的统计依赖性框架。

Abstract: Statistical dependence among uncertain input parameters in stochastic energy system optimization models is often ignored, even though this can substantially bias outcomes. To address this gap, we are developing a comprehensive framework for characterizing, modelling, and benchmarking statistical dependence. In this work, we present a copula-based workflow to identify, characterize, and model linear and monotonic correlation structures between input parameters, representing the first development step towards this framework. We demonstrate our workflow using solar generation, day-ahead electricity prices, and electricity demand data in Austria between 2019 and 2025. Our results show substantial linear and monotonic dependence between these variables, and that this dependence is well captured by the copula-based approach. Finally, building on this scalable foundation, we highlight key levers for next steps towards the full framework, including time-dependent and higher-dimensional dependence modelling.

</details>


### [301] [Mean-field model for pollution abatement via cap and trade mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22638)
*Ofelia Bonesini,Giacomo Lanaro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个在AK生产技术和排放约束下的均值场竞争企业模型，监管者通过动态拍卖机制控制排放许可供给，在平均场控制框架下求解最优政策，并利用线性二次结构和耦合FBSDEs/Riccati方程刻画均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排放交易系统(ETS)背景下，如何设计监管者的最优许可分配政策，以在减少累计排放和维持总产出之间取得平衡，同时确保市场效率。

Method: 采用平均场控制(MFC)框架，利用线性二次结构(linear-quadratic)和共同噪声(common noise)，将均衡刻画为一组耦合的前向后随机微分方程(FBSDEs)及相应的Riccati方程。

Result: 得到了大型经济体中最优许可分配政策的可处理解析刻画，揭示了排放交易机制设计的洞见。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计高效排放交易机制提供了理论依据，监管者通过动态拍卖调节许可供给可实现减排与产出的最优平衡。

Abstract: We consider a mean-field model of competitive firms operating under an AK production technology, where output is proportional to capital and production generates emissions. In this setting, we introduce a regulator whose objective is the reduction of cumulative emissions, in the spirit of Emission Trading Systems (ETS), where firms must hold and trade permits to cover their emissions in a regulated market. The regulator acts as a central planner and controls the supply of permits, balancing emission reduction and aggregate output. Permits are allocated through a dynamic auction mechanism that adjusts supply in real time to achieve both market efficiency and the regulator's long-term goals. The regulator and the firms interact through the endogenous permit price, which is determined by the regulator's policy and affects firms' optimal strategies via the market clearing condition. The regulator's optimal policy is derived within a Mean-Field Control (MFC) framework. Exploiting the linear-quadratic structure and the presence of common noise, we characterise the equilibrium via a system of coupled FBSDEs and associated Riccati equations. Our results provide a tractable characterisation of optimal permit allocation policies in large economies and offer insights into the design of efficient emission trading mechanisms.

</details>


### [302] [Upper and lower approximations of the second order. Exhausters of the second order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22647)
*I. M. Prudnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文定义了二阶方向可微函数的上、下近似，引入了二阶穷举子（Exhausters），给出了二阶Fr'echet次微分与二阶穷举子的关系，为Lipschitz函数构建了一阶和二阶穷举子的规则，并给出了基于穷举子的极值充分必要条件，讨论了穷举子在优化中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更深入地分析非光滑函数在极值点附近的局部行为，传统的次微分工具（如一阶Fr'echet次微分）在刻画高阶信息时存在局限。因此，需要引入二阶分析工具——二阶穷举子，以更精确地描述函数的局部极值条件。

Method: 首先定义了二阶方向可微函数的上、下二阶近似，并由此引入了二阶穷举子的概念。然后建立了二阶穷举子与二阶Fr'echet次微分之间的理论联系。针对Lipschitz函数，给出了构造一阶和二阶穷举子的具体规则。最后利用穷举子推导出极值存在的必要与充分条件。

Result: 成功建立了二阶穷举子的理论框架，明确了其与二阶Fr'echet次微分的关系，给出了针对Lipschitz函数的穷举子构造方法，并推导出基于穷举子的极值充分必要条件。

Conclusion: 二阶穷举子是一种有效的非光滑优化分析工具，能够扩展传统次微分方法的分析能力，为优化问题中的极值条件提供更丰富的刻画。

Abstract: The upper and lower approximations of the second order are defined for a function differentiable in the directions. Exhausters of the second order are introduced. The relation between the second-order Frechet subdifferentials and the second-order exhausters is given. A rule for constructing exhausters of the first and second orders is given for Lipschitz functions.Necessary and sufficient extremum conditions through exhausters are given. It is discussed about the use of Exhausters in optimization.

</details>


### [303] [Modulus of conically averaged mappings and its applications to angles between two subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22650)
*Honglin Luo,Shuang Song,Xianfu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了锥平均映射的模（modulus of conical averagedness）来对锥平均映射进行分类，研究了其与广义单调映射的单调值/共单调值之间的联系，在线性情形下完全刻画了锥平均矩阵并给出模的显式公式，并推广到非线性情形，包括次凸函数的近端映射与反射映射的锥平均性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥平均映射作为平均映射的推广在优化算法中广泛应用，但缺乏对其分类与刻画的系统理论工具，特别是缺乏衡量其"平均程度"的模的概念及其与单调性之间的联系。

Method: 提出锥平均映射的模作为分类指标；引入广义单调映射的单调值与共单调值；在线性情形下对锥平均矩阵进行完全刻画并推导模的显式公式；将线性结果推广到非线性情形，研究次凸函数的近端映射与反射映射的锥平均性。

Result: 获得了锥平均矩阵的完整刻画及模的显式计算公式；建立了锥平均模与单调值/共单调值之间的理论联系；计算了Dixmier角和Friedrichs角作为应用；将线性结果成功推广到非线性情形，并证明了次凸函数的近端映射与反射映射具有锥平均性。

Conclusion: 锥平均映射的模是有效的分类工具，线性情形下的显式公式便于实际计算，非线性推广拓展了理论适用范围，为优化算法中锥平均映射的分析提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Conically averaged mappings, a generalization of averaged mappings, are important in a wide range of Optimization Algorithms. In this paper, we propose the modulus of conical averagedness to classify conical averaged mappings. Introducing the monotone and comonotone values of generalized monotone mappings, we investigate their connections to the modulus of conical averagedness. In the linear setting, we completely characterize conically averaged matrices, and derive explicit and pleasing formulae for computing their modulus of averagedness. As applications, we compute the Dixmier and Friedrichs angles between two subspaces. Nonlinear results are established as extensions of the linear case. Conical averagedness of proximal and reflection mappings of hypoconvex functions are also studied.

</details>


### [304] [Variational Formulations for Fractional Integral and Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22659)
*Delfim F. M. Torres*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分数阶线性方程 L^αu = f，构造了一个对称双线性形式，使得分数阶算子 L^α 在该双线性形式下具有对称性，进而定义了分数阶变分泛函，证明了原方程的解是该泛函的临界点。对于分数阶积分方程，该双线性形式非退化，所有临界点都是原方程的解；对于分数阶微分方程，则与最小二乘法建立了联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分数阶线性方程建立变分框架，使分数阶算子在该框架下具有对称性，从而将求解问题转化为变分问题。

Method: 构造一个合适的对称双线性形式，使得分数阶算子 L^α 相对于该双线性形式对称；基于该双线性形式定义分数阶变分泛函，并分析其临界点与方程解之间的关系。

Result: 对于分数阶积分方程，所构造的双线性形式非退化，所有临界点均为原方程的解；对于分数阶微分方程，该方法与最小二乘法相关联。

Conclusion: 通过构造对称双线性形式，成功将分数阶线性方程的求解问题转化为变分问题，为分数阶微分/积分方程提供了新的变分框架，并揭示了与最小二乘法的内在联系。

Abstract: Given a fractional-order linear equation $L^αu = f$, we define an appropriate symmetric bilinear form so that the fractional operator $L^α$ is symmetric with respect to that bilinear form. Using the bilinear form, we then define a functional of the fractional calculus of variations proving that the solutions of the given fractional-order equation are critical points of the fractional variational functional. In the case of fractional integral equations, the provided bilinear form is non-degenerate, and all critical points are solutions of the given equation. In the case of fractional differential equations, a relation with the least-squares method is obtained.

</details>


### [305] [Generalized Fine-Tuning of Diffusion Models via Stochastic Control and FBSDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22660)
*Zirui Wang,Lu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机控制视角的扩散模型广义微调框架，通过引入一般运行成本函数，统一了KL散度和最优传输度量，并用前向-后向随机微分方程系统刻画了值函数，最优控制具有非线性反馈形式，引入梯度依赖惩罚项，为分布约束下的扩散微调提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型微调方法多基于熵正则化框架，缺乏更一般的理论视角，无法统一处理KL散度和最优传输度量等不同分布约束下的微调问题。

Method: 从随机控制视角出发，引入一般运行成本诱导相对广义路径成本；推导完全非线性的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程；通过前向-后向随机微分方程系统刻画值函数；证明解的存在唯一性；推导出由BSDE梯度分量驱动的非线性反馈最优控制形式。

Result: 广义微调自然引入了一个额外的梯度依赖惩罚项，为在一般分布约束下的扩散微调提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了KL散度和最优传输度量等不同微调方式，具有更强的理论完备性和通用性。

Abstract: We propose a generalized fine-tuning framework for diffusion models from the perspective of stochastic control. Beyond entropy-regularized formulations, we introduce a general running cost that induces a relative generalized path cost, encompassing both Kullback-Leibler divergence and optimal transport metrics as special cases. This leads to a fully nonlinear Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. We characterize the value function through a forward-backward stochastic differential equation system and establish existence and uniqueness under standard regularity conditions. The optimal control admits a nonlinear feedback form driven by the backward stochastic differential equation gradient component. Finally, we show that generalized fine-tuning naturally introduces an additional gradient-dependent penalty, providing a unified framework for diffusion fine-tuning under general distributional constraints.

</details>


### [306] [The Capacitated $p$-Location Problem with Territorial Coverage Constraint: Efficient Solution and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22666)
*Felipe Albuquerque,Rosa Figueiredo,Cyrille Genre-Grandpierre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带容量约束的p-选址问题（C$p$LP）及其考虑公平性的扩展模型，提出了包含 территори覆盖约束的变体（C$p$LP-TC）和多尺度变体（C$p$LP-MTC），并开发了随机采样空间投票（RSSV）数学启发式算法，实验证明了其高效性和公平约束的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的p-中位问题（$p$MP）未考虑设施容量约束，而带容量约束的p-选址问题（C$p$LP）虽引入了容量限制，但缺乏对空间公平性的考量。因此，本文旨在将公平性（以地域覆盖约束形式）融入C$p$LP模型，并开发高效求解方法。

Method: 1）提出C$p$LP-TC（单层地域覆盖约束）和C$p$LP-MTC（嵌套多尺度地域覆盖约束）两个公平性扩展模型；2）给出带有效不等式的强化整数线性规划（ILP） formulation；3）开发随机采样空间投票（RSSV）数学启发式算法，结合启发式约简与强化ILP求解。

Result: 在基准测试和真实实例上的计算实验表明，RSSV方法高效可靠，参数调整需求小；同时验证了地域公平约束对选址规划公平性的显著影响。

Conclusion: 本文成功将公平性约束引入容量约束选址问题，提出的模型和求解方法兼具灵活性与实用性，为面向公平的选址规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the Capacitated $p$-Location Problem (C$p$LP) and its extensions incorporating equity considerations. In location science, the $p$-Median problem ($p$MP) is a classical model which selects $p$ facilities from a finite set of candidates to serve a set of customers minimizing total allocation costs. The C$p$LP which includes the Capacitated p-Median Problem (C$p$MP) and its relaxed variant (C$p$MP$^r$), extends the $p$MP by incorporating capacity constraints on facilities.We first introduce the C$p$LP with Territorial Coverage Constraints (C$p$LP-TC), a extension of the CpLP that enforces equity considering the spatial units. Building on this, we propose the multi-scale variant (C$p$LP-MTC), which generalizes territorial coverage constraints across nested spatial scales, allowing equity to be enforced simultaneously at multiple levels of spatial aggregation. A strengthened Integer Linear Programming (ILP) formulation with valid inequalities is presented. In addition, we develop the Random Sampling Spatial Voting (RSSV) matheuristic, which integrates a heuristic reduction phase with the strengthened ILP. The method is flexible, requires minimal parameter tuning, and remains accessible to non specialist users. Computational experiments on benchmark and real world instances demonstrate its efficiency and reliability. The results also demonstrate the impact of equity territorial based constraints on fairness in location planning.

</details>


### [307] [Kernel-Type Fractional Extensions of Reverse Callebaut, Rogers Hölder and Cauchy Schwarz Inequalities on Time Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22670)
*Nimai Sarkar,Gobinda Ghosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在时间尺度上建立了核型分数阶反向Callebaut不等式、反向Rogers-Hölder不等式和反向Cauchy-Schwarz不等式，这些结果通过与非负核算子及diamond-α积分结合，统一了连续、离散、量子及分数阶动态设定下的反向不等式，并可退化为经典形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是在时间尺度框架下，利用核型分数阶扩展统一处理反向不等式，以包含记忆效应，并在连续、离散、量子及分数阶动态设定中建立统一的理论基础。

Method: 通过引入非负核算子与diamond-α积分，构造核型分数阶扩展形式，将经典反向Callebaut、Rogers-Hölder和Cauchy-Schwarz不等式推广到时间尺度上；通过选取特定核函数和时间尺度，退化为对应经典形式。

Result: 得到了核型分数阶反向Callebaut、Rogers-Hölder和Cauchy-Schwarz不等式，包括分数阶反向Cauchy-Schwarz不等式和加权反向Rogers-Hölder不等式等特例。

Conclusion: 这些结果为Volterra型和时滞动态方程的先验估计提供了统一的分析工具，并展示了在连续、离散、量子及分数阶动态设定下的广泛适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish kernel-type fractional extensions of reverse Callebaut, Rogers--Hölder and Cauchy--Schwarz inequalities on time scales. The proposed results are formulated by means of a nonnegative kernel operator associated with the diamond-$α$ integral, which enables the inclusion of memory effects within the framework of dynamic inequalities. By choosing particular kernels and time scales, the obtained inequalities reduce to their continuous, discrete and quantum counterparts. Moreover, when the kernel is taken as the identity kernel, the results recover the corresponding classical diamond-$α$ dynamic inequalities. Several consequences are derived as special cases, including fractional reverse Cauchy--Schwarz inequalities and weighted reverse Rogers--Hölder inequalities. The results provide a unified approach for studying reverse inequalities in continuous, discrete, quantum and fractional dynamic settings and may be applied to obtain a priori estimates for Volterra-type and delay dynamic equations.

</details>


### [308] [A QUBO-Based Optimization Framework for ATM Cash Replenishment Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22675)
*Riccardo Aiolfi,Giulia Montani,Valeria Zaffaroni,Luca Toffanetti,Davide Veronelli,Francesca Cibrario,Davide Caputo,Davide Corbelletto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于QUBO模型的ATM现金补货优化方法，利用GPU加速求解器MegaQUBO，在意大利276台ATM的真实数据集上实现约15%-18%的成本降低，同时维持99.8%-99.9%的高服务水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATM网络现金补货管理需要在不确定且时变的需求下调度补货，以最小化运营成本并维持高服务水平、避免现金耗尽，现有方法难以有效处理非线性成本交互和运营约束。

Method: 将ATM现金补货问题建模为二次无约束二元优化(QUBO)模型，通过惩罚项纳入运营约束，目标函数结合固定和可变补货成本、同地点折扣及延迟补货(导致服务中断)的惩罚，使用GPU加速的QUBO求解器MegaQUBO求解。

Result: 在意大利276台ATM的2022年四个月真实数据集上，该方法相比基于阈值的基线策略实现约15%-18%的成本削减，同时保持约99.8%-99.9%的平均服务水平。

Conclusion: 基于QUBO的优化与GPU求解相结合，可为大规模ATM现金物流提供实际可部署的决策支持工具。

Abstract: The management of cash replenishment in Automated Teller Machine (ATM) networks requires scheduling recharges in order to minimize operational costs while maintaining high service levels and avoiding cash-outs, under uncertain and time-varying withdrawal demand. This work formulates the ATM cash replenishment problem through a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model, which naturally captures nonlinear cost interactions, while incorporating operational constraints through penalty terms. The objective function combines fixed and variable replenishment costs with co-location discounts, as well as penalties for a late replenishment that could cause a service interruption. The resulting QUBO instances are solved using MegaQUBO, a GPU-accelerated QUBO solver. An empirical evaluation on a real dataset of 276 ATMs located in Italy, covering four representative months of 2022 (April, May, October, and November), benchmarks the proposed approach against a threshold-based operational policy. Results show consistent cost reductions of approximately 15%-18% while maintaining an excellent average service level (around 99.8%-99.9). Overall, the study demonstrates that QUBO-based optimization, coupled with GPU-based solving, can provide a practically deployable decision-support tool for large-scale ATM cash logistics.

</details>


### [309] [Convexifying Mean-Field Control: An Occupation-Measure and Frank-Wolfe Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22678)
*Di Yu,Sixiong You,Chaoying Pei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于占位测度空间的Frank-Wolfe优化框架来求解大规模机器人集群的均场控制问题，该方法避免了状态空间离散化，保持了O(1/k)收敛率，可自然处理交互和安全约束，并在三维多障碍物环境中实现了分钟级求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人集群需要可扩展的均场控制方法，但经典PDE方法在高维计算困难，机器学习方法虽可扩展但牺牲了近似保证。

Method: 将MFC问题提升到占位测度空间，形成测度上的凸优化松弛问题，采用Frank-Wolfe算法在测度空间中迭代求解，每一步迭代简化为一个可处理的最优控制问题。

Result: 在二维环境中与解析解和PDE基线方法吻合；可扩展到含多个障碍物的三维环境，而标准网格PDE求解器不可行；含十个障碍物的完整三维实例在工作站上数分钟内求解完成。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持收敛保证的同时实现了高维可扩展性，验证了方法的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large-scale robotic swarms motivate the use of mean-field control (MFC). Classical partial differential equation (PDE)-based formulations provide a principled framework but can become computationally challenging in higher dimensions, whereas machine learning achieves scalability at the cost of approximation and guarantees. In this work, we establish an optimization-based framework that lifts the MFC problem into the space of occupation measures, resulting in a convex relaxation formulated as an optimization over measures. The resulting problem is solved using a Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm in the measure space, with each iteration reduced to a tractable optimal control problem. This approach retains the O(1/k) convergence rate of FW, avoids discretization of the state space, and naturally incorporates interaction and safety constraints. Numerical experiments demonstrate agreement with analytic and PDE-based baselines in two dimensions and show that the method scales to three-dimensional environments with multiple obstacles, where standard grid-based PDE solvers become impractical. A full 3D instance with ten obstacles is solved in minutes on a standard workstation, underscoring the practicality and scalability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [310] [H$_1$-ISS Analysis and Boundary Control Design for Coupled Linear ODEs and Hyperbolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22881)
*Franthiescolly Vieira de Carvalho,Daniel Coutinho,Gustavo de Andrade,Christophe Prieur,Mohamed Maghenem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对由耦合线性ODE与齐次线性双曲PDE组成的分布参数系统，研究了边界控制器的设计问题，提出了基于Lyapunov的H₁范数ISS条件，并导出以线性矩阵不等式表示的控制设计条件，实现了闭环系统的渐近稳定性和扰动衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对ODE-PDE耦合系统的ISS条件多局限于较低范数空间，缺乏对H₁范数下系统状态有界性的保证，且边界扰动下的控制设计方法尚不完善。

Method: 将已有基于Lyapunov的ISS条件推广至H₁空间，在扰动输入温和假设下推导状态H₁范数的上界；进而将稳定分析与控制设计条件转化为线性矩阵不等式（LMI）形式。

Result: H₁范数下系统状态有界性得以保证，并给出了显式上界；LMI形式的控制设计条件可有效求解。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了ODE-PDE耦合边界控制系统的渐近稳定性与扰动衰减，数值算例验证了方法的有效性与潜力。

Abstract: This paper considers the general problem of the design of boundary controllers for distributed parameter systems. The control objectives are the asymptotic stability of the closed-loop system, as well as the disturbance attenuation of exogenous disturbances affecting the measurements and boundary conditions. More specifically, this work studies input-to-state stability (ISS) and stabilization for systems governed by coupled linear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) and homogeneous linear hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs), where actuation, sensing, and disturbance inputs are located at the system boundaries. First, we extend a previously established Lyapunov-based ISS condition for a similar class of systems to the H$_1$ setting under mild assumptions on the disturbance inputs. This extension not only ensures that the H$_1$-norm of the system states driven by non-vanishing disturbances and compatible initial conditions are bounded, but also provides an upper bound on the system H$_1$-norm. Subsequently, these theoretical results are applied to derive stability analysis and control design conditions expressed in terms of linear matrix inequalities. Numerical examples are provided to illustrate the effectiveness and potential of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [311] [Feedback stopping rules in path-dependent controller-stopper games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22882)
*Magnus Perninge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时间水平下的零和控制器-停止者博弈，通过非线性Snell包络表示和首次接触原理，证明了博弈存在价值，并给出了路径依赖系统的最优反馈停止规则，同时将该规则应用于非零和博弈的ε-纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立零和控制器-停止者博弈的价值存在性，并解决路径依赖系统中随机微分方程退化的情形。

Method: 基于非线性Snell包络表示，扩展首次接触原理，采用纯概率方法。

Result: 证明了博弈存在价值，并得到了路径依赖系统的最优反馈停止规则。

Conclusion: 零和博弈的最优反馈停止规则可作为非零和博弈中任意ε>0下的ε-纳什均衡的一个组成部分。

Abstract: We investigate finite-horizon, zero-sum controller-stopper games in which the stopper observes the state process and implements a feedback stopping rule. Building on a nonlinear Snell envelope representation for a related game established in a companion paper, we prove that our game admits a value by extending the associated first-contact principle. Our approach is purely probabilistic and yields an optimal feedback stopping rule for path-dependent systems while allowing for degeneracy in the underlying stochastic differential equation (SDE).
  As an application, we consider nonzero-sum controller-stopper games and show that the optimal feedback stopping rule for a zero-sum game appears as a component of a $\varepsilon$-Nash equilibrium for every $\varepsilon>0$.

</details>


### [312] [Curvature Recycling Douglas-Rachford Splitting: Transported Quasi-Newton Models for Expensive Smooth Proximal Subproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22895)
*Musa Maharramov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率回收Douglas-Rachford分裂算法(CR-DRS)，通过传输旧残差、重用拟牛顿模型来加速DRS中昂贵的平滑近端求解过程，在强凸性和光滑性条件下实现了全局线性收敛和局部超线性收敛，在ℓ1和全变分正则化问题上显著减少了昂贵梯度调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRS方法需要求解一系列中心不同但非线性曲率共享的平滑近端子问题，每次求解都涉及昂贵的梯度计算。现有方法（如重启拟牛顿或仅利用曲率信息）效率不高，亟需一种能够有效重用先前计算信息以减少昂贵梯度调用的方法。

Method: 提出CR-DRS算法，核心包括：(1) 将旧的精确残差传输到新的近端中心；(2) 重用拟牛顿模型（配对直接/逆BFGS和受保护的SR1实现）；(3) 在计算第一个新梯度之前移动旧的近端状态。认证变体通过下降测试和残差测试决定是否接受传输的拟牛顿步，否则执行固定次数的安全梯度步。

Result: 在强凸性和光滑性条件下，小增益条件保证了全局线性收敛且每外迭代只需常数次新梯度；传输的Dennis-Moré条件给出了局部超线性收敛速度。在ℓ1正则化和全变分正则化问题上，与重启拟牛顿和仅曲率拟牛顿近端求解相比，CR-DRS大幅减少了昂贵梯度的调用次数；在直接近端ℓ1模型上还优于FISTA。

Conclusion: CR-DRS通过有效重用历史曲率信息，在不牺牲收敛速度的前提下显著降低了DRS中昂贵梯度计算的成本，为处理f光滑昂贵、g非光滑近端便宜的复合优化问题提供了一种高效实用的求解框架。

Abstract: We consider \[
  \min_x f(x)+g(x), \] where $f$ is smooth but its value and gradient are expensive, while $g$ is nonsmooth and has a cheap proximal map. Douglas--Rachford splitting (DRS) then requires a sequence of smooth proximal solves. These solves have different centers but share the same nonlinear curvature.
  We introduce curvature-recycling DRS (CR-DRS). The method transports an old exact residual to the new proximal center, reuses a quasi-Newton model, and moves the old proximal state before the first new gradient is evaluated. We give paired direct and inverse BFGS and safeguarded symmetric-rank-one realizations. A certified variant accepts a transported quasi-Newton step only when it passes descent and residual tests; otherwise it uses a fixed number of safe gradient steps. Under strong convexity and smoothness, a small-gain condition gives global linear convergence with a constant number of new gradients per outer iteration. A transported Dennis--Moré condition gives local superlinear reduction of the new proximal error. We show that this condition is not automatic, derive full-model and active-subspace sufficient conditions, and relate the active subspace to the local DRS dynamics induced by the nonsmooth proximal map.
  Experiments on $\ell_1$- and total-variation-regularized problems show large reductions in expensive-gradient calls relative to restarted and curvature-only quasi-Newton proximal solves. For directly proximal $\ell_1$ models, we also compare with FISTA.

</details>


### [313] [Nesterov acceleration in optimizing over probability measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23008)
*Jiaqi Tang,Qin Li,Wilfrid Gangbo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率测度空间上的Heavy-ball和Nesterov加速方法，通过两种互补的提升程序（哈密顿量提升和希尔伯特空间提升）克服了动量定义和数值实现的困难，并建立了与欧几里得空间相匹配的非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nesterov加速梯度法从欧几里得空间扩展到概率测度空间，解决概率测度优化中缺乏加速收敛方法的空白。

Method: 引入两种互补的提升程序：(1) 哈密顿量提升——将概率测度提升到相空间，引入动量变量；(2) 希尔伯特空间提升——将概率测度提升到公共希尔伯特空间，恢复线性结构以实现收敛分析并得到可执行的粒子动力学。

Result: 建立了与欧几里得空间相匹配的非渐近收敛率，分别关于迭代次数和用于表示概率分布的粒子数量。

Conclusion: 两种互补的提升程序为在概率测度空间上设计、分析和实现基于动量的加速优化方法提供了系统方法论。

Abstract: Optimization over probability measures has become an increasingly important paradigm in modern machine learning, scientific computing, and uncertainty quantification. Motivated by Nesterov's accelerated gradient method in Euclidean space, we develop Heavy-ball and Nesterov acceleration methods over the probability measure space $\mathcal{P}_2$ and establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees that match their Euclidean counterparts. In particular, we derive convergence rates with respect to both the number of iterations and the number of particles used to represent the underlying probability distributions.
  Extending accelerated optimization from Euclidean space to probability measures is challenging. The natural notion of momentum requires concepts such as tangent bundles of the set of probability space and they are hard to operate numerically. To overcome these difficulties, we introduce two complementary lifting procedures. The first lifts probability measures to phase space through a Hamiltonian formulation, introducing momentum variables into the dynamics. The second lifts probability measures to a common Hilbert space, restoring the linear structure required for convergence analysis while simultaneously yielding executable particle dynamics. Together, these two complementary lifting procedures provide a systematic methodology for designing, analyzing, and implementing momentum-based accelerated optimization methods over probability measure spaces.

</details>


### [314] [Output Regulation for Linear Parabolic Systems Using Finite-Dimensional Tracking-Error-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23032)
*Bingsen Li,Emilia Fridman,George Weiss*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对1-D扩散反应系统，提出了一种基于有限维跟踪误差的调节器设计方法，用于解决由不稳定外系统生成扰动和参考信号下的输出调节问题。通过模态分解、观测器设计和LMI条件，将问题转化为部分镇定问题，并分析了时变测量延迟下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不稳定外系统驱动下1-D扩散反应系统的输出调节问题，此前方法多针对稳定外系统或需要更复杂的控制器结构。

Method: 基于调节方程推导组合系统，将输出调节转化为部分镇定问题；采用模态分解法，在传递零点与可观测性条件下刻画截断模态的可观测性；设计全阶观测器（非仅依赖不稳定模态），通过LMI确定观测器维度并保证范数一致有界；分析未知时变测量延迟的影响。

Result: 观测器增益可设计为全阶（维度充分大时LMI可行），且其范数在观测器维度上一致有界；数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文方法为不稳定外系统驱动的1-D扩散反应系统提供了一种可构造的有限维输出调节方案，并能处理时变测量延迟，拓展了输出调节理论在分布参数系统中的应用。

Abstract: This paper addresses the output regulation problem for 1-D diffusion-reaction system, where both the disturbance and reference signals are generated by an unstable exosystem. We propose a constructive approach to the design of a finite-dimensional tracking error-based regulator for this class of possibly unstable systems. Based on the regulator equations, a combined plant is derived, converting the output regulation problem into a partial stabilization problem for the combined system. Using the modal decomposition method, the observability of the truncated modes is characterized under appropriate transmission zeros and observability conditions. For the controller design, unlike in the standard stabilization case, where the observer gain is dependent on the unstable modes only, the observer gain here has full order due to the coupling introduced by the exosystem. We prove that the observer gain can be designed such that its norm remains uniformly bounded with respect to the dimension of the observer. LMI-based conditions are provided for determining the observer dimension, and it is shown that the LMI is feasible for a sufficiently large dimension. Finally, the output regulation problem in the presence of unknown time-varying measurement delays is analyzed. Numerical examples are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [315] [Optimization of Kinetic--Stoichiometric Growth Bounds over Autocatalytic Subnetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23107)
*Víctor Blanco,Gabriel González*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在化学反应系统中，如何选择最大化生长界（结合结构放大与动力学效率）的自催化子网络，提出了一种混合整数双线性优化模型及精确参数化算法，在多个经典反应网络中验证了该方法与仅最大化结构放大因子的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅通过最大化结构放大因子（MAF）来识别自催化子网络，但结构放大本身无法决定生长潜力，因为高放大的子网络可能动力学效率低下。作者旨在结合动力学与化学计量约束，选择理论上生长潜力最高的子网络。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数双线性优化模型，结合超图选择与广义分数放大约束；利用动力学范数的离散结构，设计了一种精确参数化算法，每个子问题简化为MAF最大化，并通过Dinkelbach型广义分数规划方法求解。

Result: 在Oregonator、formose反应、碳固定循环及合成基准网络上的应用表明，最大化动力学-化学计量界与单独最大化MAF的结果存在差异，揭示了化学计量放大、反应动力学与平衡生长之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 提出的框架提供了一种识别具有最高理论生长潜力的反应子网络的精确优化方法论。

Abstract: Autocatalytic subnetworks govern sustained production and balanced growth in chemical reaction systems and can be naturally represented as directed multihypergraphs that jointly encode stoichiometric structure and reaction kinetics. Existing optimization approaches identify such subnetworks by maximizing their structural amplification, typically measured through the Maximum Amplification Factor (MAF). However, structural amplification alone does not determine growth potential, since highly amplifying subnetworks may be kinetically inefficient. Motivated by recent kinetic--stoichiometric bounds on balanced growth under scalable dynamics, we study the problem of selecting the autocatalytic subnetwork maximizing the growth bound $(α^*-1)\|\mathbb{S}|_{\mathcal A'}\|_κ$, where $α^*$ is the MAF and $\|\mathbb{S}|_{\mathcal A'}\|_κ$ is a kinetic consumption norm. We formulate the problem as a mixed-integer bilinear optimization model combining hypergraph selection with generalized fractional amplification constraints. Exploiting the discrete structure of the kinetic norm, we develop an exact parametric algorithm in which each subproblem reduces to MAF maximization and is solved through a Dinkelbach-type generalized fractional programming method. Finite convergence and global optimality are established. Applications to the Oregonator, the formose reaction, carbon-fixation cycles, and synthetic benchmark networks show when maximizing the kinetic--stoichiometric bound differs from maximizing the MAF alone, revealing the interplay between stoichiometric amplification, reaction kinetics, and balanced growth. The proposed framework provides an exact optimization methodology for identifying reaction subnetworks with the highest theoretical growth potential.

</details>


### [316] [A Novel Optimization Model for Solving the Timetabling Problem at Finnish Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23109)
*Kimmo Nurmi,Jari Kyngäs,Pauliina Halme*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了芬兰三所大学与教育管理系统提供商合作的优化项目，提出了CRONOPT模型，用于同时优化大学资源配置、教师工作负担与学生学业进展这三个利益相关方的目标，项目分为可行性研究、准备和实施三个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 芬兰大学面临排课问题中三方利益相关者（大学、教师、学生）偏好平衡的挑战，且随着大学演变和竞争加剧，排课问题特征已发生变化，需要一种全新的规划流程管理方法。

Method: 采用三阶段（可行性研究、准备、实施）的项目架构，提出CRONOPT模型，该模型同时针对三个目标进行优化：大学资源的有效利用、教师工作量与福祉、学生学业的顺利推进。优化方案直接集成到芬兰大学主流的教育管理系统中。

Result: 在可行性研究阶段识别出若干研究空白和实际目标，开发了CRONOPT模型，并与市场领先的教育管理系统实现集成。

Conclusion: 该项目不仅简化了排课表创建过程，还实现了对整个规划流程的全新管理方式，有望同时提升大学资源效率、教师工作满意度和学生学习体验。

Abstract: We study a timetabling problem faced by Finnish universities, where the challenge is to balance the preferences of three key stakeholders: the university, teachers, and students. Educational timetabling is an essential task that ensures that everything runs effortlessly and collaboratively. Its efficiency significantly impacts the smooth operation of the institution's educational programs and its financial success. As universities have evolved and competition among them has intensified, the features of the university timetabling problem have changed. This paper describes an ongoing optimization project with three Finnish universities and a leading provider of educational management systems for universities. The main goal of this cooperation is not merely to streamline the creation of timetables, but to enable an entirely new approach to manage the entire planning process. The project consists of three six-month phases: the feasibility study, the preparation phase, and the implementation. During the feasibility study, several research-related gaps and novel practical goals were identified. To address these gaps and goals, this paper presents a unique CRONOPT model for tackling three targets simultaneously: effective use of university resources, teacher workload and well-being, and smooth progression of students' studies. The optimization solution developed through the project integrates directly with the market-leading educational management system for Finnish universities.

</details>


### [317] [KAYROS: An Anytime and Exact Open-Source Solver for Duration-Minimization Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing. A Technical Report and a Case Study in Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23116)
*Florian Rascoussier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: KAYROS是一个开源求解器，用于带/不带时间窗的最小化时长时变车辆路径问题(TDVRPTW/TDVRP)，支持任意时刻输出渐近改进的解并给出可验证的最优性证明，且基于分段线性旅行时间函数而非时间离散化。论文同时引入Poryos2026基准测试集，包含1,080个实例。该工作也是人机协作的案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变车辆路径问题中旅行时间随出发时间变化，但此前缺乏同时具备任意时刻输出解、可证明最优性、开源、无专有依赖且支持丰富分段线性旅行时间函数的求解器，这限制了该领域研究的可重复性和实践应用。

Method: KAYROS扩展了Lera-Romero等人的开源分支定价切割求解器，增加了开放LP后端、任意时刻与热启动行为、校验器精确定价、以及阶跃旅行时间的精确处理。采用分支定价切割框架，通过时间依赖函数组合与精确求解技术实现。

Result: 在MAMUT-routing基准测试集上发布了468个最优性证明，每个需四个独立求解器一致同意；其中5个证明严格改进了已发表参考值。Poryos2026包含1,080个配对的CVRP/VRPTW/TDVRP/TDVRPTW实例，均提供校验器验证的最佳已知解。

Conclusion: KAYROS是首个同时具备任意时刻输出和精确最优性证明能力的开源TDVRP求解器；Poryos2026为时变路径问题提供了标准化的基准测试集。该工作证明了密集人机协作在保持结果独立可验证性的同时实现大规模研究的可行性。

Abstract: KAYROS is an open-source solver for duration-minimization time-dependent vehicle routing problems, with or without time windows (TDVRPTW, TDVRP). In these variants, travel times change with departure time, and each route's dispatch time is a decision. To the best of the author's knowledge, it is the first openly available solver that is both anytime, streaming improving solutions from the first seconds, and exact, proving optimality with publicly verifiable certificates, for these problems over rich piecewise-linear travel- time functions rather than a time discretization. It has no proprietary dependency and installs with one command. It builds on the state of the art for time-dependent function composition and exact solving, extending the open-source branch-price-and-cut solver of Lera-Romero, Miranda Bront and Soulignac (2020) with an open LP backend, anytime and warm- start behavior, checker-exact pricing, and exact treatment of stepwise travel times. On the MAMUT-routing benchmark collection, KAYROS stands behind 468 published optimality certificates, each requiring agreement among four independent solves, and five certificates strictly improve published reference values. The report also introduces Poryos2026, a benchmark family designed and generated by the author from real OpenStreetMap city road networks. Its 1,080 paired CVRP, VRPTW, TDVRP and TDVRPTW instances combine real road geometries with controlled synthetic demands, time windows and congestion. Every instance carries a checker-validated best-known solution. This report presents the solver, its certification protocol, the benchmark's generation and feasibility guarantees, and their experimental connection for a broad technical audience. It is also a case study in the intensive human-AI collaboration that made this body of work feasible while keeping its claims independently verifiable.

</details>


### [318] [Support profiles of full-capacity Pareto spectra of order three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23320)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三阶实矩阵在达到最大帕累托特征值数量（9个）时的支持结构分布，证明所有可能的支持分配模式仅有三种：(1,5,3)、(2,4,3)和(2,5,2)，并为每种模式构造了显式矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知n=3的实矩阵最多有9个不同的帕累托特征值，但达到该最大值时这些特征值如何由不同大小的支持（大小为1、2、3的支持）产生尚不清楚，本文旨在分类这些产生模式。

Method: 利用n=2问题的已知界，结合对单点支持、成对支持和全支持的多种限制条件（尤其是成对支持产生2个值时的图中不含三角形这一图论性质），推导出三种可能的数值分配模式，并为每种模式构造了显式的满容量矩阵。

Result: 证明了在达到9个不同帕累托特征值时，每种支持选择下，大小为1、2、3的支持产生的特征值数量分布只能是(1,5,3)、(2,4,3)或(2,5,2)三种情况，且每种模式都在一个非空欧几里得开集中可实现。

Conclusion: 完整分类了三阶矩阵达到最大帕累托特征值数量时的支持产生模式，为帕累托特征值理论提供了结构性刻画。

Abstract: For a given real matrix $A\in\R^{n\times n}$ of order $n\geq 1$, a Pareto eigenvalue is a scalar $λ\in\R$ for which there exists a nonzero vector $x\in\R^n_+$ such that $Ax-λx\in\R^n_+$ and $\langle x,Ax-λx\rangle=0$. It is knwon that a real matrix of order $n=3$ can have at most $9$ distinct Pareto eigenvalues. We study the matrices for which this maximal number is attained and classify the way in which the $9$ Pareto eigenvalues are produced by their supports.
  A Pareto eigenvalue may be produced by more than one support. We therefore choose one producing support for each distinct values. The main contribution of this paper is to prove that, for every such choice, the numbers of values assigned to supports of sizes $1$, $2$, and $3$ are necessarily $$
  (1,5,3),\qquad (2,4,3),\qquad\text{or}\qquad (2,5,2). $$ The proof uses bounds from the order $n=2$ problem and several restrictions on singleton, pair, and full supports. In particular, the graph formed by the pair supports producing $2$ values contains no triangle. These arguments also gives anohter proof that the maximum Pareto capacity in order $3$ is equal to $9$.
  For each of the $3$ profiles, we give an explicit full-capacity matrix with $9$ regular Pareto eigenvalues. We also prove that each profile occurs on a nonempty Euclidean-open set where every Pareto eigenvalue has a unique producing support.

</details>


### [319] [New Globalized Newton-Type Methods for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23433)
*Vo Thanh Phat,Tuyen Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的线搜索牛顿框架，用于无约束优化，通过仅在牛顿方向定义良好且合适时利用该方向，避免了反复的Hessian正则化。该框架统一了多种混合梯度-牛顿方法，并自然衍生出一种新的超梯度牛顿方法。在PLK条件下建立了全局收敛性，在适当正则性假设下证明了局部超线性和二次收敛，并首次将其应用于强拟凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局收敛的牛顿型方法通常要求目标函数为凸或强凸，非凸优化方法则需在每次迭代进行Hessian正则化，计算成本高。本文旨在设计一个避免反复Hessian正则化、同时保持全局收敛性的通用牛顿框架。

Method: 提出一种通用线搜索牛顿框架，仅在牛顿方向定义良好且合适时使用牛顿方向，否则切换到梯度方向。该框架通过线搜索机制保证全局收敛，包含多种混合梯度-牛顿方法作为特例，并衍生出新的超梯度牛顿方法。理论分析基于PLK条件建立全局收敛，并在正则性条件下证明局部超线性与二次收敛。

Result: 在PLK条件下建立了全局收敛性，允许孤立和非孤立聚点。在适当正则性假设下证明了局部超线性和二次收敛。首次为强拟凸优化这类非凸问题提供了牛顿型算法及完整的收敛性分析。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的通用线搜索牛顿框架有效解决了非凸优化中牛顿法的全局收敛问题，无需每次迭代进行Hessian正则化，并拓展了牛顿法在强拟凸优化这一重要非凸问题上的应用。

Abstract: Newton's method is one of the most effective second-order algorithms for smooth optimization because of its fast local convergence. However, existing globally convergent Newton-type methods typically require convexity or strong convexity of the objective function, while approaches for nonconvex optimization often rely on Hessian regularization at every iteration. In this paper, we propose a general line-search Newton framework for unconstrained optimization that avoids repeated Hessian regularization by exploiting the Newton direction only when it is well-defined and suitable. The proposed framework encompasses several existing hybrid gradient--Newton methods as special cases and naturally yields a new extragradient Newton method. We establish global convergence under mild assumptions, including the Polyak--Lojasiewicz--Kurdyka (PLK) condition, allowing both isolated and nonisolated accumulation points. We further prove local superlinear and quadratic convergence under appropriate regularity assumptions. Finally, we apply the proposed framework to strongly quasiconvex optimization and provide, to the best of our knowledge, the first Newton-type algorithm together with a comprehensive convergence analysis for this important class of nonconvex optimization problems. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [320] [On the Bellman equation in recursive stochastic dynamic programming with the CES aggregator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23475)
*Anna Jaśkiewicz,Andrzej S. Nowak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有CES聚合器和熵风险度量确定性等价的离散时间无限期马尔可夫决策过程，证明了贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性，以及Koopmans方程对任意平稳策略的唯一解，从而得出贝尔曼方程中可测最大值选择器即为最优平稳策略，并将方法推广到其他确定性等价情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对具有递归效用的动态规划模型的理论基础研究不足，尤其是在CES聚合器与熵风险度量相结合的情形下，贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性尚未得到充分证明。

Method: 在原始数据的温和条件下，通过不动点定理证明贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性；利用Koopmans方程证明任意给定平稳策略下的唯一解；通过可测选择器定理建立最优平稳策略的充分性条件。

Result: 证明了贝尔曼方程解的存在唯一性；证明了任意平稳策略下Koopmans方程的唯一解存在性；证明了可测最大值选择器是最优平稳策略。

Conclusion: 本文为具有CES递归效用和熵风险度量的无限期MDP奠定了坚实的理论基础，且方法可推广至风险中性等其他确定性等价情形。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate discrete-time infinite horizon Markov decision processes (dynamic programming models) with recursive utilities defined by the classical \emph{CES} aggregator and the certainty equivalent operator being the negative of the entropic risk measure. Under mild conditions on the primitive data we prove existence and uniqueness of a solution to the Bellman equation. Moreover, we show that for any given stationary policy, the Koopmans equation has a unique solution. These two facts imply that a measurable selector of maxima in the Bellman equation is an optimal stationary policy. The methods of our proofs are applied to study solutions to the Bellman equation for other certainty equivalents, including risk-neutral case.

</details>


### [321] [Convexity and SOS-Convexity of Sum of Separable and Biquadratic Quartic Polynomials and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23476)
*Shaon Naskar,Sujeet Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类名为SPBQ的四次多项式结构子类，系统分析了凸性与SOS凸性之间的关系。证明了当关联的双二次型为n×2大小时，每个凸SPBQ多项式必然是SOS凸的；但构造了一个3×3双二次型的反例，表明凸性不蕴含SOS凸性。此外，展示了SPBQ在无约束和有约束优化问题中的计算优势，并应用于凸多项式回归和流体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断四次及以上多元多项式的非负性和凸性是强NP难问题，为缓解计算困难，SOS凸性被提出作为一种可处理的代数松弛。本文旨在系统研究一类结构化四次多项式（SPBQ）中凸性与SOS凸性之间的关系，探索何时凸性等价于SOS凸性，并利用SPBQ结构实现计算优势。

Method: 定义SPBQ（Separable and Biquadratic之和）多项式子类，通过代数分析研究凸性与SOS凸性的关系。构造具体反例展示凸性不蕴含SOS凸性的情况，并将SPBQ应用于优化问题的SDP松弛以及凸多项式回归和流体动力学等实际场景。

Result: 对于n×2大小的双二次形式，每个凸SPBQ多项式必然是SOS凸的；但对于3×3双二次形式，存在凸但不SOS凸的SPBQ反例。SPBQ方法相比通用SOS方法在计算上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: SPBQ是一类兼具表达能力和计算效率的多项式子类，在凸性与SOS凸性之间建立了部分等价关系，并在实际应用中展示了优越的计算性能。

Abstract: Determining whether multivariate polynomials of degree four or higher are nonnegative and convex is a strongly NP-hard problem. To mitigate these computational difficulties, sum-of- squares (SOS) convexity has been proposed as a tractable algebraic relaxation that yields a checkable sufficient condition for convexity and can be expressed as a semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we introduce a structured subclass of quartic polynomials, called the Sum of Separable and Biquadratic (SPBQ) forms, and conduct a systematic analysis of the connection between convexity and SOS-convexity within this class. Specifically, we show that every convex SPBQ polynomial is necessarily SOS-convex when the associated biquadratic form has size n x 2. We then construct an explicit SPBQ example with a 3 x 3 biquadratic form that is convex but fails to be SOS-convex. Finally, we examine both unconstrained and constrained optimization problems involving SPBQ polynomials, demonstrate notable computational benefits compared to general SOS-based methods, and illustrate their use in convex polynomial regression and fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [322] [Existence of stable Lur'e systems for which the O'Shea-Zames-Falb stability test fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23599)
*Andrey Kharitenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了O'Shea-Zames-Falb稳定性检验对于具有斜率受限非线性的Lur'e系统的鲁棒稳定性并非必要，从而否定了J. Carrasco的一个猜想。通过构造一个全块乘子来证明一个特定的Lur'e互连系统的稳定性，而该系统不满足OZF乘子条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳J. Carrasco关于O'Shea, Zames和Falb稳定性检验是斜率受限非线性Lur'e系统鲁棒稳定性必要条件的猜想。

Method: 构造一个具体的Lur'e互连系统，该系统不满足OZF乘子条件，但通过构造一个全块乘子（full-block multiplier）来证明其稳定性。

Result: 成功构造了一个反例：该Lur'e系统虽能通过全块乘子方法证明稳定，但无法找到OZF乘子，表明OZF检验并非必要条件。

Conclusion: O'Shea-Zames-Falb稳定性检验对于斜率受限非线性Lur'e系统的鲁棒稳定性不是必要的，相关猜想被推翻。

Abstract: In this note we show that the well-known stability test proposed by O'Shea, Zames and Falb is not necessary for robust stability of Lur'e systems with slope-restricted nonlinearities and thereby disprove a conjecture by J. Carrasco. Specifically we construct a full-block multiplier which certifies stability of a constructed Lur'e interconnection that does not admit an OZF multiplier.

</details>


### [323] [Exact Worst-case Convergence Rates of Distributed Gradient Tracking Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23601)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过特征分解方法，精确推导了两种梯度跟踪分布式优化算法DIGing和AugDGM的最坏情况收敛速率公式，发现AugDGM的最优速率始终优于DIGing，并揭示了图连通性存在拐点σ=1/3：当连通性高于此拐点时，AugDGM在目标函数条件数足够差时可达到集中式梯度下降的最优速率；低于此拐点时则无法达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化算法的收敛性分析大多给出充分性条件，导致收敛速率估计偏保守，无法精确刻画算法性能。本文旨在提供DIGing和AugDGM两种典型梯度跟踪算法的精确最坏情况收敛速率。

Method: 采用特征分解将多输入多输出（MIMO）系统的稳定性分析简化为一系列参数变化的单输入单输出（SISO）系统，从而推导出精确的最坏情况收敛速率显式公式。

Result: 1) DIGing和AugDGM的平均状态动力学相同，但梯度跟踪子系统不同，后者完全决定算法收敛性；2) 相同假设下，DIGing的最优最坏情况收敛速率大于AugDGM；3) 图连通性存在拐点σ=1/3，连通性高于此拐点时AugDGM可在目标函数条件数足够差时达到集中式梯度下降最优速率，低于此拐点时则无法达到。

Conclusion: 本文提供了两种分布式梯度跟踪算法的精确最坏情况收敛速率，揭示了算法间的性能差异以及图连通性对收敛性能的根本性影响。

Abstract: Different from most existing literature in the analysis of distributed optimization algorithms that reports sufficient convergence conditions leading to a conservative convergence rate, this work provides the exact worst-case convergence rates for two typical gradient tracking algorithms, DIGing and AugDGM. By eigen-decomposition, we show that two algorithms share the same average-state dynamics, while they differ from each other in the gradient tracking subsystems, which entirely govern algorithm convergence. Exploiting the diagonal structure of this decomposition, we reduce the stability analysis of MIMO systems to that of a set of parameter-varying SISO systems, from which explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate can be derived. These formulas clearly show that the optimal worst-case convergence rate of DIGing is larger than that of AugDGM under the same assumptions on objective functions and communication networks. Furthermore, we find that there is an inflection point in the graph connectivity, which is $σ=1/3$. For graphs with connectivity better than this inflection point, the optimal convergence rate of centralized gradient descent can be achieved by AugDGM provided the condition number of objective functions is worse enough. On the other hand, for graphs with connectivity worse than this inflection point, the centralized optimal rate can never be achieved. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [324] [When Rates Are Geometric: Rate-Certificate Transfer for Contact Splittings in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23642)
*George A Kevrekidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出接触哈密顿系统作为连接连续时间ODE和离散优化算法的框架，通过接触分裂方法将连续时间收敛证书精确转移到离散算法，并用二次重球法、强凸目标等实例验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散优化算法通常通过连续时间极限ODE分析，但ODE的收敛证明不能自动保证离散算法的收敛性，需要一种严格的转移机制来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 引入接触哈密顿系统，利用其固有衰减恒等式$\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$构建连续时间证书；通过接触分裂（contact splitting）方法和后向误差分析，在满足三个独立可检验假设的条件下，将连续证书以$O(h^r)$精度转移到离散算法；设计了$H=K+V+D$（动能+势能+耗散项）的模块化设计模板。

Result: 二次重球法的投影耗散蛙跳谱与现有的共形辛优化理论一致；接触哈密顿增强能量给出了尖锐的目标-证书比较，验证了转移假设；对于含状态依赖阻尼的强凸目标，通过辅助阴影推论实现了Bregman型李雅普诺夫证书的转移；数值实验证实了共形因子跟踪阶数，并在病态基准和深度学习任务上表现良好。

Conclusion: 接触哈密顿系统为连续到离散的收敛证书转移提供了精确的理论框架，接触分裂方法能以可控精度继承连续衰减特性，且该框架具有通用性和模块化设计能力。

Abstract: Discrete optimization algorithms are often analyzed through continuous-time limiting ODEs, but a convergence certificate for the ODE is not automatically one for the discrete algorithm. We develop contact Hamiltonian systems as a setting where the transfer can be made precise. A contact Hamiltonian $H$ on $J^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ obeys the intrinsic decay identity $\dot H = -H\,\partial_s H$, so an augmented energy $\mathcal{E}$ built from $H$, together with the conformal rate $\partial_s H$, is a continuous-time rate certificate whenever $\mathcal{E}$ controls the objective gap. Our main theorem states, under three named and independently checkable hypotheses, that an order-$r$ contact splitting with step $h$ transfers this certificate over the finite horizon set by backward error analysis. The discrete decay envelope is governed by the modified conformal factor up to $O(h^r)$ perturbations plus a backward-error shadowing defect, and the mechanism is inherited exactly because the modified Hamiltonian is itself a contact Hamiltonian. Quadratic heavy ball is a fully solvable example: its projected dissipative-leapfrog spectrum agrees with established conformal-symplectic optimization theory, while the augmented contact Hamiltonian yields a sharp objective-to-certificate comparison that verifies the transfer hypotheses. For strongly convex objectives with state-dependent damping, an explicit Bregman-type Lyapunov certificate instead transfers by an auxiliary-shadowing corollary. The decomposition $H=K+V+D$ into kinetic, objective-encoding potential, and dissipation terms serves as a design template, with a catalogue of closed-form sub-flows including contact-specific damping families. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted conformal-factor tracking orders and show competitive performance on ill-conditioned benchmarks and deep-learning tasks.

</details>


### [325] [Projected-Gradient Analysis for Open-Domain Convex Optimization under Boundary Blow-Up:Application to Controllability Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23674)
*Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑凸函数在紧凸集上的优化问题，目标函数仅在开域上凸且满足边界爆破条件。提出了域感知的Armijo投影梯度法，建立了安全邻域分析，保证了目标函数求值、有限回溯、充分下降和步长下界，给出了次线性收敛和全序列收敛保证。在正曲率条件下实现了线性收敛。将该框架应用于可控性评分问题，分析了可行性和Gramian映射的单射性，并通过有向网络示例展示了候选排除对最优分配的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸优化方法通常要求目标函数在整个可行域上光滑且凸，但许多实际问题中目标函数仅在开域上定义且满足凸性，边界处可能出现爆破。需要建立一套适用于这种边界爆破情况的优化理论和方法。

Method: 提出域感知的Armijo投影梯度法，引入安全邻域分析确保目标函数求值始终在定义域内；利用边界爆破条件保证紧不变子水平集的存在性；通过正曲率分析得到强凸性界和线性收敛。

Result: 证明了算法具有有限回溯、充分下降和步长的迭代无关正下界；给出了次线性目标函数和驻点收敛保证；在正曲率条件下证明了唯一性和线性收敛；在可控性评分应用中精确刻画了可行性和Gramian映射的单射性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了边界爆破条件下的凸优化问题，为可控性评分等应用提供了理论基础，揭示了候选排除对可行性和最优分配的影响具有准则依赖性和时域依赖性。

Abstract: We study convex optimization over a compact convex set when the objective is smooth and convex only on an open domain. Under a boundary-blow-up condition, every feasible initialization yields a compact invariant sublevel set separated from the complement of the objective domain, and an optimal solution exists. For a domain-aware Armijo projected-gradient method, a safe-neighborhood analysis establishes well-defined objective evaluations, finite backtracking, sufficient decrease, and a run-specific but iteration-independent positive lower bound on the accepted step sizes. These properties yield explicit sublinear objective and stationarity guarantees, together with convergence of the full iterate sequence. Positive curvature restricted to feasible displacement directions further guarantees uniqueness and linear convergence. We apply the framework to controllability scoring with prescribed input directions and compact convex allocation constraints. Feasibility is characterized exactly by controllability of the input directions eligible for positive allocation, while restricted injectivity of the Gramian map guarantees uniqueness of the optimal allocation and provides explicit strong-convexity bounds. A directed-network example illustrates how candidate exclusion can preserve or destroy feasibility and alter the optimal allocation in a criterion- and horizon-dependent manner.

</details>


### [326] [LiFT-MPC: Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning of Cost Previews for MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23832)
*Xinyi Yi,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，通过语言循环反馈调优机制在线修正MPC中的时变目标预测，并给出闭环系统性能保证，在储能管理中验证了经济性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有时变目标的MPC中，预测信号（如能源价格）难以仅从历史轨迹准确预测，因为它们依赖于上下文事件，这限制了MPC的经济性能。

Method: 提出LiFT-MPC框架，将语言循环反馈调优（LiFT）校正方案集成到MPC循环中，通过控制性能损失函数在线更新预测机制，并建立闭环系统的性能保证。

Result: 在基于真实电价和新闻上下文的储能管理数值实验中，验证了所提方法能够提升经济性能。

Conclusion: LiFT-MPC通过语言反馈在线校正预测，有效提升了时变目标下MPC的经济效益，且具有理论性能保障。

Abstract: In model predictive control (MPC) with time-varying objectives, predicted signals need to be often incorporated in the cost function, such as prices in energy system operation. These are, however, often difficult to predict from the historical trajectory of these signals alone, as they may depend on other contextual events. We propose LiFT-MPC, an MPC framework that integrates a LiFT (Language-in-the-Loop Feedback Tuning) correction scheme to refine such predictions within the MPC loop. The prediction mechanism is updated online via a control-performance loss function, and we establish a performance guarantee for the resulting closed loop system. Numerical experiments using a realistic example of energy-storage management with real prices and news context to improve predictions, demonstrate an improved economic performance

</details>


### [327] [Closed-loop solvability of infinite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic problem for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24004)
*Kai Ding,Fan Wu,Jie Xiong,Xinyue Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类马尔可夫体制切换跳-扩散系统的无限时域随机线性二次最优控制问题，其中状态过程的跳跃由马尔可夫链的状态切换同步诱发。通过耦合代数Riccati方程的镇定解，给出了最优控制的反馈表示，并应用于终身财富跟踪问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对马尔可夫切换系统或跳-扩散系统的SLQ控制已有较多成果，但状态过程跳跃与马尔可夫链状态切换同步发生的场景尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，并为该类系统建立闭环可解性与最优控制反馈表示。

Method: 将马尔可夫链的状态转移同时诱导状态过程发生跳跃，建立不同于经典Poisson跳-扩散的模型框架；通过求解耦合代数Riccati方程组(CAREs)的镇定解，推导最优控制的反馈表示；讨论闭环系统的可解性条件。

Result: 成功给出了该类SLQ控制问题的闭环可解性条件，并获得了基于CARE镇定解的最优控制反馈表示。将该理论应用于终身财富跟踪问题，导出了相应的最优投资策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的马尔可夫链同步跳跃模型丰富了SLQ控制理论体系，所得到的闭环可解性条件与反馈控制具有明确的工程与金融应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates a class of stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problems over an infinite horizon for Markov regime-switching jump-diffusion systems. Unlike classical diffusion models modulated by a Markov chain, we assume that the state process undergoes abrupt jumps that are synchronous with the regime switches of the Markov chain. In contrast to conventional Poisson jump-diffusion models, the jumps in the state process are entirely induced by the state transitions of the Markov chain, which can be interpreted as losses or gains of state process incurred during regime changes. Under this formulation, we thoroughly discuss the closed-loop solvability of the SLQ control problem and provide a feedback representation of the optimal control via the stabilizing solution of a system of coupled algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs). Finally, we further apply our results to a lifetime wealth tracking problem and derive the corresponding optimal investment strategy.

</details>


### [328] [Smooth Learning with Hard Constraints via Legendre-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24007)
*Zikun Lin,Rui Chen,Yijie Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Legendre正则化策略，通过将决策参数化为原始可行域上正则化优化问题的解，构建了既满足可行性约束又保持可微性的策略类，并证明了其通用逼近性质，在情境优化任务中优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情境优化方法在策略类设计上难以同时满足三个需求：表达性（学习丰富的上下文-决策关系）、严格可行性（硬约束而非软惩罚项）、以及光滑性（支持基于梯度的端到端训练）。现有方法通常只强调其中部分需求。

Method: 提出Legendre正则化策略，将决策参数化为在原始可行域上求解正则化优化问题得到的最优解。通过引入Legendre型正则化项，确保优化器映射是单值、映射到可行集相对内部、具有显式雅可比、Lipschitz连续且可任意光滑。

Result: 在情境报童问题和资源分配问题上的实验表明，所提方法在决策性能上优于基准方法。

Conclusion: Legendre正则化策略统一了显式正则化优化器和基于隐式扰动的光滑优化器，为情境优化中的策略类设计提供了理论上完备且实践有效的框架。

Abstract: We revisit contextual optimization from the perspective of policy class design. A desirable policy class should be expressive enough to learn rich context-decision relationships, should enforce hard feasibility constraints rather than soft penalty terms, and should remain smooth enough for gradient-based training on downstream decision losses. Existing approaches usually emphasize only part of these requirements. We propose Legendre-regularized policies, which parameterize decisions as solutions of regularized optimization problems over the original feasible region. This construction yields policies that are feasible by construction and differentiable with respect to learned latent parameters. We prove that the associated optimizer map is single-valued, maps onto the relative interior of the feasible set, admits an explicit Jacobian, is Lipschitz continuous, and can be made arbitrarily smooth. We also establish a universal approximation result showing that the proposed class can approximate any continuous feasible policy on compact context sets. The framework unifies explicitly regularized optimizers and implicit perturbation-based smooth optimizers. Experiments on contextual newsvendor and resource allocation problems show that our approach improves prescriptive performance relative to the benchmark methods.

</details>


### [329] [A Homogeneous Tensor Framework for High-Order Trust-Region and Spherical Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24046)
*Wenqi Zhu,Haibin Chen,Guanglu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于齐次张量表示的半径控制边界方法，将高阶泰勒子问题转化为球面上的多项式优化问题，并针对三次情况（p=3）设计了近端交替最小化（PAM）算法。将该边界步机制嵌入自适应齐次张量方法（Ada-HTM）后，达到了ARp类评估复杂度O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})。数值实验表明PAM与二阶矩-SOS证书匹配，对低秩张量扩展性好，且Ada-HTM在病态和不良缩放问题上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶方法（p≥3）虽能改善最坏情况评估复杂度，但其泰勒子问题是非凸多项式优化问题，求解困难。现有方法缺乏对任意阶p的通用表示以及p=3时高效的全局求解策略。

Method: 1) 提出半径控制边界方法：通过常数坐标增广步长，将p阶泰勒多项式精确表示为p阶齐次张量形式，在预设半径上边界模型成为球面多项式优化问题。2) 针对三次非齐次球面模型，引入二次平移并证明在显式平移界下等价于三块多线性形式，设计近端交替最小化（PAM）算法（闭式块更新）。3) 将边界步机制嵌入自适应齐次张量方法（Ada-HTM）。

Result: Ada-HTM在显式光滑性、保护区下降、弱曲率非退化及局部精化条件下达到O(ε^{-(p+1)/p})评估复杂度。PAM数值上与阶-2矩-SOS证书匹配，低秩张量扩展性好，Ada-HTM在病态和不良缩放问题上优于信赖域和三次正则化方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法为高阶非凸优化提供了一种新的可行框架，在理论保证和实际性能间取得了良好平衡，特别适合处理病态问题。

Abstract: High-order methods can improve worst-case evaluation complexity, but for orders $p\geq3$ their Taylor subproblems are nonconvex polynomial optimization problems and are generally difficult to solve. We develop a radius-controlled boundary approach based on homogeneous tensor representations. By augmenting the step with a constant coordinate, any $p$th-order Taylor polynomial can be represented exactly as an order-$p$ homogeneous tensor form; at a prescribed radius, the boundary model is a spherical polynomial optimization problem. The representation applies to arbitrary $p$, while the algorithmic development focuses on the cubic case $p=3$. For an inhomogeneous cubic on the sphere, we introduce a quadratic shift and prove, under an explicit shift bound, equivalence with a three-block multilinear formulation at global optimality. This motivates a proximal alternating minimization (PAM) method with closed-form block updates; its objective values decrease and every accumulation point is stationary. We embed the boundary-step mechanism in an Adaptive Homogeneous Tensor Method (Ada--HTM). Under explicit smoothness, safeguarded-decrease, weak-curvature nondegeneracy, and local-refinement conditions, Ada--HTM attains the adaptive-regularization-type (AR$p$-type) evaluation complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-(p+1)/p})$ for first-order stationarity. Numerically, PAM matches order-$2$ moment--sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates on the structured cubic instances for which certification is tractable, scales particularly well for low-rank tensors, and makes Ada--HTM competitive with trust-region and cubic-regularization methods, with its largest gains on ill-conditioned and badly-scaled problems.

</details>


### [330] [Stochastic Control of Addiction with State-Dependent Jump Relapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24099)
*Dounia Aissi,Ioana Ciotir,Dan Goreac,Juan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个连续时间理性成瘾模型，其中成瘾资本遵循带有状态依赖跳的分段确定马尔可夫过程（捕捉复发与恢复），通过幂函数设定瞬时效用，推导了带非局部跳项的HJB方程，并在有界控制情形下证明了唯一有界粘性解及最优策略的bang-bang性质，在无界情形下得到了显式线性反馈控制与闭式价值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理性成瘾模型多采用连续扩散过程描述成瘾资本演化，难以刻画成瘾行为中常见的复发与恢复等离散跳跃特征。本文旨在通过引入分段确定马尔可夫过程（PDMP）与状态依赖跳，更真实地建模成瘾的动态过程。

Method: 采用连续时间理性成瘾模型框架，将成瘾资本建模为分段确定马尔可夫过程（PDMP），其跳跃强度依赖于当前状态以模拟复发与恢复。瞬时效用函数采用消费与成瘾资本的幂函数形式。通过动态规划原理推导出带非局部跳项的HJB方程。在有界控制（capped control）情形下，利用粘性解理论证明解的唯一性，并刻画最优控制策略的结构；在无界控制（uncapped case）情形下，通过解析求解得到显式线性反馈控制与闭式价值函数。

Result: 在有界控制情形下，获得了唯一有界粘性解，并证明了最优策略为bang-bang控制（即在最小与最大消费水平之间切换）。在无界控制情形下，推导出了若干参数区域内的显式线性反馈控制策略及闭式价值函数。

Conclusion: 本文通过PDMP框架成功将复发与恢复纳入理性成瘾模型，拓展了该领域的分析边界。不同控制约束下的解析与粘性解结果，为理解成瘾行为中的非线性动态与最优干预策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time rational addiction model where addiction capital follows a piecewise deterministic Markov process with state-dependent jumps capturing relapse and recovery. The instantaneous utility combines consumption and addiction capital via a power specification, leading to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations with nonlocal jump terms. In the capped, bounded-control case we obtain a unique bounded viscosity solution and prove that optimal policies are bang-bang, switching between minimal and maximal consumption, while in the uncapped case we derive explicit linear feedback controls and closed-form value functions in several parameter regimes.

</details>


### [331] [Optimal Control with Expectation Constraint in a Smooth Boundary Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24114)
*Bruno Bouchard,Lucas Gnecco Heredia,Ludovic Moreau,Kim-Anh Pham*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带期望约束的效用最大化问题。首先在一致椭圆情形下证明了内源状态边界的光滑性并导出合适的Dirichlet边界条件；然后提出了一种新的截断论证用于期望约束的鞅表示，构建了可比较的PDE辅助系统并证明了收敛性。在退化情形下，通过添加小噪声恢复一致椭圆性并证明收敛。最后在玩具示例中利用神经网络进行数值求解并估计数值误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带期望约束的随机控制问题中缺乏完整理论分析和数值方法的问题，为数值方案的应用打开大门。

Method: 1) 一致椭圆情形：证明内源状态边界光滑性并导出Dirichlet边界条件；2) 提出截断论证用于期望约束的鞅表示，构建可比较的PDE辅助系统；3) 退化情形：添加小噪声项恢复一致椭圆性；4) 神经网络数值求解与误差估计。

Result: 证明了两种情形下近似序列到原始最优控制问题的收敛性；在玩具示例上成功进行了数值求解并完成了数值误差估计。

Conclusion: 首次对此类控制问题进行了完整分析，为数值方案的使用打开了大门。

Abstract: As in Bouchard et al. (2010) and Bouchard and Nutz (2014), we study a utility maximization problem with expectation constraint. We first consider a uniformly elliptic case in which the endogenous state boundary associated with the constraint in expectation is proved to be smooth. This allows one to derive a proper Dirichlet condition for the value function of the optimal control problem on this boundary. We then propose a new truncation argument in the martingale representation of the expectation constraint. This leads to an approximating sequence of auxiliary systems of PDEs for which comparison holds. Convergence to the initial optimal control problem is proved. In the degenerate case, we propose another approximation which consists in adding a small noise term to recover uniformly ellipticity. Convergence is also proved. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first time that a full analysis is performed for such control problems, so as to open the doors to the use of numerical schemes. Numerical resolution in a toy example is performed using neural networks. It is complemented by an estimation of the numerical error, also performed by using a neural network approach.

</details>


### [332] [On the hardness of deterministic second-order optimization of functions with Lipschitz gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24120)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Lipschitz连续梯度条件下，确定性零尊重算法无法在有限次二阶预言机调用内计算Goldstein近似二阶稳定点，而一般确定性算法在维度n下至少需要n-3次调用，表明确定性二阶弱凸优化是难解的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究确定性算法在计算二阶稳定点时的计算复杂度下界，特别是针对弱凸优化问题。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别证明了零尊重算法和一般确定性算法在二阶预言机模型下的下界：零尊重算法无法在有限步内收敛，一般确定性算法需要至少n-3次调用。

Result: 确定性零尊重算法无法在有限次二阶预言机调用内计算Goldstein近似二阶稳定点；一般确定性算法在n维输入下至少需要n-3次二阶预言机调用。

Conclusion: 确定性二阶弱凸优化是计算上难解的（intractable），这为理解该问题的算法复杂度提供了重要下界结果。

Abstract: We show that no deterministic zero-respecting algorithm (resp., (general) deterministic algorithm) can compute Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of functions with Lipschitz continuous gradients within a finite number of (resp., no more than $n-3$ with $n$ being the input dimension) second-order oracle calls. This, among other consequences, shows that deterministic second-order weakly convex optimization is intractable.

</details>


### [333] [On computing Goldstein approximate second-order stationary points of structured nonsmooth nonconvex programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24122)
*Jiewen Guan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机一阶算法，利用随机平滑技术计算L-光滑函数的Goldstein近似二阶驻点，具有$\widetilde{O}({ n^2}/{\varepsilon^9}+{ n^3}/{\varepsilon^7})$的预言复杂度，并扩展到了弱凸函数和双层优化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
